### [1] [StreamRig: Exploiting Intra-Rig Geometry for Streaming Multi-Camera Odometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40244)
*Yufei Wei,Shuhao Ye,Qi Wang,Xin Zheng,Qing Huang,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: StreamRig：在冻结的多视角3D基础模型上，面向标定多相机平台构建因果流式里程计，仅训练少量模块，实现跨数据集低漂移与低推理成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人和车辆常配备同步多相机系统，但许多流式3D基础模型仅支持单目输入，如何高效利用相机标定与刚体几何仍是挑战。

Method: 提出StreamRig冻结-流式框架：冻结的多视角3D基础模型前端结合标定联合感知同步视图；Rig-Resampler压缩特征，CausalBridge使用带KV缓存的因果注意力，轻量头回归平台位姿；周期性重锚定协议支持长序列稳定估计。仅训练这些模块，共74.6M参数，以相对位姿为唯一监督。两阶段训练：组重定位预训练与因果平台训练。

Result: 在NCLT、TartanGround、KITTI-360及自采人形机器人数据集ZJH上评估；仅用仿真训练，真实世界零样本测试。StreamRig在四个数据集上相较非oracle单目流式模型和平台感知离线模型取得更低平移与旋转漂移，并保持低推理成本。消融和受控相机数量实验识别增益来源，并考察更长训练窗口对长时域推理的影响。

Conclusion: StreamRig有效利用冻结多视角3D基础模型与标定几何，实现高效、稳定的多相机因果流式里程计，代码已开源。

Abstract: Mobile robots and vehicles carry synchronized multi-camera rigs, yet many streaming 3D foundation models are designed for monocular input, leaving efficient use of rig geometry a challenge. We present StreamRig, a freeze-and-stream framework that builds causal streaming odometry for calibrated rigs on a frozen multi-view 3D foundation model. The frozen front-end jointly perceives the synchronized views using rig calibration. A Rig-Resampler compresses their features, a CausalBridge applies causal attention with a key-value cache, and a lightweight head regresses rig poses. A periodic re-anchoring protocol supports stable pose estimation over long sequences. Only these modules are trained, 74.6M parameters in total, with relative poses as the sole supervision. Our two-stage training strategy combines group relocalization pretraining with causal rig training to transfer the geometric priors of the frozen front-end and the alignment ability of the pretrained modules to streaming odometry. We evaluate on NCLT, TartanGround, KITTI-360, and our self-collected humanoid-robot dataset ZJH, where training uses only simulation and real-world evaluation is zero-shot. Across all four datasets, StreamRig achieves lower translation and rotation drift than the evaluated non-oracle monocular streaming and rig-aware offline models, while maintaining low inference cost. Ablations and controlled camera-count experiments identify the sources of these gains. We further examine how longer training windows affect inference over longer horizons. Code has been released at this https URL .

</details>


### [2] [NavHarness: Adaptive Goals for Agentic Vision-Language Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39915)
*Haoxiang Shi,Zaijing Li,Muhe Ding,Xiang Deng,Yaowei Wang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种Agentic VLN框架，包含Goal、Verify、Memory和Visuomotor四个智能体，通过自适应目标设定、动态验证与多模态历史压缩，提升长时程导航一致性并降低推理开销；在R2R-CE、RxR-CE及真实环境中验证，真实世界八条路线达83.3%成功率和1.51m导航误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉语言导航要求具身智能体根据指令和观测生成动作。通用多模态智能体虽有潜力，但仅选择局部合理动作并不能保证长时程执行与预期路线一致；同时，累积的交互历史会增大后续决策输入，带来显著推理开销。

Method: 所提方法为Agentic VLN框架：Goal Agent根据指令、当前观测和执行历史设定自适应局部目标；Visuomotor Agent执行导航动作以完成目标；Verify Agent使用目标特定验证问题动态判断观测结果是否满足完成条件；验证完成后形成边界，由Memory Agent压缩对应多模态交互历史，同时保留后续导航所需信息。

Result: 在R2R-CE和RxR-CE上评估导航，在三个模型骨干上检验框架变体，并研究执行过程中的上下文演化。真实世界评估中，在八条挑战性路线上各评估三次，所提方法达到83.3%成功率和1.51m导航误差。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式目标设定、动态验证和记忆压缩能有效协调局部动作与长时程路线意图，减少推理开销，并在仿真与真实环境中取得强导航性能，为通用多模态智能体用于VLN提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) requires embodied agents to generate actions based on instructions and observations. General-purpose multimodal agents offer a promising basis for this task, but selecting plausible local actions does not ensure that execution remains consistent with the intended route, particularly in long-horizon tasks. Moreover, the accumulated interaction history increases the input required for subsequent decisions, resulting in a significant inference overhead. To this end, we introduce \method, an Agentic VLN framework that includes a Goal Agent that sets adaptive goals for local actions, a Verify Agent that dynamically verifies whether a goal has been completed, a Memory Agent for multimodal context compression, and a Visuomotor Agent to execute adaptive goals. Specifically, the Goal Agent formulates adaptive goals based on the instruction, current observation, and execution history. Then the Visuomotor Agent executes navigation actions to achieve each goal, while the Verify Agent uses a goal-specific verification question to dynamically assess whether the observed outcomes satisfy the intended completion condition. Verified goal completion then marks a boundary for the Memory Agent to compress the corresponding multimodal interaction history while preserving information needed for subsequent navigation. We evaluate navigation on R2R-CE and RxR-CE, examine framework variants across three model backbones, and study context evolution during execution. For Real-World evaluation, \method achieves 83.3\% success and 1.51\,m navigation error across eight challenging routes evaluated three times each.

</details>


### [3] [Inline Memory Meets Reusable Skills: Memory-centric Framework for Vision-Language-Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39794)
*Zaijing Li,Rui Shao,Bing Hu,Haoyu Zhang,Dongmei Jiang,Liqiang Nie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Optimus-R，一种以记忆为中心的VLA框架，将机器人适应建模为显式的查询-技能记忆调优，通过内联记忆接口、查询-技能记忆库与Bridge-and-Adapt机制实现高效技能学习并缓解灾难性遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但适应新任务和新域仍效率低下：现有方法多依赖参数微调，成本高且易导致对已学任务的灾难性遗忘。

Method: Optimus-R将机器人适应形式化为显式的查询-技能记忆调优，包含三部分：(i) 用于技能提取的内联记忆接口，将可学习记忆令牌插入VLA前缀流，使主干在原生动作条件化路径中导出控制感知的查询与技能表示；(ii) 用于技能学习的查询-技能记忆库，将技能外化为决定“检索什么”的查询原型和指定“如何行动”的技能值，支持有限参数更新下的技能复用与扩展；(iii) 用于技能更新的轻量Bridge-and-Adapt机制，通过轻量适配器和残差记忆更新，将目标域查询与技能对齐到现有记忆空间。

Result: 在域内适应、跨域适应和终身学习实验中，Optimus-R实现了数据高效的技能学习，同时缓解了灾难性遗忘。

Conclusion: Optimus-R为VLA模型适应提供了一种参数高效、可复用且可扩展的记忆调优路径，在新任务/新域适应与旧任务保持之间取得较好平衡。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown strong promise for general-purpose robotic manipulation, yet adapting them to new tasks and domains remains inefficient: existing methods often rely on parameter tuning, incurring substantial costs and risking catastrophic forgetting of previously learned tasks. To address this, we propose \textbf{Optimus-R}, a memory-centric VLA framework that formulates robotic adaptation as explicit query-skill memory tuning. Optimus-R introduces: (i) An \textbf{Inline Memory Interface for skill extraction}. It inserts learnable memory tokens into the VLA prefix stream, allowing the backbone to derive control-aware query and skill representations within the native action-conditioning pathway. (ii) A \textbf{Query-Skill Memory Bank for skill learning}. It externalizes skills into query prototypes for deciding \emph{what} to retrieve and skill values for specifying \emph{how} to act, supporting skill reuse and expansion with limited parameter updates. (iii) A lightweight \textbf{Bridge-and-Adapt mechanism for skill updating}. It aligns target-domain queries and skills with the existing memory space through a lightweight adapter and residual memory updates. Experiments on in-domain adaptation, cross-domain adaptation, and lifelong learning show that Optimus-R enables data-efficient skill learning while mitigating catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [4] [GroundingPI: A Grounding Foundation Model towards Physical Intelligence with Visual Primitives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39601)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Boyu Chen,Jiaqi Liang,Xini Ding,Yue Chen,Zetian Song,Yuran Wang,Yi Zou,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Wenxuan Song,Bohan Zhou,Mingleyang Li,Siqiao Huang,Yuqi Ye,Caigao Jiang,Wei Wei,Ruihai Wu,Hang Zhang,Yixiao Ge,Shuchang Zhou,Shilong Liu,Xianming Liu,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出4B规模的grounding基础模型GroundingPI，将点与框统一为共享词表中的量化坐标；在34个grounding基准、44个基线上取得SOTA（平均73.68%），并作为视觉骨干显著提升机器人操作与自动驾驶性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确grounding需指明目标对象及其位置，且在杂乱场景与微小目标下仍要足够快以支持闭环控制；但现有VLA与世界动作模型沿用通用视觉语言与视频生成骨干，在这些场景中表现不佳。

Method: GroundingPI用共享词表中的量化坐标统一生成点和框；训练结合多模态与空间预训练、监督微调以及GRPO强化学习，监督信号来自公开数据集与专用数据引擎。

Result: 在覆盖11类感知能力的34个grounding基准上，对44个基线取得SOTA，平均73.68%，高于更大的GPT-6 Astra（71.54%）。作为下游视觉骨干：RoboTwin 2.0四个OOD设置均优于所有评估的主流骨干，最高相对提升24.8%；RoboCasa-GR1仅用50%演示即超过基线用75%演示；nuScenes开环平均L2误差0.296 m。预训练规模扩大可提升下游操作与驾驶性能；数据配方分析显示稠密grounding对二者均有显著收益，OCR可能成为感知学习的催化剂。

Conclusion: 结果支持将grounding作为感知基础，并表明面向感知的专用预训练是通向物理智能基础模型的有前景方向。

Abstract: Precise grounding matters. It specifies which object is the target and where that object is, even in clutter and for tiny objects, and it has to be fast enough for closed-loop control. Yet vision-language-action (VLA) and world-action models (WAMs) take perception from general-purpose vision-language and video-generation backbones, which still fail in these settings. We introduce GroundingPI, a 4B grounding foundation model that generates points and boxes as quantized coordinates in a shared vocabulary. Training combines multimodal and spatial pretraining, supervised fine-tuning, and reinforcement learning with GRPO, using supervision from public datasets and dedicated data engines. Against 44 baselines across 34 grounding benchmarks spanning 11 perceptual capabilities, GroundingPI establishes a new state of the art, averaging 73.68%, above the larger GPT-6 Astra (71.54%). As a downstream visual backbone, GroundingPI improves performance on robotic manipulation and autonomous driving. On RoboTwin 2.0, it outperforms every mainstream backbone we evaluate in all four out-of-distribution settings, by up to 24.8% relative to the strongest backbone. On RoboCasa-GR1, GroundingPI trained with 50% of the demonstrations outperforms those baselines trained with 75%. On nuScenes, used as the visual backbone, GroundingPI attains an average open-loop L2 error of 0.296 m. We systematically analyze GroundingPI's pretraining in scale and data composition. Downstream autonomous driving and robotic manipulation improve as the pretraining is scaled. Analyzing the data recipe across these 11 perceptual capabilities shows dense grounding's substantial benefits for both, and OCR's potential as a catalyst for perceptual learning. These results support grounding as a perceptual foundation, and dedicated perceptual pretraining as a promising direction for foundation models of physical intelligence.

</details>


### [5] [GroundAnything: Reconciling Parallel Decoding with Precise Visual Grounding at Flash Speed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39600)
*Qize Yu,Lianrui Fan,Bowen Ping,Xini Ding,Zetian Song,Junbo Niu,Kaixuan Wang,Tianxing Chen,Yue Chen,Minghua He,Yuran Wang,Jie Huang,Haojun Zhang,Min Chen,Hao Li,Wenxuan Song,Ruihai Wu,Xianming Liu,Shilong Liu,Shuchang Zhou,Ping Luo,Shiyu Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 4B 参数视觉定位基础模型 GroundAnything，将 grounding 视为视觉证据提取，利用块级扩散并行去噪替代自回归序列生成，实现快速并行解码与精确定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 grounding 模型将空间预测序列化，引入顺序延迟并强加从左到右的因果生成顺序；但对象、位置和空间关系由图像与查询联合约束，其依赖关系并不代表存在内在的从左到右生成顺序，因此双向扩散更适合并行生成并联合细化空间假设。

Method: GroundAnything 通过 blockwise denoising 协调并行解码与精确定位；训练包含基于公开数据集和数据引擎的 grounding 预训练、AR 到扩散的直接转换与 AR/扩散联合目标、监督微调，以及基于 GRPO 的强化后训练；同时探索熵引导解码和可选自推测解码等策略。

Result: 在 30 个 grounding 基准上，自回归变体 GroundAnything-VLM 在同类规模模型中取得 72.42% 的总体 SOTA，并接近 GPT-6 Astra 的 71.35%；熵引导解码使 GroundAnything 平均达到 61.75%，超过快速 MTP-based LocateAnything 的 53.32%；自推测模式相对 AR 对应模型实现 4.51 倍加速，COCO F1mIoU 仅下降 0.74 个百分点；基础设施实验表明渐进推理优化可将并行解码转化为实际加速。

Conclusion: 该方法支持延迟敏感真实系统中的高效视觉定位，表明扩散式并行解码在 grounding 任务中兼具性能与效率潜力。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) grounding models serialize spatial predictions, introducing sequential latency and imposing a causal order on output tokens. We view grounding as visual evidence extraction: objects, locations, and spatial relations are jointly constrained by the image and query, yet their dependencies do not imply an intrinsic left-to-right generation order. This distinction makes bidirectional diffusion a natural fit, allowing spatial hypotheses to emerge in parallel and be jointly refined through iterative denoising. We introduce GroundAnything, a 4B-parameter grounding foundation model that reconciles fast parallel decoding with precise localization through blockwise denoising. Training combines grounding pretraining from public datasets and dedicated data engines, direct AR-to-diffusion conversion with joint AR and diffusion objectives, supervised fine-tuning, and GRPO-based reinforcement post-training. Across 30 grounding benchmarks, our autoregressive variant, GroundAnything-VLM, establishes a new overall state of the art among similarly sized models at 72.42%, remaining competitive with GPT-6 Astra (71.35%). With entropy-guided decoding, GroundAnything also surpasses the prior state of the art at this scale, averaging 61.75% versus 53.32% for the fast MTP-based LocateAnything model. We further explore decoding strategies, showing that an optional self-speculative mode achieves a $4.51\times$ speedup over the AR counterpart with a 0.74 percentage-point drop in COCO F1mIoU. Infrastructure experiments show that progressive inference optimizations translate parallel decoding into practical speedups. These support efficient visual grounding in latency-sensitive real-world systems.

</details>


### [6] [Matisse: Evidence-Space Reasoning for Active 3D Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38746)
*Xihang Yu,Kaichen Zhou,Lorenzo Shaikewitz,Clément Jambon,Xiao Zhan,Rajat Talak,Luca Carlone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Matisse 是一种免训练框架，利用预训练生成式3D模型中的证据，统一主动重建与关键帧选择，通过证据不确定性和信息增益指导视角获取与关键帧筛选，在多个数据集上降低Chamfer距离、加速并大幅减少长时程重建所需视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动视角获取方法通常仅对已观测或已实例化几何估计不确定性，难以推理未观测结构；长时程重建方法又常保留冗余观测，因此在有限计算预算下难以高效获取并保留有用几何信息。

Method: Matisse 从与3D潜在token相关的交叉注意力证据中估计证据不确定性，并推导证据信息增益，以期望后验熵减少量指导视角获取和关键帧选择。它通过遮挡感知、物体平衡的聚合支持多物体场景，并在规划时通过中间潜在变量传播不确定性，避免完整重建。

Result: 在 GSO30、YCB-V 和 Replica 上，Matisse 相比各自最佳基线将 Chamfer 距离分别降低 12.7%、3.8% 和 9.2%；在 GSO30 上使用相同重建后端时，端到端速度达到最佳主动重建基线的1.50倍。在GSO30长时程重建关键帧选择实验中，Matisse仅用14%输入视角即达到与Stream3D相当的Chamfer距离。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于预训练生成式3D模型证据的不确定性/信息增益可有效统一主动视角获取与关键帧选择，在有限计算预算下提升重建精度、效率及长时程重建的视角利用效率。

Abstract: How can a 3D reconstruction system acquire and retain useful information to understand the geometry of a scene from partial views under a limited computation budget? Existing active view acquisition methods typically estimate uncertainty over observed or instantiated geometry, limiting their ability to reason about unseen structure, while long-horizon reconstruction methods often retain redundant observations. We introduce Matisse, a training-free framework that unifies active reconstruction and keyframe selection by leveraging evidence provided by a pretrained generative 3D model. Matisse estimates Evidential Uncertainty from cross-attention evidence associated with 3D latent tokens and derives an Evidential Information Gain to guide both view acquisition and keyframe selection based on the expected reduction in posterior entropy. Matisse supports multi-object scenes through occlusion-aware, object-balanced aggregation and propagates uncertainty through intermediate latents to avoid full reconstruction during planning. Matisse reduces Chamfer distance by 12.7%, 3.8%, and 9.2% on GSO30, YCB-V, and Replica, respectively, relative to the best baseline on each dataset, and achieves a $1.50\times$ end-to-end speedup over the best active reconstruction baseline on GSO30 with the same reconstruction backend. In the GSO30 keyframe selection experiment for long-horizon reconstruction, Matisse achieves comparable Chamfer distance using 14% of the input views compared with Stream3D.

</details>


### [7] [Vision-Language-Action Autonomous Driving Agent with Language-based Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38641)
*Kai Yan,Xiangyu Chen,Yulong Cao,Alex Naumann,Peter Karkus,Yan Wang,Jef Packer,Alex Schwing,Yuxiong Wang,Boris Ivanovic,Wenjie Luo,Marco Pavone*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AD-Memo，一种基于语言记忆的通用 VLA 驾驶智能体，通过将关键物体记录为思维链扩展并采用 SFT+Da Capo 半闭环 RL 训练，解决 VLA 因图像 token 成本高导致的记忆受限问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 在自动驾驶中具前景，但受限于可输入帧数，难以处理 all-way stop、长时程场景理解等依赖记忆的任务；已有潜在向量记忆通过交叉注意力访问，不可解释且不便迁移。

Method: 将记忆作为 CoT 扩展输出，记录驾驶关键周围物体，并将其作为后续输入；构建基于记忆的数据集；两阶段训练：SFT 与 Da Capo 半闭环 RL，记忆用轨迹级优势、驾驶用步级优势，以改善信用分配。

Result: 在 all-way stop 和一般驾驶等场景中，AD-Memo 提升驾驶质量，改善驾驶场景问答，并产生可即插即用于其他模型的记忆。

Conclusion: AD-Memo 以可解释、可迁移的语言记忆增强 VLA 驾驶智能体的长时程与记忆依赖能力，验证了语言记忆与半闭环 RL 训练的有效性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) foundation models have recently emerged as one of the prevailing solutions for autonomous driving, as they can utilize knowledge acquired during vision-language pretraining for accurate and interpretable driving. However, VLAs can take only a limited number of frames as visual input due to the high token cost of an image, which is problematic for memory-dependent tasks such as determining the arrival order at all-way stops and long-horizon driving scene understanding. Existing solutions use latent vector memories accessed through cross-attention, which are neither interpretable nor portable. In this paper, we propose AD-Memo, a general-purpose VLA driving agent with language-based memory. The agent outputs memory as an extension of its Chain-of-Thought (CoT) to record surrounding objects critical to driving; this memory becomes part of the agent's future input. We curate memory-based datasets and train VLAs with a two-stage recipe: Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) and \textit{Da Capo}, a novel semi-closed-loop Reinforcement Learning (RL) algorithm which uses trajectory-level advantage for memory and step-level advantage for driving, leading to better credit assignment. Across scenarios such as all-way stops and general driving, AD-Memo improves driving quality, enables better question answering on driving scenes, and produces plug-and-play memory for other models.

</details>


### [8] [HIGS: Hierarchical Implicit Grids for Joint Geometric and Semantic Scene Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38620)
*Hanwen Cao,Wenqiang Wu,Kuang-Ting Tu,Mathias Otnes,Jeffrey Delmerico,Rui Wang,Yulun Tian,Nikolay Atanasov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种分层神经场，利用多分辨率子图实现高效可扩展的隐式表示，并统一支持几何与语义特征，用于大规模场景重建与开放词汇物体定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经隐式表示多聚焦几何重建，缺乏语义信息，难以支持高层空间理解与任务规划；同时环境规模与复杂度增大时，后端优化面临计算效率挑战。

Method: 构建分层神经场：将场景分解为重叠子图并在局部子图内分层优化；设计统一查询与解码机制，将可学习地图特征转为几何和语义输出；引入特征编码器预测初始分层网格特征，减少从零优化时间；在隐式特征空间中对齐融合子图以校正漂移，避免解码最终输出；嵌入几何特征与视觉-语言潜在特征。

Result: 在SDF构建和开放词汇物体定位任务上验证；显著提升计算与内存效率，保持高估计精度，并在大规模真实世界基准上赋予机器人空间感知能力。

Conclusion: 该方法通过分层子图、统一查询解码和隐式空间融合，同时解决语义缺失与大规模优化效率问题，实现高效、可扩展且兼具几何与语义的神经隐式建图。

Abstract: Neural implicit representations have had a significant impact on scene reconstruction by enabling robots to build continuous, differentiable, and high-fidelity 3D maps. Most existing works focus on geometric reconstruction and lack semantic information for high-level spatial understanding and task planning. Also, as the scale and complexity of the environment increase, neural representations face the challenge of maintaining computational efficiency in back-end optimization. To resolve these two challenges, we introduce a hierarchical neural field that leverages multiresolution submaps to achieve an efficient and scalable implicit representation, and a unified query and decoding mechanism to support both geometric and semantic features. More specifically, the learnable map features can be converted to the output with the query and decoding process for both training and inference. For large-scale representation, we decompose a scene into overlapping submaps and do hierarchical optimization within each local submap, thus enabling scalable computation. To further improve efficiency, we design feature encoders that predict initial hierarchical grid features to substantially reduce the time needed to optimize the submap features from scratch. To correct estimation drift among submaps, we align and fuse them entirely within the implicit feature space, leading to substantial acceleration by avoiding the need to decode the final output. Building upon this efficient hierarchical representation, we embed both geometric features and vision-language latent features into the map, and demonstrate it on both Signed Distance Field (SDF) construction and open-vocabulary object grounding. Our approach significantly improves computation and memory efficiency, maintains high estimation accuracy, and endows the robot with spatial awareness on large-scale real-world benchmarks.

</details>


### [9] [AssemblyWorld: Rethinking 3D Assembly with General-Purpose Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40353)
*Jiahao Zhang,Yeying Fan,Moitreya Chatterjee,Suhas Lohit,Bernhard Egger,Tim K. Marks,Anoop Cherian,Stephen Gould*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出 AssemblyWorld 交互式 3D 装配环境与 AssemblyWorldBench 基准，评估通用预训练智能体能否仅靠视觉交互完成 3D 装配；最强系统零件精度 80.9%，完整装配成功率 59.4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究预训练通用智能体是否无需装配专门微调，即可通过视觉交互理解零件及其关系并完成精确 3D 空间装配；同时为该问题提供统一评测环境。

Method: 构建 AssemblyWorld：智能体查看渲染视图、操作给定刚性零件，可参考图像或装配手册；仅通过 2D 视图感知几何，不直接访问 mesh 顶点/面；装配结果按几何评估。基于此构建 AssemblyWorldBench：100 个任务、80 个物体，涵盖家具、工业装配与断裂重组。

Result: 评估 8 个智能体系统，能力差异显著。最强系统零件精度 80.9%，完整装配成功率 59.4%。开源系统在执行可靠性和装配精度上明显落后于更强的闭源系统。对视觉参考、交互轨迹和失败的分析表明，智能体能修正装配但仍残留定位误差。

Conclusion: AssemblyWorld 为评估交互式装配智能体提供统一环境，并刻画从近似结构恢复到精确重建之间的差距。

Abstract: The task of 3D assembly requires translating an understanding of parts and their relationships into precise spatial arrangements. Can pretrained general-purpose agents assemble objects through visual interaction without additional assembly-specific fine-tuning? To investigate this question, we introduce AssemblyWorld, an interactive 3D environment in which agents inspect rendered views and manipulate supplied rigid parts, guided by images or assembly manuals when available. Agents perceive part geometry through 2D views rather than direct access to mesh vertices or faces, while their resulting assemblies are evaluated geometrically. Building on this environment, we construct AssemblyWorldBench, comprising 100 assembly tasks across 80 objects spanning furniture, industrial assembly, and fracture reassembly. Evaluating eight agent systems reveals substantial differences in their capabilities. The strongest system achieves 80.9% part accuracy but 59.4% complete-assembly success. The evaluated open-source systems lag substantially behind their stronger closed-source peers in both execution reliability and assembly accuracy. Analyses of visual references, interaction trajectories, and failures show how agents revise assemblies while leaving residual positioning errors. AssemblyWorld provides a common setting for both assessing the capabilities of interactive assembly agents and characterizing the gap between approximate structure recovery and precise reconstruction.

</details>

### [10] [Unified Optimality Conditions for Stochastic Optimal Control in the Rough Path and Itô Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38395)
*Thomas Lew*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 该文证明随机最优控制中伊藤PMP与粗糙PMP的伴随方程通过条件期望相连，并建立统一PMP及若干应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伊藤微积分与粗糙路径理论为随机微分方程最优控制提供了不同的PMP框架：前者基于FBSDE，后者基于粗糙微分方程；二者关系尚不清晰，且粗糙PMP多针对确定性控制。需要统一理解这些框架并将其扩展到适应控制情形。

Method: 首先针对适应控制推导不使用FBSDE的粗糙随机PMP，证明通过引入随机针状变分，将确定性控制下的粗糙随机PMP推广而来。其次，利用Itô-Stratonovich转换公式与正向切方程、后向伴随方程之间的对偶恒等式，建立连接伊藤PMP和粗糙PMP的统一PMP。应用方面，重新推导生成模型微调的伴随匹配方法，并提出用于反馈问题的间接打靶法。

Result: 得到伊藤与粗糙PMP伴随变量的条件期望关系：p_t^Itô = E[p_t^rough | F_t]；给出适应控制的粗糙随机PMP以及连接两种框架的统一PMP；并在生成模型微调和反馈控制中展示具体应用。

Conclusion: 这些结果在伊藤与粗糙路径两种主流随机最优控制框架之间建立了基于条件期望的桥梁，统一了PMP视角，并为相关算法提供了新的理论联系与应用途径。

Abstract: Stochastic differential equations (SDEs) can be studied via Itô calculus and rough path theory. For stochastic optimal control, these two frameworks give distinct Pontryagin Maximum Principle (PMP) optimality conditions with forward-backward SDEs (FBSDEs) or rough differential equations. We show that the adjoint equations of the Itô and rough PMPs are connected via the conditional expectation $p_t^{\text{Itô}}=\mathbb{E}[p_t^{\text{rough}} \mid \mathcal{F}_t]$, where $\mathcal{F}_t$ represents information available at time $t$. First, we derive a rough stochastic PMP for problems with adapted controls that does not use FBSDEs. Its proof extends the rough stochastic PMP over deterministic controls by considering stochastic needle variations. Second, we derive a unified PMP connecting the Itô and rough PMPs, using Itô-Stratonovich conversion formulas and duality identities between the forward tangent and backward adjoint SDEs. As a first application, we rederive the adjoint matching method for fine-tuning generative models. As a second application, we propose an indirect shooting method for a class of feedback problems. Overall, these results give a new conditional bridge connecting two popular frameworks for stochastic optimal control.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [11] [Belief-Aware Multi-Agent Path Finding under Map Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40269)
*Viraj Parimi,Shao-Hung Chan,Han Zhang,Jingkai Chen,Brian Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MAGIC框架，面向Belief-Aware MAPF，利用高斯马尔可夫随机场与高斯置信传播在线维护共享可通行性信念，并将绕行感知代价融入标准MAPF规划器；在96.3%的基准实例上降低执行总代价，可扩展至800个智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典MAPF假设静态障碍完全已知，但真实环境中坠落物、泼洒等局部扰动会导致地图变化且初始未知。若这些变化具有空间相关性，一次观测可推断邻近未观测位置的可通行性。现有基于临时计划或直接观测重规划的方法未利用这种空间依赖，无法用单次观测预判附近未观测障碍，从而导致后续高代价绕行。

Method: 聚焦Belief-Aware MAPF：执行期地图差异固定但初始未知，且观测信息可超出观测位置。提出MAGIC框架，基于多智能体观测在线更新共享可通行性信念；采用高斯马尔可夫随机场建模空间相关性，并用高斯置信传播近似推断可通行性，进而为经典MAPF规划器构造绕行感知代价。

Result: 在MAPF基准测试中，MAGIC在96.3%的实例上相比现有方法降低执行总代价；实验覆盖多种规划器家族及最多800个智能体的团队，表明其适用于大规模MAPF问题。

Conclusion: MAGIC通过在线共享信念推断与空间相关可通行性建模，使标准MAPF规划器能够主动预判未观测障碍并减少执行代价，在多种规划器和大规模团队中均表现有效。

Abstract: Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) aims to find collision-free paths for multiple agents in a shared environment. Classical MAPF assumes that all static obstacles are known in advance, but real-world environments can change unexpectedly due to fallen objects, spills, or other local disturbances. When such changes are spatially correlated, an observation can inform traversability estimates beyond the observed location. Prior approaches address uncertainty in traversability through contingent plans or replanning based on direct observations, but do not leverage this spatial dependence to infer the traversability of nearby unobserved locations. As a result, they cannot use one observation to anticipate nearby unobserved obstacles that may cause costly rerouting later. We focus on Belief-Aware MAPF, where map discrepancies are fixed during execution but initially unknown, and observations can be informative beyond the observed location. We propose Multi-Agent Gaussian belief Inference for Coordination (MAGIC), a framework that updates a shared belief about traversability online based on agents' observations. MAGIC uses a Gaussian Markov Random Field and Gaussian Belief Propagation to approximately infer traversability and construct detour-aware costs for standard MAPF planners. Our experiments on MAPF benchmarks show that MAGIC reduces the executed sum of costs compared to existing approaches on 96.3% of instances, across several planner families and teams of up to 800 agents, demonstrating its applicability to large-scale MAPF problems.

</details>


### [12] [Divide and Collapse: MAPF-Collapse via Exact Decomposition into Independent Sub-Instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39559)
*Oren Salzman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究多智能体路径规划（MAPF）计划的后优化问题MAPFC：在保持可行性的前提下删除冗余移动。作者发现MAPFC实例可自然分解为独立子问题，其中大多数只涉及单智能体、无需智能体间协调，从而提出分解与混合求解框架，在保持精确性和质量的同时显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代基于学习的MAPF求解器常产生可删除的冗余移动，而MAPFC是NP难问题。现有Judgelight使用整数线性规划（ILP）求解，但ILP对所有智能体联合建模，代价由整个实例规模决定，而非由实际需要联合推理的小规模耦合部分决定，因此效率受限。

Method: 首先识别哪些智能体需要协调运动，并据此将实例划分为独立子问题。对于无需协调的部分，提出极轻量求解器；对于需要协调的部分，可使用Judgelight，或提出在较容易问题上更高效的类CBS求解器。整体框架是精确的，且不使用商业ILP求解器。对于协调密集实例，进一步提出区域感知混合规划器，回退到Judgelight。

Result: 无需协调场景下，轻量求解器约比Judgelight快1900倍。整体框架在协调较轻的大多数实例上匹配Judgelight质量且运行显著更快；在协调密集实例上通过混合规划器回退到Judgelight。在所有测试基准上，该规划器相对Judgelight取得中位数10.5倍的逐实例加速。

Conclusion: 通过利用MAPFC实例的可分解性，并针对协调需求采用不同求解策略，可以在保持精确性和解质量的同时大幅提升后优化效率，尤其适用于当前学习型MAPF求解器产生的含冗余移动计划。

Abstract: In this work we study the problem of MAPFC, a post-optimization step for Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) plans where we are given a feasible plan produced by a modern MAPF solver and are tasked with removing avoidable moves while preserving feasibility. This NP-hard problem naturally arises when using learning-based state-of-the-art (SOTA) solvers which construct plans that contain redundant moves that can be removed. Recently, Tang et al. presented Judgelight, which uses Integer Linear Programming (ILP) to solve MAPFC. Importantly, the ILP is constructed over all agents jointly, so its cost is governed by the full instance rather than by the small coupled residue that actually requires joint reasoning. Our key insight, motivating this work, is that MAPFC instances naturally decompose into independent sub-problems, most of which involve a single agent and can be solved without any inter-agent reasoning. To this end, we first identify which agents need to coordinate their motion and partition the instance into sub-problems accordingly. For the cases where no coordination is required, we introduce an extremely lightweight solver that is $\approx\!1{,}900\times$ faster than Judgelight. For cases where coordination is required, Judgelight can be used but we introduce an alternative CBS-like solver which is more efficient on easier problems. The resulting framework is exact, uses no commercial ILP solver, and matches Judgelight's quality while running substantially faster on the coordination-light majority of instances; on the coordination-heavy instances we propose a regime-aware hybrid planner that falls back to Judgelight. Over all benchmarks tested, this planner achieves a median $10.5\times$ per-instance speedup over Judgelight.

</details>

### [13] [Optimize, Learn, Refine: Whole-Body Grasping and Pick-and-Throw with a Spiral Soft Robot](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38202)
*Marwah Basuhai,Tingcong Liu,Ibrahim Alsarraj,Yuhao Wang,Ke Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对软体连续机器人从初始未抓取状态实现全身抓取与拾取投掷的问题，提出基于结果的动作空间优化框架，利用无导数CMA-ES与优化-学习-精化策略，使抓取、加速和释放从柔顺交互中涌现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体连续机器人可利用分布式柔顺性进行全身操作，但在接触不断变化的情况下合成行为仍很困难；现有方法通常需要预先指定接触力、接触位置或身体构型。

Method: 将抓取量化为尖端角扫掠和身体-物体包络，投掷进一步加入释放方向对齐与最小释放速度；采用无导数CMA-ES在优化-学习-精化框架中处理非光滑的动作-结果映射，由任务条件预测器学习热启动，CMA-ES再对未见条件精化。

Result: 仿真中达到492/500次成功抓取（98.4%），三个方向投掷试验成功率分别为98%、97%和94%；学习初始化将抓取成功率从78.6%提升至98.4%，并将CMA-ES中位展开次数从1184降至816；硬件实验中50次执行抓取成功率100%，30次拾取投掷成功率100%，每个方向重复10次。

Conclusion: 仿真与硬件结果共同表明，该框架能有效完成软体连续机器人的全身抓取与拾取投掷任务，并可在不预设接触力、接触位置和身体构型的情况下实现从柔顺交互中涌现的操作行为。

Abstract: Soft continuum robots can exploit distributed compliance for whole-body manipulation, but synthesizing behavior through changing contacts remains difficult. We address whole-body grasping and pick-and-throw from an initially ungrasped state through outcome-based actuation-space optimization. Grasping is quantified by tip angular sweep and body-object enclosure, while throwing further incorporates release-direction alignment and minimum release speed. These objectives allow grasping, acceleration, and release to emerge from compliant interaction without prescribing contact forces, contact locations, or body configurations. Because the resulting actuation-to-outcome mapping is nonsmooth, we utilize derivative-free CMA-ES within an optimize-learn-refine framework. CMA-ES generates solutions for sampled conditions, a task-conditioned predictor learns warm starts, and CMA-ES refines them for unseen conditions. In simulation, the method achieves 492/500 successful grasps (98.4%) and success rates of 98%, 97%, and 94% across three directional throwing trials. Learned initialization increases grasping success from 78.6% to 98.4% while reducing the median rollout count from 1184 to 816 in CMA-ES. Hardware experiments achieve a 100% grasping success rate across 50 executions and a 100% pick-and-throw success rate across 30 executions, with 10 repetitions per direction. Together, these simulation and hardware results demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed framework across both simulated and physical whole-body manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [14] [Ego4WAM: What Matters When Scaling Egocentric Human Data for Robot Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40341)
*Zhihao Sun,Liu Liu,Xinjiang Wang,Haoyi Jiang,Wei Feng,Huiqiang Zhang,Xiaosong Jia,Zhizhong Su,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在统一的世界-动作模型框架下系统研究自我中心人类数据，揭示数据对齐、任务多样性、监督形式与使用策略如何共同影响机器人学习效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自我中心人类数据可扩展机器人经验来源，但人机对齐、行为覆盖和监督信号差异大；现有工作仅显示数据量增加带来收益，尚不清楚哪些数据属性驱动下游机器人性能提升，以及如何在整个训练流程中有效利用这些数据。

Method: 提出Ego4WAM框架，在固定模型主干下，解耦并系统分析人机对齐程度、数据时长与任务多样性、动作监督以及数据使用策略对下游机器人学习的影响；并通过真实机器人和RoboDojo进行闭环策略评估验证。

Result: 对齐的人类演示显著提升分布外泛化能力，并减少目标任务所需机器人数据；数据时长和任务多样性对下游能力影响不同；仅视频监督即使无动作标签仍有效，可为后续视频-动作训练提供强基础。

Conclusion: 不应仅将数据时长视为唯一扩展轴；Ego4WAM表明，对齐、任务多样性、可用监督和使用策略共同决定自我中心人类数据对机器人学习的价值。

Abstract: Egocentric human data provides a scalable source of experience for robot learning, but varies substantially in human-robot alignment, behavioral coverage, and available supervision. Existing work shows favorable scaling with increasing human data, but it remains unclear which data properties drive downstream robot gains and how to use such data throughout the training pipeline. We present a systematic study of egocentric human data with different alignment and supervision under a unified world-action model framework. With the model backbone fixed, we disentangle the effects of human-robot alignment, data duration and task diversity, action supervision, and data usage strategies. We find that aligned human demonstrations substantially improve out-of-distribution generalization and reduce target-task robot data requirements; data duration and task diversity affect downstream capabilities differently; and video-only supervision remains effective without action labels, providing a strong foundation for subsequent video-action training. We validate these findings through closed-loop policy evaluation on both real robots and RoboDojo. Rather than treating data duration as the sole scaling axis, Ego4WAM shows how alignment, task diversity, available supervision, and usage strategy jointly shape the value of egocentric human data for robot learning.

</details>


### [15] [DynaHarness: A Dynamic Physical Harness for Self-Evolving Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40306)
*Haoyuan Deng,Jiebin Liu,Tengxiao Zhang,Langning Yan,Hongye Cao,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DynaHarness：一种动态物理执行框架，通过共享执行契约将语义推理与物理治理耦合，并利用失败归因驱动可复用能力的验证式修订，实现长时程机器人操作的自演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练机器人策略虽提供动作先验，但长时程操作仍要求语义推理与物理执行协同；语义推理的时间尺度比物理交互更粗，且仅凭回合级失败难以判断应修订哪个系统组件。

Method: DynaHarness 采用慢脑/快脑架构：慢脑提出能力及符号参数；快脑对命令进行 grounding 与监控，拒绝未解析动作、替换能力并在需要时请求重规划。物理执行契约约束每个被接受命令，并记录分析技能、恢复技能与冻结 VLA 的执行证据；失败归因在记录中定位故障，指导对可复用能力或执行机制进行定向修订；配对回归检查决定接纳或拒绝，形成自演化闭环。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Pro 的 800 个新采样初始状态上，DynaHarness 达到 75.2%，冻结策略为 17.5%；在相同能力库下，完整动态执行达 74.0%，而名义单步重规划为 63.9%。

Conclusion: 结果表明 DynaHarness 作为动态物理 harness，能有效治理现有能力在执行中的 grounding、监控与协调，显著提升长时程操作成功率；项目页面已提供。

Abstract: Pretrained robot policies provide useful action priors, but long-horizon manipulation still requires coordination between semantic reasoning and physical execution. Semantic reasoning operates at a coarser timescale than physical interaction, while episode-level failures provide limited guidance on which system component should be revised. We propose DynaHarness, a dynamic physical harness that couples semantic reasoning with physical governance through a shared execution contract and turns failure evidence into validated capability revisions. To be more specific, the slow brain proposes capabilities and symbolic arguments, while the fast brain grounds and monitors commands, refuses unresolved actions, substitutes capabilities, and requests replans when needed. The physical execution contract bounds each accepted command and records execution evidence across analytic skills, recovery skills, and the frozen VLA. Failure attribution localizes faults in these records and directs targeted revisions of reusable capabilities or execution mechanisms. Paired regression checks govern admission or rejection, closing the self-evolution loop. On LIBERO-Pro, DynaHarness achieves 75.2% on 800 newly sampled initial states, compared with 17.5% for the frozen policy. With the same capability library, full dynamic execution reaches 74.0% versus 63.9% under nominal one-step replanning. This demonstrates the value of DynaHarness as a dynamic physical harness that governs how existing capabilities are grounded, monitored, and coordinated during execution. Our project page is at this https URL .

</details>


### [16] [GPU-Accelerated Path-Dependent Marginal Information Gain for Autonomous Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40297)
*João Félix Mendes,Rodrigo Ventura,Meysam Basiri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种GPU加速方法计算路径依赖的边际信息增益，利用深度缓冲表示祖先观测以识别重叠，并在规划树中按深度顺序并行评估，从而在保持精度的同时大幅加速自主探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主探索需要评估候选视点的信息增益与执行成本，但基于采样的规划器因体素光线投射计算昂贵，通常假设视点相互独立，忽略同一路径上视点间的观测重叠，导致增益估计不准确。

Method: 该方法用深度缓冲替代存储和合并未知体素：将候选射线投影到祖先节点的深度缓冲以识别观测重叠并排除预期已观测区域；按深度顺序评估规划树以保持视点与优化偏航角间的依赖，同时在GPU上并行处理各层级的候选节点与射线。

Result: 与基于体素哈希图的精确边际增益相比，误差在5-10%以内；在桌面GPU上加速最高118倍，在NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX上加速28倍。集成到两种基于采样的探索规划器并在三个仿真环境中评估，六种组合中有五种减少了达到95%覆盖率的时间；真实实验显示达到95%覆盖率的时间减少30%，并更早终止探索。

Conclusion: 该方法在保持边际增益估计精度的同时显著提升计算效率，能有效缩短探索覆盖时间，适用于资源受限平台并提升自主探索性能。

Abstract: Autonomous exploration demands that robots continuously evaluate candidate viewpoints based on their expected information gain and execution cost. Sampling-based planners estimate this gain by volumetric raycasting and, due to its computational cost, evaluate candidates under an assumption of mutual independence, ignoring the overlap between viewpoints along the same path. This work presents a GPU-accelerated method for computing path-dependent marginal information gain, where instead of storing and merging the observed unknown voxels along each candidate path, previous observations are represented using depth buffers. Candidate rays are projected into the depth buffers of their ancestors to identify observation overlap and exclude regions expected to be observed. The planning tree is evaluated in depth order to maintain the dependency between viewpoints and their optimized yaws, while candidate nodes and rays at each level are processed in parallel on the GPU. The proposed method stays within 5-10% of the exact marginal gain computed using voxel hash maps, with speed-ups of up to 118x on a desktop GPU and 28x on an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX. The method was integrated into two sampling-based exploration planners and evaluated in three simulation environments, where marginal gain reduced the time to 95% coverage in five of the six evaluated planner-environment combinations. Real-world experiments also showed a 30% reduction in the time to 95% coverage, as well as earlier exploration termination times.

</details>


### [17] [STARS: From Spatiotemporal Dynamics to Social Representations in Human-Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40245)
*Nathan Tsoi,Michael J. Munje,Tejas Oberoi,Rishab Maheshwari,Pengen Zheng,Tanush Chauhan,Peter Stone,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 SocialNav-SUB 基准，用视觉问答评估 VLM 在真实社交机器人导航场景中的场景理解能力，发现当前最优 VLM 仍不如简单规则基线和人类共识基线，存在关键差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态、以人为中心的机器人导航需要社会合规决策和稳健场景理解。VLM 在目标识别、常识推理和上下文理解上表现潜力，但其能否准确理解复杂社交导航场景（如智能体间时空关系与人类意图）尚不明确，且现有工作缺乏系统评估。

Method: 构建 SocialNav-SUB：一个面向真实社交机器人导航场景理解的 VQA 数据集与基准。提供统一评估框架，在需要空间、时空和社会推理的 VQA 任务上，将 VLM 与人类基线和规则基线进行对比，并对当前最先进 VLM 开展实验。

Result: 实验表明，表现最好的 VLM 与人类答案一致的概率较高，但仍低于更简单的规则方法基线和人类共识基线，说明当前 VLM 在社交场景理解上存在关键不足。

Conclusion: 该基准为社交机器人导航的基础模型研究奠定基础，并提供框架以探索如何使 VLM 适配真实世界社交机器人导航需求。

Abstract: Robot navigation in dynamic, human-centered environments requires socially-compliant decisions grounded in robust scene understanding. Recent Vision-Language Models (VLMs) exhibit promising capabilities such as object recognition, common-sense reasoning, and contextual understanding, capabilities that align with the nuanced requirements of social robot navigation. However, it remains unclear whether VLMs can accurately understand complex social navigation scenes (e.g., inferring the spatial-temporal relations among agents and human intentions), which is essential for safe and socially compliant robot navigation. While some recent works have explored the use of VLMs in social robot navigation, no existing work systematically evaluates their ability to meet these necessary conditions. In this paper, we introduce the Social Navigation Scene Understanding Benchmark (SocialNav-SUB), a Visual Question Answering (VQA) dataset and benchmark designed to evaluate VLMs for scene understanding in real-world social robot navigation scenarios. SocialNav-SUB provides a unified framework for evaluating VLMs against human and rule-based baselines across VQA tasks requiring spatial, spatiotemporal, and social reasoning in social robot navigation. Through experiments with state-of-the-art VLMs, we find that while the best-performing VLM achieves an encouraging probability of agreeing with human answers, it still underperforms simpler rule-based approach and human consensus baselines, indicating critical gaps in social scene understanding of current VLMs. Our benchmark sets the stage for further research on foundation models for social robot navigation, offering a framework to explore how VLMs can be tailored to meet real-world social robot navigation needs. An overview of this paper along with the code and data can be found at this https URL .

</details>


### [18] [Centralized Multi-UAV Exploration and 3D Reconstruction Using Single-UAV Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40208)
*João Félix Mendes,Meysam Basiri,Rodrigo Ventura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种集中式多无人机探索框架，将现有单无人机采样规划器扩展至多机协作，并在仿真中验证分离起始部署能提升探索速度与覆盖率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将单无人机探索方法扩展到多无人机可提升覆盖速度与鲁棒性，但面临一致建图、安全导航和部署策略等挑战。

Method: 构建集中式多无人机探索框架：基于voxblox适配建图流程，将多无人机深度测量实时融合到共享全局TSDF地图；采用集中式规划复用单无人机采样规划器；集成无人机间避碰与机器人自过滤，防止将其他无人机重建为静态障碍；在仿真中使用RH-NBVP、KRH-NBVP、AEP和KAEP四种采样规划器进行评估，仅做系统级适配。

Result: 在多种环境和两种部署配置（Joint Start，JS；Separated Start，SS）下实验，SS部署在所有规划器中均一致实现更快的探索和更高的覆盖率。

Conclusion: 部署策略对多无人机探索性能具有重要影响，分离起始配置优于联合起始配置。

Abstract: Extending single Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) exploration methods to multi-UAV teams can improve coverage speed and robustness, but introduces challenges such as consistent mapping, safe navigation, and deployment strategy. In this work, we present a centralized multi-UAV exploration framework that enables the use of existing single-UAV sampling-based planners in a multi-UAV setting. The proposed architecture allows multiple UAVs to collaboratively explore unknown environments using a shared global Truncated Signed Distance Field (TSDF) map and centralized planning. Building on the voxblox library, we adapt its mapping pipeline to support real-time fusion of depth measurements from multiple UAVs into a common TSDF representation. In addition, inter-UAV collision avoidance and robot self-filtering mechanisms are integrated into the system to ensure safe navigation and prevent reconstruction of other UAVs as static obstacles. The framework is evaluated in simulation using four sampling-based exploration planners - RH-NBVP, KRH-NBVP, AEP, and KAEP - whose core sampling logic is preserved, with only system-level adaptations for multi-UAV operation. Experiments are conducted across multiple environments and under two deployment configurations: Joint Start (JS), where UAVs are initialized in close proximity, and Separated Start (SS), where UAVs are initialized in distinct locations. Results show that SS deployments consistently achieve faster exploration and improved coverage across all planners, highlighting the importance of the deployment strategy in multi-UAV exploration performance.

</details>


### [19] [Non-Invasive Inspection of Water Canals Using Dronar](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40178)
*Michael Zielinski,Zhizhan Wang,Benjamin Dymond,Reza Razavian,Zhongwang Dou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究开发并验证了一种低成本、非侵入式的运河河床检测系统 dronar，将消费级声纳集成到无人水面艇（USV）上，并在亚利桑那运河进行三次现场试验，证明其可重复测量渠底沉积物变化，作为概念验证可行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放混凝土运河是凤凰城都会区数百万人的关键输水基础设施，但衬砌变形、混凝土开裂和渠底泥沙淤积等问题仅在四年一次的干渠期检查，资源消耗大且无法优先维护最严重渠段。因此需要一种无需排空水、易于部署的非侵入式检测方法。

Method: 研究团队集成现成无人机与声纳技术（dronar）：将消费级声纳系统安装于无人水面艇（USV），换能器置于运河水面下方。在亚利桑那运河 Phoenix 段开展三次现场试验，利用 DownScan 深度剖面检测渠底，比较淤积段与清理后同段，并重复运行清洁段以评估测量重复性。

Result: 淤积段 DownScan 深度剖面一致浅于清理后同段，沿测线偏移可达 15 cm；清洁段重复运行获得高度重叠的 DownScan 深度剖面，表明 dronar 在渠床自然变化下具有可重复测量能力。

Conclusion: dronar 可作为非侵入式运河河床检测的可行概念验证工具，有望支持无需干渠的巡检与维护优先级决策。

Abstract: Open concrete canals play a vital role in water transportation, serving as primary water infrastructure for millions of people across the Phoenix, Arizona, metro area. Over time, the concrete canals can experience a range of issues, including canal lining deformation, cracked concrete, and sediment buildup on the canal floor. Identifying such critical issues is a resource-intensive process, which currently happens only during four-year dry-up cycles. This prevents the maintenance crew from prioritizing operations on the most affected canal segments. To address this issue, the research team has developed and verified an easily deployable and non-invasive method to inspect canal beds without draining the water. This inspection system integrates affordable, off-the-shelf drone and sonar technology (termed dronar). This dronar system includes a consumer-grade sonar system integrated into an unmanned surface vehicle (USV) that carries the sonar transducer just under the surface of the canal water. This paper presents a proof-of-concept demonstration of the dronar system across three field tests on the Arizona Canal in Phoenix. DownScan depth profiles from the sedimented canal segment were consistently shallower than profiles from the same segment after cleaning, with offsets of up to 15 cm observed along the track. Repeated runs over the clean segment produced closely overlapping DownScan depth profiles, confirming that the dronar yields repeatable measurements across the natural variation of the canal bed. These results establish the dronar as a viable proof-of-concept tool for non-invasive canal bed inspection.

</details>


### [20] [Social-WM: Safety-Aware Latent World Models for Robot Social Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40177)
*Zhihao Zheng,Mooi Choo Chuah*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Social-WM：一种从自我中心RGB视频中训练的潜世界模型规划框架，通过建模“名义动作”与“可实现动作”的差异，在安全社会导航中提前评估动作可执行性并减少行人碰撞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全社会导航不仅需要预测动作的未来后果，还需判断名义动作在周围物理与社会约束下是否真正可执行；例如自由空间中可执行的前进动作，朝向行人或障碍物时必须被约束。

Method: Social-WM 从自我中心RGB视频序列训练高效潜世界模型。关键观察是社会导航经验中存在名义动作与可实现动作的系统性差异。方法通过动作条件未来预测学习安全相关后果，目标为每条指令后实际观察到的未来；引入可实现逆动力学目标，将观察到的潜状态转移与真正实现的动作而非名义动作关联。部署时，候选动作在潜世界模型中想象，逆动力学模型估计其可实现性，并用名义-可实现差异作为执行前的安全信号。学习到的动力学与可实现性模型不依赖目标，支持位置目标和图像目标导航。

Result: 在 Social-HM3D 上达到 63.77% 成功率，并将人类碰撞降至 21.67%；在零样本迁移到 Social-MP3D 时仍保持强性能。无需显式行人跟踪、特权人类状态或在线强化学习。

Conclusion: Social-WM 通过学习名义动作与可实现动作之间的差异，提供了无需在线强化学习或特权信息的通用社会导航安全规划能力，并在跨环境零样本迁移中表现稳健。

Abstract: Safe social navigation requires a robot to anticipate not only the future consequences of its actions, but also whether a nominal action can actually be executed under surrounding physical and social constraints. We present Social-WM, an efficient latent world-model planning framework trained from egocentric RGB video sequences. Our key observation is that social-navigation experience contains a systematic discrepancy between the nominal action and the realizable action: a nominal forward action may be fully executed in free space, but needs to be constrained when heading towards a pedestrian or obstacle. Social-WM learns these safety-relevant consequences directly through action-conditioned future prediction, where the target is the actual observed future following each command. We further introduce a realizable inverse-dynamics objective that associates observed latent transitions with the action actually realized rather than the nominal one. At deployment, candidate actions are imagined through the latent world model, and the inverse dynamics model estimates their realizability; nominal--realizable discrepancy then provides a safety signal before execution. The learned dynamics and realizability model remain goal-independent and support both position- and image-goal navigation. On Social-HM3D, Social-WM achieves 63.77% success while reducing human collisions to 21.67%, and maintains strong performance under zero-shot transfer to Social-MP3D, without explicit pedestrian tracking, privileged human state, or online reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [21] [PrefPI: Preference-Guided Steering into Out-of-Distribution Behaviors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40165)
*Seungeun Rho,Wontaek Kim,Danfei Xu,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PrefPI 是一个仅用自生成轨迹相对偏好来引导预训练生成式机器人策略的迭代框架，可推动策略超越初始有效支撑并习得原本罕见或未见的新行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前偏好学习方法主要锐化策略已覆盖的行为模式，难以引导策略进入初始策略有效支撑之外、目标行为很少或从未出现的情形。

Method: 将偏好学习建模为偏好条件生成建模：偏好轨迹定义条件分布，其与更广行为先验的密度比提供隐式偏好信号，并通过无分类器引导（CFG）放大；重复“偏好条件建模+引导”步骤形成偏好引导的策略迭代，将增量改进转化为对原本不可达行为的逼近。

Result: 在扩散策略与 PI0.5 flow-matching VLA 上，于仿真和真实世界中均以有限反馈实现显著行为偏移；真实硬件上仅用 150 条偏好标注轨迹，将物体搬运高度从 10.7 cm 提升到 19.8 cm。

Conclusion: PrefPI 能利用自生成轨迹的相对偏好有效引导预训练生成式机器人策略超越初始有效支撑，实现此前难以或无法获得的新行为。

Abstract: We present PrefPI (Preference-Guided Policy Iteration), an iterative framework for steering pretrained generative robot policies using only relative preferences over self-generated trajectories. Unlike prior preference-learning methods that primarily sharpen modes already represented by the policy, we study steering beyond the initial effective support, where desired behaviors are rarely or never observed under the initial policy. Our key idea is to formulate preference learning as preference-conditioned generative modeling: preferred trajectories define a conditional distribution, whose density ratio with the broader behavior prior provides an implicit preference signal amplified by classifier-free guidance (CFG). Repeating this preference-conditioned modeling and guidance step yields a form of preference-guided policy iteration, turning incremental improvements toward previously inaccessible behaviors. Across diffusion policies and the PI0.5 flow- matching VLA in simulation and the real world, PrefPI produces substantial behavioral shifts with limited feedback. In particular, PrefPI increases object transport height from 10.7 cm to 19.8 cm on real hardware with only 150 preference-labeled trajectories.

</details>


### [22] [Rethinking Legibility in Social Robot Hallway Navigation: Impact of Intent Representation and Human Distraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40158)
*Pranav Goyal,Andrew Stratton,Christoforos Mavrogiannis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究聚焦社交导航中机器人可读运动生成，通过两项受控用户研究（各N=45）在共享模型预测控制框架下比较不同可读性公式，发现交互级意图表示（如通行侧可读性）能提升协调与人类印象，且部分效果在行人分心时仍存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可读运动生成框架多针对静态观察者，而社交机器人导航需在动态且行人注意力分散的环境中，在保障安全的同时清晰传达机器人意图。研究旨在动态受限空间（如走廊）中生成可读运动，并探究意图表示选择与行人注意力水平如何影响导航表现及人类主观印象。

Method: 在共享模型预测控制（MPC）框架下实现多种可读性公式，针对走廊导航开展两项受控用户研究。研究1（N=45）考察意图表示的影响，比较通行侧可读性（含自适应更新）、目的地基可读性与非可读基线；研究2（N=45）考察行人注意力（分心）对可读运动效果的影响。

Result: 研究1显示，通行侧可读性（尤其自适应更新）使人类运动更平滑，并被评价为更有能力、心理与身体需求更低，优于目的地基与非可读基线。研究2表明，可读运动在行人分心时仍能实现平滑的人类运动，但该优势未一致反映在主观评分中。

Conclusion: 社交机器人导航中的有效可读运动受益于交互级意图表示，此类表示有助于人机协调，且部分效果在人类注意力分散时仍能持续。代码已开源。

Abstract: We focus on legible robot motion generation in social navigation settings. Legibility in human-robot interaction (HRI) is often described as the property of robot motion that enables an observer to confidently infer the robot's intent. While mature frameworks exist for generating legible motion in front of static observers, social robot navigation presents a new challenge: the robot must clearly convey its intent while ensuring human safety in dynamic pedestrian environments where human attention is often divided. With the goal of enabling robots to generate legible motion in dynamic and constrained spaces, we investigate how the choice of representation and the level of human attention shape navigation performance and human impressions. Focusing on the ubiquitous and demanding scenario of hallway navigation, we conduct two controlled user studies involving alternative legibility formulations implemented within a shared model predictive control framework. Study 1 (N = 45) investigates the role of intent representation, showing that passing-side legibility, particularly when adaptively updated, leads to smoother human motion and is perceived as more competent and less mentally and physically demanding than destination-based and non-legible baselines. Study 2 (N = 45) examines the effect of pedestrian attention, demonstrating that legible motion allows for smooth human motion even under distraction, even if this is not consistently reflected in subjective ratings. Together, these findings suggest that effective legible motion in social robot navigation benefits from interaction-level intent representations that support coordination, with some effects persisting even when human attention is divided. Code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [23] [Dream4ACT: A Shared Visual Action Interface for Multi-Embodiment Video-Action Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40153)
*Xiangyu Zhu,Jin Xu,Yue Guo,Xin Wu,Yifan Sun,Xiancong Ren,Jianxin Sun,Yong Dai,Xiaozhu Ju*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Dream4ACT 提出动作视图这一共享视觉动作接口，用 URDF 正运动学渲染关节配置，将视频生成模型的时空先验用于跨具身视频-动作联合建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频生成模型（VGM）可为具身观察-动作建模提供强时空先验，但关节空间动作向量缺乏显式图像空间结构，且不同具身的维度与语义不一致；末端执行器可视化又不足以指定机器人执行所需的完整关节构型。

Method: 提出 Dream4ACT 世界模型：使用基于 URDF 正运动学的四虚拟相机渲染目标关节配置，形成共享视觉动作接口 action views，保留具身特定关节几何，并让观察与动作序列共享视频自编码器和扩散 Transformer；通过掩码流匹配，按损坏的未来序列不同，在单一联合训练模型中支持前向动力学、逆向动力学和联合观察-动作生成；从预测动作视图恢复可执行动作时，采用无需训练、受 URDF 约束的多视图恢复机制，无需学习具身特定解码器。

Result: 在 RoboTwin 2.0 上平均成功率为 88.98%，在 TriWorldBench 上总体得分为 65.66，支持有效闭环操作，并在动作条件多视图预测上具有竞争力。

Conclusion: Dream4ACT 通过视觉动作接口统一跨具身动作表示，使 VGM 的时空先验能够服务于关节视频-动作建模与闭环操作，并避免依赖具身特定解码器。

Abstract: Video generation models (VGMs) offer strong spatiotemporal priors for embodied observation--action modeling. However, joint-space action vectors lack explicit image-space structure and vary in dimensionality and semantics across embodiments, making it challenging to directly leverage the rich spatiotemporal priors of VGMs. End-effector visualizations provide an alternative but do not specify the full articulated configuration needed for robot execution. We present Dream4ACT, a world model built for joint video-action modeling across embodiments. To unify action representations across embodiments, we introduce a shared visual action interface, called action views, which render target joint configurations from four prescribed virtual cameras using URDF-based forward kinematics. This shared visual representation preserves embodiment-specific articulated geometry while allowing observation and action sequences to share a video autoencoder and diffusion transformer. Through masked flow-matching, our model supports forward dynamics, inverse dynamics, and joint observation--action generation within a single jointly trained model by varying which future sequences are corrupted. To recover executable action sequences from predicted action views, we propose a training-free, URDF-constrained multiview recovery mechanism, without a learned embodiment-specific decoder. Dream4ACT achieves an average success rate of 88.98\% on RoboTwin~2.0 and an overall score of 65.66 on TriWorldBench, supporting effective closed-loop manipulation and competitive action-conditioned multiview prediction through the visual action interface.

</details>


### [24] [Tactile Curiosity Drives Robot Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40134)
*Klemens Iten,Alexander Proshkin,Bhavya Sukhija,Stelian Coros,Andreas Krause,Pieter Abbeel,Carmelo Sferrazza*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TacEx，将触觉反馈融入基于认知不确定性的探索，通过跨模态分解模型不确定性并将好奇心导向触觉通道，提升机器人操作 RL 的样本效率，并支持离线策略学习与 VLA 后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流强化学习依赖随机动作采样，训练预算大量消耗在无接触的自由空间运动；现有基于模型分歧或认知不确定性的内在动机虽优于各向同性噪声，但仍会奖励功能无关的过渡，例如自由空间中的乱动。作者认为触觉反馈是探索接触动力学的自然信号。

Method: TacEx 将触觉纳入认知不确定性驱动的探索：按感官模态分解模型不确定性，并把好奇心导向触觉通道。该方法无需任务奖励或专家演示即可探索复杂接触动力学，学习操作与抓取；利用触觉驱动好奇心收集交互密集数据集，用于离线学习下游 pick-and-place 策略且无需额外环境交互；进一步用触觉驱动探索对 VLA 模型进行后训练。

Result: 触觉驱动探索促使机器人发现复杂接触动力学并学会抓取和操作；所收集数据支持离线学习下游 pick-and-place 策略且无需额外交互；对初始未使用触觉反馈预训练的 VLA 进行 TacEx 后训练后，下游性能显著提升，同时保持高样本效率。

Conclusion: 触觉反馈可作为有效的内在探索信号；将好奇心锚定到触觉通道能显著提高机器人操作学习的样本效率，并可增强离线策略学习与 VLA 后训练性能。

Abstract: Mastering robot manipulation skills via reinforcement learning (RL) remains largely sample-inefficient. The most common RL algorithms rely on random action sampling to discover new strategies, resulting in agents that allocate most of their training budget to motions in free space, away from the contacts from which manipulation skills emerge. Existing intrinsic motivation methods based on model disagreement or epistemic uncertainty improve on isotropic noise, but they can also reward uncertainty in functionally irrelevant transitions, such as erratic motions in free space. In this work, we argue that tactile feedback provides a natural signal for exploration, and introduce TacEx, a framework that incorporates touch into epistemic uncertainty-driven exploration by decomposing model uncertainty across sensory modalities and directing curiosity toward the tactile channel. By anchoring curiosity to the sense of touch, TacEx drives the robot to discover complex contact dynamics, learning to manipulate and grasp objects without task rewards or expert demonstrations during exploration. The interaction-dense dataset collected through this tactile-driven curiosity supports offline learning of downstream pick-and-place policies without additional environment interaction. We further use tactile-driven exploration to post-train vision-language-action (VLA) models. Although the VLAs are initially pre-trained without tactile feedback, post-training with TacEx substantially improves downstream performance while remaining highly sample-efficient.

</details>


### [25] [Passive Stiffness Shaping in Cable-Suspended Aerial Manipulation via Movable Compliant Anchors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40102)
*Antonio Franchi,Amr Afifi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出重力感知准静态理论，将无人机视为可移动柔顺锚点，用于预测并调控缆索悬吊负载的被动笛卡尔刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 缆索悬吊空中操作具有轻量、适合协同运输与物理交互的优势，但负载端被动机械响应仍缺乏充分理解与系统利用。

Method: 针对任意数量无人机通过绷直、不可伸长缆索连接点负载的场景，在重力平衡下将锚点柔顺与缆索横向几何柔顺按串联、各腿刚度按并联建模；对各向同性锚点，每腿等效为虚拟单边弹性缆索，形成由平衡张力主导的轴向-横向刚度分解。进一步建立从锚点指令位形到负载被动刚度的非线性映射，并利用其微分实现通过锚点重定位的局部保约束刚度整形。同时定义含非线性载具控制、弹性阻尼腱与环境接触的动态刚体验证框架。

Result: 理论可预测并塑造悬吊负载的被动刚度，揭示其由平衡张力与几何/锚点柔顺共同决定；动态验证用于评估该刚度预测在超出解析模型假设时的有效范围。

Conclusion: 该工作为缆索悬吊协同空中操作中的被动刚度建模、调控及模型适用性验证提供了理论基础和验证框架。

Abstract: Cable-suspended aerial manipulation offers a lightweight architecture for cooperative transportation and physical interaction, yet the passive mechanical response perceived at the load remains insufficiently understood and systematically exploited. This work interprets aerial vehicles as movable compliant anchors and develops a gravity-aware quasi-static theory for predicting and shaping the passive Cartesian stiffness of a suspended load. The formulation applies to an arbitrary number of aerial vehicles connected to a point load by taut, straight, inextensible cables. At a selected gravity-loaded equilibrium, aerial-anchor compliance and transverse cable geometric compliance combine in series within each leg, while the leg stiffnesses act in parallel on the load. For isotropic aerial-anchor behavior, each leg is exactly equivalent to a virtual unilateral elastic cable, revealing an axial--transverse stiffness decomposition governed by the equilibrium tension. These results define a nonlinear map from commanded-anchor configuration to passive load stiffness, whose differential enables local constraint-preserving shaping through anchor repositioning. A dynamic rigid-body validation framework with nonlinear vehicle control, elastic-damped tendons, and environmental contact is defined to assess when and to what extent the derived stiffness remains predictive beyond the assumptions of the analytical model.

</details>


### [26] [BatSLAM 2.0: Sequence-Verified Sonar Place Recognition in a Robust Pose Graph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40085)
*Jan Steckel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BatSLAM 2.0：一种仅用声呐的 SLAM 系统，通过改进声学前端、回环候选序列验证器与基于高性能因子图框架的位姿图，解决声呐地点识别歧义与错误回环导致拓扑地图崩溃的问题，并在仿真和真实数据中验证了鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十多年前 BatSLAM 已证明仿生双耳声呐机器人可凭声学信号识别地点并构建拓扑地图，但声呐地点识别本质有歧义，走廊等环境回声序列几乎相同，错误回环会破坏拓扑地图，因此需要更鲁棒的仅声呐 SLAM。

Method: BatSLAM 2.0 由三部分构成：更新的声学前端；跟踪并验证回环候选的序列验证器；在高性能因子图框架上实现的位姿图。系统在仿真和真实世界录音中进行了全面评估。

Result: 在仿真与真实数据中，BatSLAM 2.0 均能鲁棒地创建拓扑地图，抵抗地图崩溃，并稳健扩展地图规模。

Conclusion: 结果表明，通过序列验证回环候选并结合因子图位姿图优化，仅声呐 SLAM 可有效应对声呐地点识别歧义，实现鲁棒且可扩展的拓扑建图。

Abstract: Echolocating bats can navigate dark and cluttered spaces using echolocation. Over a decade ago, BatSLAM showed that a robot with a biomimetic binaural sonar can build a topological map of the environment, by recognizing places from the received acoustic signals. Sonar place recognition, however, is ambiguous by nature: corridors produce nearly identical echo trains, and wrong loop closure can collapse the topological map. In this paper, we introduce BatSLAM 2.0, a novel sonar-only SLAM system built from three elements: an updated acoustic front-end, a sequence verifier that tracks and verifies loop closure candidates and a pose graph implemented on a high performance factor graph framework. The system was thoroughly evaluated both in simulated as well as real world recordings. In both cases, the BatSLAM2.0 algorithm shows the capability of robust topological map creation, countering map collapse, and robust scaling of map size.

</details>


### [27] [Identifiable Decomposition of Submovements in Human Hand Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40012)
*Adrian Prados,James Hermus,Ramon Barber,Sylvain Calinon*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Sub-ID，以时空核相关作为可辨识性准则，通过自适应岭正则化偏好低相关解，实现子运动分解，并在合成与真实三维长时程运动数据上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随意运动可能由离散基元（子运动）组成，但现有分解方法扩展性差，且缺乏原则性标准判断分解是否具有信息量或可辨识性。

Method: 将基元对之间的时空核相关作为可辨识性判据；Sub-ID 将其嵌入自适应岭正则化，使优化器偏向低相关解；当基元共线时可辨识性丢失，空间分离时可恢复。

Result: 在合成数据上，Sub-ID 能恢复真值参数分布，而现有方法失败；当基元重叠严重难以区分时，方法显式检测歧义而非输出误导结果。Sub-ID 还能从真实三维长时程运动中提取子运动，这是先前方法未覆盖的场景。

Conclusion: 该方法有望为运动控制研究和模仿学习识别具有生理依据的基元。

Abstract: Voluntary movements have long been hypothesised to be comprised of discrete primitives called submovements, as a descriptive model of human motor behaviour. However, existing methods scale poorly, and no principled method exists to determine whether a decomposition is informative. We propose a spatiotemporal kernel correlation between primitive pairs as an identifiability criterion. Submovement-Identifiable Decomposition (Sub-ID) embeds this criterion in its adaptive-ridge regularisation, biasing the optimiser toward low-correlation solutions. Identifiability is lost when primitives become collinear and recovered when they diverge spatially. On synthetic data, Sub-ID recovers ground-truth parameter distributions where existing methods fail; furthermore, when primitives overlap too heavily to be distinguished, the method explicitly detects this ambiguity rather than outputting misleading results. Sub-ID extracts submovements from real three-dimensional, long-horizon movements, a regime no prior method addresses. This method has the potential to identify physiologically grounded primitives for motor control research and imitation learning.

</details>


### [28] [Multi-Link Safety Filtering for VLA Policies Around Moving Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40007)
*Yatharth Agarwal,Vijay Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种免训练的运行时防护罩，用五个椭球覆盖机械臂并借稀疏光流跟踪危险物，在不重训VLA策略的情况下显著减少碰撞并提升安全成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅凭任务成功不能证明预训练VLA策略可安全部署于杂乱环境，因为策略可能在完成任务时碰倒无关物体；若要在运行时防护且不重训策略，还需覆盖末端执行器之外的臂段、跟踪移动危险物，并与策略共享机载算力。

Method: 提出免训练shield：以五个椭球覆盖夹爪、腕部和前臂，对每条指令运动用一个barrier程序相对于reset时由RGB-D感知拟合的keep-out椭球进行过滤；随后用稀疏光流将椭球中心随危险物移动，无需重复检测或重拟合。另通过裁剪视觉-语言前缀和减少flow-matching步数优化π0.5策略调用延迟。

Result: 在六个仿真危险物运动条件下，碰撞率从65.62%降至27.27%，safe-success从29.35%升至50.43%。消融表明防护臂段优于仅防护末端执行器，且跟踪可挽回冻结reset估计所损失的大部分保护。在异构边缘硬件上，五椭球barrier在CPU上99分位耗时2.2 ms；裁剪VLM前缀并减少flow-matching步数使集成GPU上的π0.5策略调用从343 ms缩短至177.3 ms。在真实SO-101机械臂四任务中，shielded 16次中3次触碰危险物，unshielded 16次中11次。

Conclusion: 该免训练运行时防护方法能在不重训VLA策略的情况下有效降低杂乱环境中的碰撞、提升安全成功率，且计算可行于边缘设备并能在真实机械臂上复现保护效果。

Abstract: A vision-language-action (VLA) policy can finish a manipulation task while knocking over objects unrelated to it, so task success alone does not show that the policy is safe to deploy in clutter. We study how to keep a pretrained VLA policy clear of such hazards at run time without retraining it, which requires guarding more of the arm than the end effector, following the hazard as it moves, and sharing onboard compute with the policy. Our training-free shield covers the gripper, wrist, and forearm with five ellipsoids and filters every commanded motion through one barrier program against a keep-out ellipsoid fitted from RGB-D perception at reset. Sparse optical flow then carries that ellipsoid's center along with the hazard, with no repeated detection or refitting. Over six simulated hazard-motion conditions, the shield lowers collision from $65.62\%$ to $27.27\%$ and raises safe-success, task completion without collision, from $29.35\%$ to $50.43\%$. Ablations show that guarding the arm links protects beyond end-effector shielding, and that tracking recovers most of the protection lost when the hazard estimate is frozen at reset. On heterogeneous edge hardware, the five-ellipsoid barrier runs on the CPU in $2.2$~ms at the 99th percentile, and trimming the vision--language prefix and taking fewer flow-matching steps shortens each $\pi_{0.5}$ policy call on the integrated GPU from $343$ to $177.3$~ms. On a physical SO-101 arm across four tasks, the arm touched the hazard in 3 of 16 shielded episodes versus 11 of 16 unshielded ones. Project page: this https URL

</details>


### [29] [EWAM: Emergent Depth-Wise Specialization in a Unified Embodied Model -- From Semantic Understanding through Visual Foresight to Action](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39973)
*Hao Wang,Jiajun Wen,Jingzhi Liu,Shuoshuo Xue,Zhiliang Chen,Min Lin,Yicheng Chang,Xiaoyu Guo,Yukang Zhuo,Zheng Chong,Yunshuang Nie,Jian Zhang,Weijia Liufu,Qingman Wu,Heming Xu,Bingchang Song,Dantong Wu,Zhiyuan Wang,Hang Xu,Jianhua Han,Bokui Chen,Shen Zhao,Rui Li,Xiaodan Liang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EWAM是一种以动作为中心的统一具身模型，通过非对称联合注意力让动作token在每一层读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来与动作信息，并在无逐层监督下自发形成从语义理解到视觉预见再到动作生成的深度特化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略强调语义理解，WAM学习环境动态的预测表示；同时暴露给两者的系统往往仍将动作计算集中于单一专家，未能有效统一语义、视觉预见与动作生成。

Method: EWAM采用非对称联合注意力：动作token在每层可读取语义、当前视觉、预测未来和动作信息，而感知专家保持各自角色；无逐层监督。模型分别在跨具身机器人轨迹和人类自我中心视频两种范式下预训练，并引入子任务阶段监督。

Result: 无逐层监督下出现深度特化：浅层动作查询主要关注视觉语言特征，中层关注预测未来帧，深层关注动作token；该交接跨任务复现且跨去噪步稳定。检查点追踪与因果干预表明该特化是学习得到的且动作生成依赖它。仿真和真机实验中超越现有VLA、WAM及混合基线；人类自我中心数据提升跨具身迁移与真机鲁棒性，子任务阶段监督提升长时程完成。

Conclusion: 统一具身学习可诱导从语义理解、经视觉预见、到动作形成的有序内部演进。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies emphasize semantic understanding, whereas world-action models (WAMs) learn predictive representations of environment dynamics. Systems that expose a policy to both sources often still concentrate action computation on a single expert. We present EWAM, an action-centric unified embodied model whose asymmetric joint attention lets action tokens read semantic, current-visual, predicted-future, and action information at every layer while the perceptual experts retain their distinct roles. Without layer-wise supervision, EWAM develops an emergent depth-wise specialization: action queries attend mainly to vision-language features in shallow layers, to predicted future frames in intermediate layers, and to action tokens themselves in deep layers. This handoff replicates across tasks and is stable across denoising steps. Checkpoint tracking and causal interventions show that it is learned and that action generation depends on it. EWAM is pretrained in two separate regimes, one on cross-embodiment robot trajectories and one on human egocentric video. In simulation and real-robot experiments, it surpasses existing VLA, WAM, and hybrid baselines. Human egocentric data improve both cross-embodiment transfer and real-robot robustness, and subtask-phase supervision improves long-horizon completion. Together, these results suggest that unified embodied learning can induce an ordered internal progression from semantic understanding, through visual foresight, to action formation.

</details>


### [30] [When Instructions Retrieve Trajectories: Diagnosing and Mitigating Generalization Failures in VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39971)
*Hung-Jen Chen,Yu-Hsun Hou,Yan-Hong Chen,Yan-Fu Chen,Binghua Cai,Min Sun,Chun-Yi Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLA模型在分布内任务成功率可超90%，但在需要不同动作的反事实变化下仍会失败，这种失败被称为指令-动作绑定失败；作者提出等变反事实训练（ECT），通过反事实演示数据与配对损失，在LIBERO-PRO、CALVIN和真实UR5e上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合鲁棒性分数会掩盖更具体的失败：策略虽同时响应语言与视觉，却未将二者结合以选择任务所需动作。指令易诱发熟悉轨迹族，视觉仅调整执行；模仿目标中窄的条件动作支持使基于视觉grounding的解与基于指令的解在演示上不可区分。

Method: 对微调的π0.5和GR00T-N1.7进行行为分析、内部读出与干预，发现失败rollout保留源行为或切换到另一已演示任务，说明语言未被忽略，且选择与任务条件内部状态相关。据此提出Equivariant Counterfactual Training（ECT）：数据层面提供同一指令在可区分场景中要求不同动作的有效演示；损失层面在同一次更新中将每个演示与其反事实配对共同训练。

Result: 在受控LIBERO-PRO比较中，完整ECT将π0.5的平均位置交换成功率从36%提升至59%。在CALVIN上，由于原始数据已含反事实配对，仅使用ECT损失即在无需新演示的情况下提升五任务完成率。在固定演示预算的真实UR5e上，完整ECT将未见位置成功率从8%提升至88%。

Conclusion: ECT能针对性缓解指令-动作绑定失败，提升VLA模型在反事实变化与未见位置上的泛化，且在有/无新演示、仿真与真实机器人上均有效。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models can exceed 90% success on in-distribution tasks and withstand nuisance changes that preserve the required action, yet fail under counterfactual changes that demand a different action. Aggregate robustness scores can therefore conceal a more specific failure, in which a policy responds to both language and vision yet does not combine them to select the action the task requires. We call this failure instruction-action binding. Instructions cue familiar trajectory families, and visual feedback adjusts their execution. Behavioral analyses of fine-tuned $\pi_{0.5}$ and GR00T-N1.7 policies reveal that failed rollouts often retain the source behavior or switch to another demonstrated task. These switches show that language is not simply ignored. Readouts and interventions connect these choices to task-conditioned internal states. Our analysis of the imitation objective shows how narrow conditional action support can leave grounded and instruction-keyed solutions indistinguishable on the demonstrations. This motivates Equivariant Counterfactual Training (ECT), which acts at two levels. ECT data supply valid demonstrations in which the same instruction requires different actions in distinguishable scenes, while the ECT loss trains each demonstration with its counterpart in the same update. In a controlled LIBERO-PRO comparison, full ECT raises $\pi_{0.5}$'s mean position-swap success from 36% to 59%. On CALVIN, where counterparts already occur in the original data, the ECT loss improves five-task completion without new demonstrations. On a real UR5e under a fixed demonstration budget, full ECT raises unseen-position success from 8% to 88%.

</details>


### [31] [PhasePlan: Ordered Future-Phase Planning for Robot Brain Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39970)
*Xiaoyu Yang,Yafei Zhang,Wensheng Li,Qing Zhan,Nan Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器人脑模型的有序未来阶段规划方法，通过预测未来动作位置对应的任务阶段来条件化动作生成，从而改善动作时序与阶段转换建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数机器人脑模型预测固定长度动作块，这些动作块可能跨越多个任务阶段，会掩盖阶段转换并偏向频繁出现的动作模式，导致动态环境中的动作时序受损。

Method: 从当前多模态观测出发，预测每个未来动作位置的任务阶段；所得规划表征用于条件化对应动作，保持任务进度与动作生成之间的时间对齐。训练时先学习规划器，并在动作模型适配阶段冻结规划器以维持稳定的阶段表征。方法在预训练 π0.5 与 AcrossWAM1.0 机器人脑模型上实例化，定量评估使用 π0.5 实现。

Result: 在传送带操作任务上，相比原始 π0.5 模型，离线关节动作误差降低约 22.5%；同时改善了阶段转换建模和跨阶段动作预测。

Conclusion: 结果表明，有序未来阶段规划对连续动作生成具有价值。

Abstract: Robot brain models integrate vision, language, and robot state to generate actions for complex manipulation tasks. Most predict fixed-length action chunks that may span multiple task phases. This can obscure phase transitions and favor frequent action patterns, compromising action timing in dynamic environments. We propose \method, an ordered future-phase planning method for robot brain models. From current multimodal observations, it predicts the task phase at each future action position. The resulting planning representations condition the corresponding actions, preserving temporal alignment between task progress and action generation. Training first learns the planner, then freezes it during action-model adaptation to maintain stable phase representations. We instantiate \method on pretrained $\pi_{0.5}$ and AcrossWAM1.0 robot brain models. Detailed quantitative evaluation uses the $\pi_{0.5}$ implementation. On conveyor-belt manipulation, \method reduces offline joint-action error by approximately 22.5\% relative to the original $\pi_{0.5}$ model. It also improves phase-transition modeling and cross-phase action prediction. These results demonstrate the value of ordered future-phase planning for continuous action generation.

</details>


### [32] [TACTIC: Temporal and Context-Aware LLM Tactical Planning for Roadside LiDAR Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39969)
*Yiming Gao,Shaocheng Luo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出TACTIC，一种利用多模态大语言模型（MLLM）进行场景感知的路侧LiDAR自适应攻击框架，在灰盒威胁模型下通过语义场景图选择并配置攻击原语，在CARLA中实现100%碰撞率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理LiDAR攻击常使用固定原语和人工选择参数，但攻击效果强烈依赖周围交通环境；固定策略难以适应动态场景，可能遗漏上下文相关的LiDAR失效模式。

Method: 在灰盒威胁模型下，仅依赖攻击者控制的路侧感知栈，不访问受害者LiDAR原始点云或内部处理。局部感知提供车辆度量状态，MLLM结合路侧图像推断关系性交通上下文并构建语义场景图。基于该表示选择并配置两种互补原语：push-away（偏移前车感知距离）和phantom-obstacle braking（注入障碍触发紧急制动）。利用实测交通状态和经验校准约束将战术限定在物理可行区域。为缓解MLLM延迟，异步重叠推理与执行，并由高速率局部感知检测场景变化并触发重规划。

Result: 280次随机CARLA试验中，完整策略碰撞率达100%，固定规则为35%，随机选择为60%，使用默认参数的受限LLM模式选择为75%。物理与图像联合输入成功率为100%，仅物理测量为65%，仅图像为75%。异步Δ刷新将场景突变响应时间从7.4秒降至2.0秒。

Conclusion: 场景依赖的战术规划能够暴露固定攻击策略可能忽略的上下文敏感LiDAR失效模式。

Abstract: Physical LiDAR attacks are often evaluated using fixed primitives and manually selected parameters, despite their strong dependence on surrounding traffic. We present TACTIC, a scene-aware framework that uses a multimodal large language model (MLLM) to coordinate state-adaptive roadside LiDAR attacks. Under a gray-box threat model, TACTIC relies only on an attacker-operated roadside perception stack, without accessing the victim LiDAR's native point clouds or internal processing. Local perception provides metric vehicle states, while the MLLM combines these measurements with roadside imagery to infer relational traffic context and construct a semantic scene graph. Based on this representation, TACTIC selects and configures two complementary primitives: \emph{push-away}, which shifts the perceived range of a lead vehicle, and \emph{phantom-obstacle braking}, which triggers emergency braking through obstacle injection. Measured traffic states and empirically calibrated constraints ground the generated tactics in physically feasible operating regions. To accommodate MLLM latency, TACTIC overlaps reasoning and execution asynchronously while high-rate local perception detects scene changes and triggers replanning. Across 280 randomized CARLA trials, the full policy achieves a 100% collision rate, compared with 35% for a fixed rule, 60% for random selection, and 75% for a restricted LLM using mode selection with default parameters. Joint physical-and-image input achieves 100% success, versus 65% with physical measurements alone and 75% with imagery alone, while asynchronous $\Delta$ refresh reduces scene-mutation response from 7.4 s to 2.0 s. These results show that scene-dependent tactical planning can expose context-sensitive LiDAR failure modes that fixed attack policies may miss.

</details>


### [33] [Magnetic based In-situ Self 3D Pose Estimation for a Modular Soft Tendon-Driven Continuum Robot via IMU-Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39950)
*Zheng Cao,Guo Ning Sue,Xiangyun Bu,David Quinn,Junzhe Hu,Carmel Majidi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合IMU与主动磁场的嵌入式位姿感知框架，用于连续体机器人无外部视觉的实时构型估计与闭环控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连续体机器人因固有柔顺性适合轻柔操作，但连续可变形结构导致精确构型估计困难，尤其在外部视觉系统不可用或受遮挡时。

Method: 将IMU角测量与主动磁场参考融合，改善局部姿态估计并减少运行中累积的姿态误差；该位姿感知方案更新率达16.7 Hz，可提供实时反馈。

Result: 通过闭环控制实验验证，利用估计的机器人构型在与环境物体交互时保持末端执行器于期望位置。

Conclusion: 结果表明分布式磁-惯性传感在连续体机器人实时位姿估计与闭环控制方面具有潜力。

Abstract: Continuum robots are well suited for gentle manipulation because of their inherent compliance and ability to adapt to complex environments. However, their continuously deformable structure makes accurate configuration estimation challenging, particularly when external vision systems are unavailable or obstructed. In this work, we present an embedded pose sensing framework that combines inertial measurement units (IMUs) and active magnetic fields to estimate the robot configuration without relying on external cameras. The angular measurements from the IMU and magnetic-field references are fused to improve local orientation estimation and reduce accumulated orientation error during operation. This pose sensing scheme achieves an update rate of 16.7~Hz, allowing real-time feedback. The proposed system is experimentally validated through closed-loop control, where the estimated robot configuration is used to maintain the end-effector at a desired position while interacting with an object. These results demonstrate the potential of distributed magnetic--inertial sensing for real-time pose estimation and closed-loop control of continuum robots.

</details>


### [34] [Active Mapping of Underwater Litter Using Camera-Sonar Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39898)
*David Rete,Patrick Boros,Lucian Busoniu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种融合前视声呐与相机的主动建图框架，在共享贝叶斯占据图上逐步优化下一最佳视角，以更快定位水下海洋垃圾；仿真表明优于割草机式覆盖和单传感器方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海洋垃圾威胁水下生态，现有调查多沿预设路径或仅用相机/声呐单一模态，分别受低能见度与噪声、低分辨率限制，因此需要能在大范围高效自主定位碎片的主动调查方法。

Method: 构建声呐与相机共同更新的共享贝叶斯占据图；每步求解优化问题选择下一最佳视角，候选视角由两项效用评分：以体素熵探索不确定区域，以及利用可能物体进行开发；各传感器检测与虚警概率由数据得到并随距离和方位变化。

Result: 在逼真水下仿真中，主动建图比割草机式覆盖模式更快找到目标；双传感器融合优于单独使用相机或声呐。

Conclusion: 主动、多模态的贝叶斯建图与视角规划可提升水下垃圾调查的效率与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Marine litter is a growing threat to the underwater ecosystem, driving demand for autonomous survey methods that can locate debris efficiently over large areas. Existing survey methods typically follow predefined paths or operate with a single sensing modality, typically a camera (with image quality suffering in poor-visibility conditions) or sonar (usually noisy and low-resolution). We present an active mapping framework in which a forward-looking sonar and a camera both feed into a shared Bayesian occupancy map, and an optimization problem is solved at each step to decide on the next best view. Candidate viewpoints are scored by a two-term utility that balances exploration of uncertain regions via voxel entropy against exploitation of likely objects.
