Each sensor is characterized by range- and bearing-dependent detection and false-alarm probability tables determined from data. We evaluate the approach in a realistic underwater simulator, demonstrating that active mapping finds objects faster than a lawnmower coverage pattern, and that the dual-sensor approach works better than using either of the individual sensors.

</details>


### [35] [SplineWAM: Adaptive Action Horizons for World Action Models via B-Spline Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39873)
*Jun Guo,Xiaoshen Han,Qiwei Li,Nan Sun,Peiyan Li,Heyun Wang,Hang Lai,Weinan Zhang,Xinghang Li,Huaping Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SplineWAM，将动作轨迹自适应压缩为固定大小的三次 B 样条参数窗口，并按运动特性拟合节点时间，使单次策略调用可解码出不同时间分辨率与持续时长的动作块；同时提出 JP-RTC 支持异步部署中的动作块连续性校正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界动作模型（WAM）每次推理输出固定长度动作块，计算预算在时间上均匀分配，无法在自由空间运动中执行更久，也无法在接触丰富操作中投入更多推理，从而限制云端服务吞吐量。

Method: SplineWAM 用固定参数预算的 cubic B-spline 表示动作轨迹，并让节点时间自适应运动特征，因此同一参数预算可解码为变时间分辨率、变时长的动作块，执行跨度与下次策略调用间隔由预测决定。视频监督按示范轨迹拟合的节点时间对齐，而非均匀网格，使监督帧集中在动作复杂处。异步部署中提出 Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking（JP-RTC），在解码后的机器人原始动作上施加块连续性，并通过解码器反传校正样条参数，使已执行前缀与已提交动作一致。

Result: 在 LIBERO-Plus 和 RoboCasa 上，SplineWAM 相比动作分块 WAM 成功率分别提升 8.2 和 4.4 个百分点，同时每回合策略调用次数减少 22% 和 26%。在三个双臂真实机器人任务异步执行下，性能领先或匹配基线，且每次调用解码执行的运动量是基线的 1.2 至 1.6 倍。

Conclusion: SplineWAM 通过样条参数化动作轨迹与异步连续性校正，实现了非均匀时间预算分配，在提升操作成功率的同时降低策略调用频率并提高单次调用执行效率，适用于云端部署的具身策略。

Abstract: World action models (WAMs) are large embodied policies that jointly predict future video and the actions to execute, emitting a fixed-length action chunk per inference call. Such a policy allocates its computational budget uniformly in time, unable to execute for longer over free-space motion or to spend more inference on contact-rich manipulation, which limits the throughput a WAM can reach when served in the cloud. We present SplineWAM, which adaptively compresses the action trajectory into a fixed-size window of cubic B-spline parameters, fitting the knot times to the characteristics of the motion. One parameter budget then decodes into chunks of varying temporal resolution and duration, and both the executed span and the interval until the next policy call follow from the prediction itself. Aligning the video supervision to the fitted knot times of the demonstration rather than to a uniform grid concentrates the supervised frames where the action trajectory is complex. For asynchronous deployment we introduce Jacobian-Pullback Real-Time Chunking (JP-RTC), which imposes chunk continuity on the decoded raw actions the robot executes rather than on the spline parameters, and corrects the parameters through the decoder so that the executed prefix agrees with the actions already committed. On LIBERO-Plus and RoboCasa, SplineWAM improves success rate over an action chunking WAM by $8.2$ and $4.4$ points while cutting policy calls per episode by 22% and 26%. On three bimanual real-robot tasks under asynchronous execution, it leads or matches the baseline while decoding 1.2 to 1.6 times as much executed motion per call.

</details>


### [36] [Magic-W0: A Structured World-Action Foundation Model for Physical Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39870)
*Xuhua Chen,Zhenhan Yin,Yuan Zhang,Lingfeng Zhang,He Zheng,Tong Mu,Shun Zuo,Dian Zhou,Di Wu,Xuan Zhou,Shaojie Wan,Rongtian Shen,Qiulong Xu,Yiduo Li,Yinglong Wang,Yanqian Wang,Kun Wang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Magic-W0世界-动作基础模型，用结构化世界转移联合建模物理状态演化与连续动作，并通过层级对齐交互架构耦合预测与控制，在仿真与真机任务上取得领先表现和强泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界-动作模型多依赖未来观测重建或通用隐空间预测，缺乏结构化、面向控制且与动作生成紧耦合的世界表示。

Method: Magic-W0将交互表示为结构化世界转移：当前状态结合视觉语言上下文与当前3D几何；转移用3D运动表征动作诱导的三维变化；未来状态用未来语义描述任务相关结果。提出层级对齐的世界-动作交互架构，使演化中的动作假设条件化世界转移预测，同时预测的世界表示持续指导动作生成。模型在大规模第一人称人类操作、UMI、真机与仿真数据上预训练，并利用预训练视觉模型对几何、3D运动和未来语义进行隐监督。

Result: 推理时干预表明，结构化世界表示会随候选动作变化系统性响应，动作相关信息经共享3D表示传播至未来语义预测。在RoboDojo-Sim上平均Score达27.10，为对比世界-动作模型中最高；在多个真机任务上，经有限下游数据微调后仍展现强下游性能，支持泛化与快速适应。

Conclusion: Magic-W0通过结构化世界-动作表示与层级对齐交互，实现预测与控制紧耦合，在仿真和真实任务中有效，并具备泛化与快速适应能力。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) augment robot policies with action-conditioned environment dynamics, yet existing approaches largely rely on future observation reconstruction or generic latent prediction and lack structured, control-oriented world representations tightly coupled with action generation. We introduce Magic-W0, a world-action foundation model that jointly models structured physical state evolution and continuous actions. Magic-W0 represents interaction as a Structured World Transition consisting of Current State, Transition, and Future State. Current State combines vision-language context with Current 3D Geometry; Transition is represented by 3D Motion capturing action-induced three-dimensional changes; and Future State is represented by Future Semantics describing task-relevant outcomes. To couple prediction and control, we propose a layer-aligned world-action interaction architecture in which evolving action hypotheses condition world-transition prediction, while predicted world representations continuously inform action generation. Magic-W0 is pre-trained on large-scale egocentric human manipulation, UMI, real-robot, and simulation data, with latent supervision for geometry, 3D motion, and future semantics from pre-trained visual models. Inference-time interventions show that structured world representations respond systematically to changes in candidate actions and that action-related information propagates through shared 3D representations into future semantic predictions. On RoboDojo-Sim, Magic-W0 achieves an average Score of 27.10, the highest among the compared WAMs. Across multiple real-robot tasks, it also demonstrates strong downstream performance after fine-tuning with limited downstream data, supporting generalization and rapid adaptation.

</details>


### [37] [RL-Guided PAC-NMPC for Probabilistically-Safe Perception-Based Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39854)
*Adam Polevoy,Dillon Capalongo,Katherine Tang,Mark Gonzales,Marin Kobilarov,Joseph Moore*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出结合随机非线性模型预测控制（SNMPC）与强化学习（RL）的方法，通过概率模型和PAC-NMPC硬约束实现未知环境下概率安全的感知导航，并在仿真与固定翼无人机硬件实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 感知型RL导航在未知环境中通常缺乏概率安全保证，难以兼顾长时程性能与碰撞概率约束。

Method: 先用RL训练概率actor-critic和传感器预测模型；再将其嵌入基于采样的SNMPC框架PAC-NMPC，通过硬约束提供有限时间统计保证，包括碰撞概率与价值函数改进；使有限时域SNMPC策略在期望上降低价值函数，从而接近RL长时程性能并满足概率安全约束。

Result: 仿真实验表明，该方法能提升基于感知的RL导航策略安全性，并可扩展至高维系统、大传感器输入空间和复杂非线性动力学；硬件实验在未知环境中使用敏捷固定翼飞行器进行视觉导航，性能得到提升。

Conclusion: 该方法可有效结合RL的长时程性能与SNMPC的概率安全保证，适用于高维感知导航任务，并具有实际硬件部署可行性。

Abstract: In this paper, we present an approach for combining stochastic nonlinear model predictive control (SNMPC) and reinforcement learning (RL) to enable probabilistically-safe perception-based navigation in unknown environments. Our method first uses RL to train probabilistic actor-critic and sensor prediction models. We then leverage these probabilistic models in a sampling-based SNMPC framework known as Probably Approximately Correct (PAC)-NMPC, which uses hard constraints to enforce finite-time statistical guarantees on the probability of collision and value function improvement. By ensuring that our finite-horizon SNMPC policies decrease the value function in expectation, we can approach the long-horizon performance of the RL approach while satisfying probabilistic safety constraints. Through simulation experiments, we show that our approach can improve the safety of perception-based RL navigation policies and scale to high dimensional systems with large sensor input spaces and complex nonlinear dynamics. We also demonstrate our approach through hardware experiments, showing improved performance for vision-based navigation with an agile fixed-wing aerial vehicle in unknown environments.

</details>


### [38] [Toward Real-Time VLAs: Stage-Aware Two-Step Flow Denoising and System-Level Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39822)
*Di Wu,Rongtian Shen,Ping Liu,Yan Shen,Zhenhan Yin,Shun Zuo,Xuhua Chen,He Zheng,Lingfeng Zhang,Jianglin Zhang,Tao Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLA模型低速率推理与高速率机器人执行之间的时序鸿沟，提出两阶段非均匀去噪与分布式实时VLA框架，并在长时衣物折叠任务上评估六种实时执行方法，支持推理效率与系统时序的联合优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型存在推理速率低而机器人执行速率高的时序差距。端到端延迟中，Flow Matching重复去噪显著增加推理开销，机器人侧延迟主要来自感知采集、通信调度和物理响应。速度场分析显示早期积分阶段幅值与方向较稳定，末端步骤方向校正更强，因此需利用阶段异质性并联合优化模型推理与机器人系统时序。

Method: 通过端到端延迟测量刻画模型推理与机器人执行链；分析速度场并提出两阶段非均匀去噪，将去噪步数从10降至2，模型推理时间从61.557 ms降至21.956 ms；构建分布式实时VLA框架，支持独立推理、动作发布与机器人控制速率，模块化观测采集和动作溯源日志；以π0.5为基线，在长时物理衣物折叠任务上评估六种实时执行方法。

Result: 在基于训练的方法中Legato整体最佳，在免训练方法中Temporal Smoothing最佳；二者在任务成功率、完成时间、动作连续性和加速度平滑性方面表现较强。将两步去噪与代表性执行方法结合，可显著降低推理成本，同时仅带来小幅任务性能下降。

Conclusion: 结果说明实时VLA需要联合优化模型推理效率与机器人系统时序，协同设计去噪调度、执行策略和分布式控制时序。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models face a timing gap between low-rate inference and high-rate robot execution. We characterize this gap through end-to-end latency measurements of model inference and the robot execution chain. Repeated Flow Matching denoising contributes substantially to inference cost, while robot-side delays mainly arise from perception acquisition, communication scheduling, and physical response. Analysis of the velocity field shows relatively stable magnitude and direction in early integration, followed by stronger directional correction near the terminal steps. Based on this stage heterogeneity, we propose two-stage non-uniform denoising, reducing the number of steps from 10 to 2 and model-inference time from 61.557 ms to 21.956 ms. We also develop a distributed real-time VLA framework with independent inference, action-publication, and robot-control rates, modular observation acquisition, and action-provenance logging. Using {\pi}0.5 as the baseline, we evaluate six real-time execution methods on a long-horizon physical garment-folding task. Legato performs best overall among training-based methods, while Temporal Smoothing leads among training-free methods; both perform strongly in task success, completion time, action continuity, and acceleration smoothness. Combining two-step denoising with representative execution methods substantially reduces inference cost with a small reduction in task performance. These results motivate joint optimization of model-inference efficiency and robot-system timing.

</details>


### [39] [Learning from Runtime Feedback through Failure-Bank Self-Evolution for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39820)
*Mingyue Cui,Zheyuan Liu,Yihan Zhu,Zheyuan Zhang,Meng Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FailBank 是一个四阶段自演化框架，将运行时 CBF 安全反馈转化为持久策略改进，从而在 VLA 机器人操作中同时提升任务成功率并降低策略引发的累积代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型虽具备较强泛化能力，但在复杂环境中需平衡任务成功与意外接触。运行时 shield 仅纠正单个动作，不改变底层策略，因此反复分歧会造成持续的策略-shield 不匹配，阻碍任务推进。

Method: FailBank 包含四阶段自演化流程：采集阶段以固定 CBF 安全模块作为仅观察教师，在策略控制下生成反事实纠正；结果感知准入将有用提议转化为纠正目标，并将成功且未被纠正的动作保留为安静锚点，用于带保护的 LoRA 更新。

Result: 在 VLA-Arena 基准、两个难度等级和两个 VLA 主干上评估。相比基础策略，FailBank 改善联合成功-代价工作点：两个主干任务成功率分别提升 8.5 和 6.9 个百分点，策略引发的累积代价分别降低 35.6% 和 23.8%。相比运行时 shielding，成功率分别提升 25.4 和 9.5 个百分点，同时策略引发的累积代价相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，运行时反馈不仅可作为临时动作约束，还可作为持久策略监督，用于持续改进 VLA 策略。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models generalize broadly across robotic manipulation tasks, but complex environments require balancing task success with unintended contact. Runtime shields can correct individual actions, but they leave the underlying policy unchanged, so repeated disagreements may create a persistent policy-shield mismatch that blocks task progress. To address this challenge, we introduce FailBank, a four-stage self-evolving framework that converts runtime feedback into persistent policy improvement. During collection, a fixed CBF-based safety module serves as an observe-only teacher, producing counterfactual corrections while the policy remains in control. Outcome-aware admission then converts useful proposals into corrective targets and retains successful uncorrected actions as quiet anchors for guarded LoRA updates. We evaluate FailBank on the VLA-Arena benchmark across two difficulty levels and two VLA backbones. Compared with the base policies, FailBank improves the joint success-cost operating point. Across the two backbones, FailBank improves task success rate by 8.5 and 6.9 percentage points, while reducing policy-induced cumulative cost by 35.6\% and 23.8\%, respectively. Compared with runtime shielding, FailBank raises task success rate by 25.4 and 9.5 percentage points, while maintaining comparable policy-induced cumulative cost. These results show that runtime feedback can serve as persistent policy supervision rather than only as a temporary action constraint.

</details>


### [40] [Tool-Policy Co-Design for Powder Weighing in Laboratory Automation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39797)
*Nikola Radulov,Xin Yang,Kevin S. Luck,Gabriella Pizzuto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向机器人粉末称重的工具-策略协同设计框架，通过双层优化同时优化分粉工具形态与控制策略，在真实实验中相对标准工具降低45%称重误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主粉末称重是实验室自动化的瓶颈之一，源于异质材料复杂的非线性动力学；现有机器人化学家沿用为人类手部灵巧性设计的标准工具，其固定几何限制了控制策略可调节的动力学范围。

Method: 将问题建模为双层优化，在目标粉末流动性分布上最小化分粉误差：外层用贝叶斯优化与Hyperband调整工具深度、宽度、边缘尖刺拓扑等设计参数，内层为每个候选形态优化控制策略；并引入几何相似度度量，从结构相似设计的缓存策略热启动策略训练，在相同算力下多探索28%的配置；评估采用流动性信息驱动的机器人-材料仿真框架。

Result: 在涵盖七种具有不同物理动力学的材料的机器人粉末称重任务中，协同设计的工具形态使真实世界称重误差相对标准工具降低45%，且在先前未见材料上同样有效；相同算力预算下配置探索量提升28%。

Conclusion: 该方法能够同时适配控制策略与物理工具至目标材料动力学，为实验室自动化中的材料操作提供了新范式。

Abstract: Autonomous powder weighing is one of many bottlenecks in laboratory automation due to the complex, non-linear dynamics of heterogeneous materials. Robot chemists performing this task utilise standard tools shaped for the dexterity of human hands, whose fixed geometry sets the dynamics that the control policy needs to regulate. This work introduces a tool-policy co-design framework that concurrently optimises the morphology of a dispensing tool and its control policy for use by robots in chemistry laboratories, formulated as a bi-level optimisation that minimises dispensing error over a target distribution of powder flowabilities. The outer loop varies tool-design parameters such as tool depth, width and rim spike topology using Bayesian optimisation and hyperband, while an inner loop optimises a control policy for each candidate morphology. We also introduce a geometric similarity metric that warm-starts policy training from cached policies of structurally similar designs, exploring 28% more configurations under the same compute budget. The proposed framework is evaluated on a robotic powder weighing task across seven materials with distinct physical dynamics in a flowability-informed robot-material simulation framework. Experimental results demonstrate that our co-designed tool morphology reduces real-world weighing errors by 45% relative to a standard tool, including on previously unseen materials. These results demonstrate our method can adapt both the control policy and the physical tool to the dynamics of the target material, bringing a new paradigm for material manipulation to the field of laboratory automation.

</details>


### [41] [DiffWAM: A Fast and Efficient Navigation World Action Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39763)
*Mo Zhu,Yuze Wu,Xijie Huang,Xiao Cui,Fei Gao,Xin Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DiffWAM，一种几何条件导航世界-动作模型，可直接从冻结视频基础模型的预测表征中恢复无人机连续3D运动，避免部署时未来视频合成与几何重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预训练视频基础模型蕴含丰富语义与时空先验，但将其用于无人机具身导航通常依赖昂贵的未来视频合成和几何重建。本文探究能否直接从冻结视频模型的预测表征中恢复未来视觉预测中隐含的运动信息。

Method: DiffWAM将多级预测特征直接映射为连续相机轨迹；Grid-Motion模块保留时空运动关联，Latent2Pose利用首帧几何将其接地为具有度量意义的3D运动。完整视频展开和几何重建仅用于离线监督，部署时无需未来视频解码与多帧重建。FastDreamer将预测与几何计算同飞行过程重叠，并通过时间戳感知的异步轨迹交接实现无人机连续执行。

Result: 在1000样本DiffWAM-1000基准上，DiffWAM取得0.3492 m轨迹RMSE和74.40%终点成功率；真实世界实验展示了受限穿越、环绕、S形飞行和多阶段导航等复杂行为；板载DiffWAM-Flash在NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor上达到1.08 s模型流水线延迟。

Conclusion: 预测视频表征可被高效接地为连续3D运动，为“先生成再重建”的导航流水线提供了一种直接替代方案。

Abstract: Pretrained video foundation models encode rich semantic and spatiotemporal priors for embodied navigation, yet converting these priors into UAV motion typically requires expensive future-video synthesis and geometric reconstruction. We investigate whether the motion implicit in future visual prediction can instead be recovered directly from the predictive representations of a frozen video model. To this end, we present DiffWAM, a geometry-conditioned navigation world-action model that directly transforms multi-level predictive features into continuous camera trajectories. Its Grid-Motion module preserves spatial-temporal motion associations, while Latent2Pose grounds them with first-frame geometry to recover metrically meaningful 3D motion. Complete video rollouts and geometric reconstruction are required only for offline supervision, eliminating future-video decoding and multi-frame reconstruction during deployment. We further introduce FastDreamer, which overlaps predictive and geometric computation with ongoing flight and performs timestamp-aware asynchronous trajectory handoff for continuous UAV execution. DiffWAM achieves a trajectory RMSE of 0.3492 m and an endpoint success rate of 74.40% on the 1,000-sample DiffWAM-1000 benchmark, while representative real-world experiments demonstrate complex behaviors including constrained traversal, orbiting, S-shaped flight, and multi-stage navigation. An onboard DiffWAM-Flash implementation further reaches 1.08 s model-pipeline latency on NVIDIA Jetson AGX Thor. These results demonstrate that predictive video representations can be efficiently grounded into continuous 3D motion, providing a direct alternative to generate-then-reconstruct navigation pipelines. Project page: this https URL .

</details>


### [42] [Experience-Driven Continual Learning of Terrain Traversability for Quadruped Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39755)
*Luca Bricarello,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Alberto Sanchez-Delgado,Fulvio Mastrogiovanni,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种面向四足机器人的持续学习导航管线：利用预接触图像预测地形-机器人交互结果，并通过带验证门控的经验回放持续更新预测器，最终生成保守可通行性地图用于导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生地形上安全高效地导航，需要在接触前预测机器人是否会打滑、如何受力以及能耗等交互后果；仅依赖几何和视觉外观无法揭示这些信息，因此需要从运动经验中学习预接触图像到交互指标的映射，并能随新接触持续适应。

Method: 使用冻结的DINOv3主干生成预接触视觉描述子，将其映射到五种按测量可靠性加权的本体感知指标：平面足端打滑、平均法向地面反作用力、牵引指数、运输成本、触地加载率。采用紧凑的证据回归器同时预测这些指标及其偶然不确定性和认知不确定性。持续适应结合有界经验回放与验证门控：仅当候选模型在近期留出数据上提升、且历史留出数据退化在容许范围内时，才替换部署预测器。预测值与认知不确定性被投影到局部多层地图，并组合为保守可通行性评分图，其属性权重无需重新训练即可调整。ROS2实现支持在Unitree Go2仿真和硬件上评估，各域分别训练模型。

Result: 在硬件上对三个此前未见过地形组成的顺序数据流，门控回放相比无门控回放将最终锚点负对数似然（NLL）退化降低23.1%，同时对新地形适应性能相近。

Conclusion: 该方法表明，利用预接触视觉与运动经验进行持续学习，可预测四足机器人地形交互后果并构建可调保守可通行性地图，且验证门控的经验回放能在适应新地形时更好缓解对历史知识的遗忘。

Abstract: Safe and efficient quadruped navigation over unfamiliar terrain requires predicting terrain-robot interaction before contact: geometry and visual appearance alone cannot reveal how the robot will slip, load its feet, or expend energy. This paper presents a continual learning pipeline that uses locomotion experience to learn these interaction outcomes from pre-contact images and continually updates the predictions as new contacts are observed. Pre-contact descriptors, produced by a DINOv3 backbone model frozen during training, are mapped to five proprioceptive indicators weighted according to measurement reliability: planar foot slip, mean normal ground-reaction force, traction index, cost of transport, and touchdown loading rate. A compact evidential regressor allows us to predict these indicators together with aleatoric and epistemic uncertainty from the visual descriptors. Continual adaptation combines bounded experience replay with a validation gate: candidate models replace the deployed predictor only when they improve performance on recent held-out data while keeping degradation on historical held-out data within a prescribed tolerance. Predictions and epistemic uncertainty are projected into a local multilayer map and combined into a conservative traversability score map whose property weights can be adjusted without retraining. The resulting map is used for downstream navigation tests. The ROS2 implementation supports evaluation on a Unitree Go2 in simulation and on hardware, with models trained separately in each domain. On a sequential hardware stream over three previously unseen terrains, gated replay reduces final anchor negative log-likelihood (NLL) degradation by 23.1% relative to replay without the gate while attaining similar new-terrain adaptation.

</details>


### [43] [ChunkTrust: Adapting Execution Horizons for Robot Policies with Action-Expert Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39754)
*Fanding Huang,Jingyan Jiang,Shifeng Bao,Mingkang Pu,Shiwei Li,Jing Xu,Shijia Xu,Guanbo Huang,Chenghao Gu,Yuzhi Huang,Chenxin Li,Faisal Nadeem Khan,Huan Yang,Yan Wang,Cheng Chi,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ChunkTrust 将机器人基础策略重规划前的执行动作数（执行时域）建模为可从动作专家证据推断的潜变量，提出免训练 AHS 与可选轻量 QHA，在多个仿真基准和真实家庭任务上提升成功率与过程分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人基础策略通常预测动作块，但重规划前应执行多少动作取决于当前任务阶段；固定执行时域超参数难以适应不同阶段，因此需要自适应推断。

Method: 将执行时域视为潜变量而非固定超参数。免训练的 Action-aware Horizon Selector (AHS) 结合块内生成轨迹的频谱稳定性与块间已执行历史和预测动作的连续性；用带核遗忘的在线 Beta 后验跨重规划跟踪时域偏好。可选的 Query-based Horizon Adapter (QHA) 从互补证据学习上下文条件密集先验，并与当前证据及回合局部 Beta 记忆融合，同时冻结基座策略。消融分析两项证据项、时间记忆和学习先验的贡献。

Result: 在 RoboTwin2.0 与 RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop 上，AHS 对每个评估的基座策略配置均提升任务平均成功率：π0.5 在 RoboTwin2.0 全部 50 个任务上提升 +6.80 个百分点，Qwen3GR00T 在 RoboCasa 上提升 +9.67 个百分点。AHS+QHA 在八任务 π0.5 评估中将相对 Base 的增益提高到 +9.44 个百分点。在四个真实家庭任务中，AHS 将等任务均值归一化过程分数从 50.4% 提升至 57.5%。

Conclusion: 执行时域可由动作专家证据驱动地推断；AHS 与可选 QHA 能有效提升多种机器人基础策略的任务成功率与真实过程表现，且基座策略可保持冻结。

Abstract: Robot foundation policies predict action chunks, but how many actions to execute before replanning depends on the current task phase. We introduce ChunkTrust, which treats the execution horizon as a latent variable inferred from action-expert evidence rather than a fixed hyperparameter. Its training-free Action-aware Horizon Selector (AHS) combines intra-chunk spectral stability of generation traces with inter-chunk continuity between executed history and predicted actions. An online Beta posterior with kernel forgetting tracks horizon preferences across replans. A lightweight Query-based Horizon Adapter (QHA) optionally learns a context-conditioned dense prior from complementary evidence, fused with current evidence and episode-local Beta memory while the base policy remains frozen. Across RoboTwin2.0 and RoboCasa GR1 Tabletop, AHS improves overall task-averaged success for each evaluated base-policy configuration, including gains of +6.80 percentage points on $\pi_{0.5}$ over all 50 RoboTwin2.0 tasks and +9.67 percentage points on Qwen3GR00T in RoboCasa. AHS+QHA raises the gain over Base to +9.44 percentage points on the eight-task $\pi_{0.5}$ evaluation. On four real-world household tasks, AHS improves the equal-task mean normalized process score from 50.4% to 57.5%. Ablations examine the contributions of both evidence terms, temporal memory, and the learned prior. Project page is this https URL

</details>


### [44] [Beyond Policy Alignment: Closing the Planning-Learning Loop for Robot Control with Learned World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39751)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Yuhan Liu,Abdeslam Boularias*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PL-MPC 在 TD-MPC 的规划-学习闭环中，额外改进 critic 监督与规划器终端价值估计，在 HumanoidBench 部分任务显著提升，并实现零样本 sim-to-real 迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于学习世界模型的规划将在线轨迹优化与学习到的价值/策略函数结合；规划器决定学习经验，critic 与 actor 又反过来评分和提议未来计划，形成闭环。TD-MPC 是该设计的代表，近期策略约束变体仅强化闭环的一部分。

Method: PL-MPC 保持世界模型架构与 MPPI 优化器不变，引入三项改进：1）混合多步 TD 目标，使 critic 更新在自举前接触更多真实奖励；2）不一致性感知的终端价值估计，降低 MPPI 规划中不确定 critic 值的影响；3）回报加权 actor 蒸馏，强调高回报回合中规划器执行的动作。

Result: 在 HumanoidBench 上，最大增益出现在 balance-hard，TAR 从 98±18 提升至 387±255；hurdle 从 199±13 提升至 466±200。更广泛基准上性能仍依赖任务，在 DMControl 上保持竞争力。受控消融显示两个任务中组件交互不同。进一步在 7-DoF KUKA IIWA14 的 wrench-nut alignment 上展示零样本 sim-to-real 迁移，在训练物体尺寸和两个未见尺寸上均取得高于 TD-M(PC)^2 的观测成功率。

Conclusion: PL-MPC 通过同时改进 critic 监督和规划器终端价值估计，增强了规划-学习闭环，在部分高维控制任务上带来大幅收益，并具备任务依赖性和实际迁移潜力。代码与数据将公开。

Abstract: Planning with learned world models combines online trajectory optimization with learned value and policy functions for high-dimensional control. Because the planner determines the experience used for learning, while the learned critic and actor in turn score and propose future plans, planning and learning form a closed feedback loop. TD-MPC is a prominent instance of this design. Recent policy-constrained variants strengthen one part of the loop by aligning the learned policy with planner behavior. We introduce PL-MPC (Planning-Learning MPC), which additionally modifies critic supervision and planner terminal-value estimation. Hybrid multi-step TD targets expose critic updates to more realized rewards before bootstrapping; disagreement-aware terminal estimates reduce the influence of uncertain critic values during MPPI planning; and return-weighted actor distillation emphasizes planner-executed actions from high-return episodes. The world-model architecture and MPPI optimizer are otherwise unchanged. On HumanoidBench, the largest gains occur on \texttt{balance-hard}, where Total Average Return (TAR) increases from $98\pm18$ to $387\pm255$, and \texttt{hurdle}, from $199\pm13$ to $466\pm200$; performance across the broader benchmark remains task dependent, and PL-MPC remains competitive on DMControl. Controlled ablations show different component interactions across the two tasks. We further demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer on wrench-nut alignment with a 7-DoF KUKA IIWA14, obtaining higher observed success than TD-M(PC)^2 on the training object size and two unseen sizes. Code and data will be available at: this https URL .

</details>


### [45] [RoboCoach: World Models as Active Coaches for Compositional Robot Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39685)
*Jiajun Liu,Yifan Chen,Yichao Liu,Jiayi Zhang,Ruoqu Chen,Shaoxuan Xie,Guocai Yao,Mengdi Xu,Sen Cui,Changshui Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ROBOCOACH：一种由世界模型引导的“教练式”自改进框架，通过想象失败来定向请求演示并更新技能专家，从而以较少额外数据提升长时程机器人操作性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程机器人操作依赖可复用技能进行任务组合，但用额外端到端演示改进这些组合代价高昂；实际系统需要同时决定“下一步教什么”和“把监督信号用在哪里”。

Method: ROBOCOACH 的核心是 Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve（RIDI）循环：在共享的动作条件世界模型 COACHWORLD 中执行可复用技能专家，利用进度判定器记录首个未完成的子任务；聚合这些记录后，选择需要采集哪些子任务演示，以及更新哪些专家适配器。

Result: 在两个仿真套件和两个真实机器人平台上，22 个任务-策略对的想象成功率与部署成功率相关系数 rho=0.840；在匹配数据预算和更新计划下优于对照基线。仅增加 150 个子任务演示，Franka 成功率从 13.3% 升至 75.0%，AgileX 从 40.0% 升至 83.8%；coached experts 迁移到四个留出组合平均成功率达 35.0%，而使用均匀采集演示更新的共享策略基线为 0%。

Conclusion: 世界模型可作为主动教练，将想象失败转化为定向监督，用于模块化策略改进，从而高效提升长时程机器人操作的任务组合能力。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation reuses skills across many task compositions, but improving these compositions with additional end-to-end demonstrations is costly. A practical self-improving system must decide both what to teach next and where to apply that supervision. We present ROBOCOACH, a world-model-guided coaching framework that uses imagined failures to guide demonstration requests and expert updates. Its Route-Imagine-Diagnose-Improve (RIDI) loop executes reusable skill experts inside COACHWORLD, our shared action-conditioned world model, and uses a progress judge to record the first subtask that fails to complete. Aggregated records select which subtask demonstrations to acquire and which expert adapters to update. Across two simulation suites and two real-robot platforms, imagined and deployed success correlate over 22 task-policy pairs (rho = 0.840). Controlled comparisons show that our coaching method outperforms matched baselines under matched data budgets and update schedules. With only 150 additional subtask demonstrations, success rises from 13.3% to 75.0% on Franka and from 40.0% to 83.8% on AgileX. The coached experts also transfer to four held-out compositions, achieving an average success of 35.0%, compared with 0% for a shared-policy baseline updated with uniformly acquired demonstrations. Together, these results show that world models can serve as active coaches, turning imagined failures into targeted supervision for modular policy improvement. Project Page: this https URL

</details>


### [46] [From Local Whole-Body VLA Behaviors to Scene-Scale Aerial Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39670)
*Weixiang Guo,Rui Jin,Haotian Jin,Xinhang Xu,Ruiyang Liu,Haoran Zhao,Yi Wang,Weiqi Gai,Kun Cao,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向关节式无人机操作器（UAM）的合成策略训练与场景级执行统一框架，用可重构场景管线生成训练数据，并用MPAR、关系场景图和拓扑引导迁移完成跨站点任务；仿真中局部技能成功率65.0%，多站点任务42.0%，并在物理UAM上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型虽支持任务条件交互，但扩展到场景级空中操作面临整机演示成本高、延迟导致动作-状态错位、跨站点行为组合困难。

Method: 可重构场景管线合成任务条件、动力学可行轨迹与同步多视角观测，无需物理平台演示；MPAR将异步返回的动作块与实测执行进度对齐并实现为连续动力学可行轨迹；关系场景图将语言目标锚定到物体实例与可行交互区域，拓扑引导迁移连接不同站点的局部行为。

Result: 局部VLA技能在oracle目标与可行交接条件下仿真成功39/60（65.0%）；在增加500ms延迟且有无短暂命令更新停顿时，MPAR相较标称时间对齐将中位接管相位误差降低0.212s；完整系统完成21/50仿真多站点任务（42.0%），并在物理关节式UAM上进一步验证。

Conclusion: 该框架缓解了物理演示依赖、延迟错位和跨站点组合问题，为场景级空中操作提供了合成训练与可执行部署路径，并具备仿真到物理验证的可行性。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models enable task-conditioned interaction, but extending them to scene-scale aerial manipulation remains challenging due to costly whole-body demonstrations, latency-induced action-state misalignment, and cross-site behavior composition. We present a unified framework for synthetic policy training and scene-scale execution on articulated uncrewed aerial manipulators (UAMs). A scene-reconfigurable pipeline synthesizes task-conditioned, kinodynamically feasible trajectories and synchronized multiview observations for VLA training without physical-platform demonstrations. Measured-progress-aligned realization (MPAR) aligns asynchronously returned action chunks with measured execution progress and realizes them as continuous, dynamically feasible trajectories. A relational Scene Graph grounds language goals to object instances and feasible interaction regions, while topology-guided transfer connects local behaviors across sites. Local VLA skills achieve 39/60 successes (65.0%) in simulation under oracle target and feasible-handoff conditions. Under 500-ms added latency, with and without a transient command-update stall, MPAR reduces median takeover phase error by 0.212 s over nominal-time alignment. The complete system completes 21/50 simulated multi-site missions (42.0%) and is further validated on a physical articulated UAM.

</details>


### [47] [Making Waves: A Membrane-Coupled Delta Array for Manipulating Objects Below the Actuator Spacing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39652)
*Bailey Dacre,Andrés Faíña,Oliver Kroemer,Zeynep Temel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过将8×8三自由度Delta机器人末端执行器与可拉伸织物耦合，把64个离散接触点变为连续表面，突破执行器间距对可操纵物体尺寸的硬性下界；利用位移场操作原语和低阶DCT系数策略，在单一表面上实现15–90 mm物体的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式机械臂系统通过多执行器协同运动操作物体，但物体必须同时被多个执行器支撑，因此执行器中心距成为可操纵物体最小尺寸的硬性下界，限制了小物体操作。

Method: 构建8×8三自由度Delta机器人阵列，并将末端执行器与可拉伸织物耦合，形成连续操作表面；将阵列视为表面上的位移场，研究局部准静态场和循环场作为操作原语，可全局施加或围绕每个被跟踪物体局部施加以并行操作多个物体；训练作用于低阶离散余弦变换系数的策略，并在相同动作维度下使用19个Delta邻域进行控制。

Result: 在相同动作维度下，控制19个Delta邻域的策略将放置误差降低为控制整个阵列策略的一半；策略无需适配即可迁移到硬件，成功率为76%；平台在单一表面上可操作15 mm–90 mm的物体，尺寸范围达六倍，并跨越43.3 mm的执行器间距两侧。

Conclusion: 该方法消除了执行器间距对可操纵物体尺寸的下界，实现了连续表面上的跨尺度与并行多物体操作，并验证了低阶DCT策略的硬件可迁移性。

Abstract: Distributed manipulator systems manipulate objects through the coordinated motion of many actuators. However, an object must be supported by several actuators at once, so the centre-to-centre actuator spacing imposes a hard lower bound on manipulable object size. We remove this bound by coupling the end-effectors of an 8 x 8 array of three degrees-of-freedom delta robots with a stretchable fabric, turning 64 discrete contacts into a continuous surface capable of manipulating objects smaller than the actuator spacing. Viewing the array as a displacement field over that surface, we investigate local quasi-static and cyclic fields as manipulation primitives. These primitives can be applied globally across the array or locally confined around each tracked object to independently manipulate several objects in parallel. We then train a policy acting on low-order discrete cosine transform coefficients: at equal action dimension, commanding a nineteen-delta neighbourhood halves the placement error of commanding the whole array. The policy transfers to hardware without adaptation at 76% success. The platform manipulates objects from 15mm-90mm, a six-fold range spanning both sides of the actuator spacing, 43.3mm, on a single surface.

</details>


### [48] [Prediction is Better than Detection: Traffic Congestion Control using Drones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39637)
*Samira Hayat,Christian Raffelsberger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 研究无人机团队在闭环交通信号控制中执行拥堵检测与预测时，性能如何随车队规模扩展；发现性能在车队规模约等于监测路口数时饱和，且基于预测的调控收益约为基于检测的两倍，瓶颈转向预测精度而非感知覆盖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部署移动机器人团队进行持续监测时，需理解任务性能与车队规模的关系，以及当感知直接驱动下游行动而非仅观察时，该扩展规律是否仍成立。

Method: 构建多智能体仿真：车辆采用Nagel-Schreckenberg元胞自动机动力学，无人机以轮询策略巡逻路口，无人机报告驱动自适应交通信号控制器形成闭环；扫描车队规模、交通水平和网络规模，评估检测率、检测延迟和预测率。

Result: 性能随车队规模增长而趋于平台，平台点约在车队规模接近被监测路口数量时；提出持续监测部署的通用车队配置规则。在预测到拥堵时调整信号而非检测到后调整，可使拥堵时长减少量约翻倍。预测精度而非感知覆盖成为进一步改进的约束。

Conclusion: 在感知到行动管线中，机载预测的价值可能超过增加机器人数量；未来应聚焦机载推理与预测能力，而非单纯扩大车队规模。

Abstract: A central question in deploying teams of mobile robots for persistent monitoring is how task performance scales with fleet size, and whether this scaling holds once sensing drives downstream action rather than mere observation. We study this question for a team of drones performing traffic-jam detection and prediction in a simulated road network, whose reports drive an adaptive traffic-signal controller in closed loop. We build a multi-agent simulation, with vehicles following Nagel-Schreckenberg cellular-automaton dynamics and drones patrolling junctions via a round-robin policy, and sweep fleet size, traffic level, and network size to evaluate detection rate, detection delay, and prediction rate. We show how performance plateaus for fleet size approximating the number of junctions being monitored, and offer a general fleet-provisioning rule for persistent-monitoring deployments. More significantly, adapting the signal on a predicted jam, rather than a detected one, roughly doubles the resulting reduction in jam duration, showing that the value of onboard prediction in a sensing-to-action pipeline can exceed the value of adding more robots. Prediction accuracy, not sensing coverage, is now the binding constraint on further improvement, pointing to onboard inference, not fleet size, as the more promising direction for future work.

</details>


### [49] [TO-mdiSPAs: Topology Optimization of multi-directional Soft Pneumatic Actuators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39618)
*Swagatam Islam Sarkar,Prabhat Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于拓扑优化的多向软气动执行器设计方法，结合三场鲁棒性公式与Darcy定律建模气动载荷，通过min-max优化获得非常规几何，仿真实现多向运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软气动执行器在软体机器人中前景广阔，但多向/全向SPA需优化设计以在3D空间灵活弯曲运动；同时需保证制造鲁棒性并处理气动载荷的设计依赖性。

Method: 采用系统化拓扑优化方法；实施考虑blueprint、dilated、eroded的三场公式以保证制造鲁棒性；使用含排水项的Darcy定律模拟设计相关气动加载；基于SPA单元目标输出变形构建min-max优化问题，并用移动渐近线法求解。

Result: 优化得到高性能、非常规的几何设计；数值仿真表明优化后的SPA能够成功实现多样的多向运动。

Conclusion: 该拓扑优化方法可有效设计多向软气动执行器，实现多功能多向运动，为软体机器人SPA优化提供了系统途径。

Abstract: Soft pneumatic actuators (SPAs) are highly promising and have been extensively explored within the field of soft robotics. Under pneumatic pressure loads, an SPA undergoes bending deformation to perform specific tasks. A multi- or omni-directional SPA can bend and move in any direction within a 3D space, leveraging its multiple degrees of freedom to realize various mechanical functions. This work presents a systematic methodology using topology optimization (TO) to achieve an optimized design for a multi-directional SPAs. To ensure manufacturing robustness, a three-field TO formulation considering blueprint, dilated, and eroded designs is implemented. Additionally, Darcy's law, incorporating a drainage term, is used to model the design-dependent nature of the pneumatic loading. A min-max optimization problem is formulated based on the target output deformations of the SPA unit and solved using the Method of Moving Asymptotes. The optimization yields a high-performing, unconventional geometric design. Finally, numerical simulations demonstrate that the optimized SPA successfully achieves versatile multi-directional movements.

</details>


### [50] [Towards Agile Vision-Based Multi-UAV Flight: Revisiting State Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39611)
*Michal Pliska,Matouš Vrba,Ondřej Víta,Martin Jiroušek,Viktor Walter,Martin Saska*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作提出将视觉检测到的倾角/推力方向信息融入邻机状态估计，以消除仅用位置测量带来的高阶状态估计结构延迟，从而提升多无人机敏捷飞行中的速度、加速度估计与闭环跟踪性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 敏捷多无人机飞行需要机载低延迟、准确地估计邻机运动状态以支持避碰与协同。多数视觉方法仅依赖位置测量，再通过位移间接推断速度和加速度，这会引入固定的结构性延迟，限制可达敏捷性。

Method: 作者提出利用先进视觉检测器提供的倾角测量，获取共面多旋翼无人机的推力方向信息；基准测试了4种仅位置估计器和5种姿态感知估计器，其中包括一种新的线性推力约束卡尔曼滤波器（thrust-constraining KF）。在2个真实世界数据集和1个高保真照片级仿真数据集上，覆盖3–21 m/s²的不同敏捷度进行评测。

Result: 姿态感知估计在三个数据集上一致地将平均速度估计误差降低40%、加速度估计误差降低57%，所提出的KF公式优于其他估计器。仅位置滤波器在加速度阶跃响应中表现出约300 ms的恒定延迟，且与敏捷度无关；倾角约束估计器则通过先观测推力方向变化、后观测位移累积，达到受相机帧率限制的物理响应极限附近。在NMPC闭环领航-跟随仿真中，仅位置估计领航者状态无法使跟随者稳定悬停，而所提估计器可跟踪超过2g的横向机动。

Conclusion: 融合倾角/推力方向测量可显著缓解仅位置视觉估计的结构延迟，提升邻机高阶状态估计精度与闭环敏捷跟踪能力；所提线性推力约束KF在多种数据集和敏捷度下表现最佳。

Abstract: Agile multi-UAV flight requires accurate and low-latency onboard estimation of the kinematic states of neighboring UAVs for collision avoidance, motion coordination, etc. Most vision-based approaches rely on position-only measurements, inferring velocity and acceleration indirectly from displacement. We show that this introduces a fixed structural delay in the estimation of higher-order states, which limits the achievable agility. To address this, we propose to integrate tilt measurements, provided by a state-of-the-art visual detector, which inform about the thrust direction of co-planar multirotor UAVs. We benchmark four position-only and five pose-aware estimators, including a novel formulation of a linear thrust-constraining Kalman filter, on two real-world and one high-fidelity photorealistic simulated dataset over different levels of agility (3-21 m/s^2). In our setup, pose-aware estimation consistently reduces the average velocity and acceleration estimation errors by 40% and 57% across the three datasets with the proposed KF formulation outperforming the other estimators. Position-only filters exhibit a constant ~300 ms delay in acceleration step response independent of agility, whereas the tilt-constrained estimators operate near the physical response limit given by the camera frame-rate by observing the change in thrust direction before the displacement accumulates. In a closed-loop leader-follower simulated experiment with NMPC control, position-only estimation of the leader's state fails to facilitate stable hovering of the follower, while the proposed estimator enables tracking of lateral maneuvers exceeding 2g of acceleration.

</details>


### [51] [Text-to-3D Policy: Fine-Grained Language-Behavior Alignment for Unseen Specification Generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39599)
*Xinhao Yang,Wenhao Wu,Ning Lv,Yanshen Ding,Zhenhong Sun,Daoyi Dong,Chunlin Chen,Zhi Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出T3DP，一种面向细粒度语言-行为对齐的文本到3D策略框架，通过语言token与行为片段之间的双向token级对应，提升对训练中未见细粒度行为规范的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有文本到3D策略依赖预训练语言表示和全局行为-语言对齐，能捕捉粗粒度任务语义，但难以区分需要不同行为的相近规范，导致在未见目标位置、位移、关节状态等细粒度行为规范上泛化不足。

Method: T3DP不将指令和演示压缩为单一全局嵌入，而是保留局部结构，建立语言元素与行为片段之间的双向token级对应，将细微语言变化映射到受影响的行为组件，避免相近规范在表示空间坍塌；由此得到规范敏感的语言表示，用于条件化基于点云的3D扩散策略，且不修改底层策略架构。

Result: 在Meta-World、ManiSkill和RoboTwin上，T3DP相对全局语言-行为对齐在留出规范上的平均成功率提升+11.0至+14.2个百分点，15个任务族全部取得增益；真实机器人任务平均成功率从47.5%提升到65.0%（+17.5个百分点）。表示与动作探针分析表明，细粒度对齐更好保留规范几何和动作相关变化。

Conclusion: 细粒度语言-行为对齐能将局部行为grounding与下游控制联系起来，使文本到3D策略更精确地遵循未见细粒度行为规范，并提升跨仿真与真实机器人操作性能。

Abstract: 3D visuomotor policies provide a strong foundation for spatially precise manipulation, yet current text-to-3D policies struggle to follow unseen fine-grained behavioral specifications beyond those covered by demonstrations. We study this challenge as unseen specification generalization, where language specifies behaviorally significant variations, such as target position, displacement, or articulated state, that are absent from policy training. We find that pretrained language representations and conventional global behavior-language alignment capture coarse task semantics but often blur nearby specifications that require distinct behaviors. We introduce T3DP, a Text-to-3D Policy framework for fine-grained language-behavior alignment. Rather than compressing each instruction and demonstration into a single global embedding, T3DP preserves their local structures and establishes bidirectional token-level correspondence between linguistic elements and behavioral segments. This directly grounds subtle linguistic variations in the behavior components they affect, preventing closely related specifications from collapsing in the representation space. The resulting specification-sensitive language representation conditions a point-cloud-based 3D diffusion policy, enabling more precise control over unseen behavioral specifications without modifying the underlying policy architecture. Across Meta-World, ManiSkill, and RoboTwin, T3DP improves average held-out-specification success over global language-behavior alignment by +11.0-14.2 points, with gains on all 15 task families; on real-robot tasks, it further raises average success from 47.5% to 65.0% (+17.5 points). Representation and action-probe analyses show that fine-grained alignment better preserves specification geometry and action-relevant variation, linking local behavior grounding to downstream control.

</details>


### [52] [MVP-SLAM: Multi-Camera Visual-Inertial Floorplan-Prior SLAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39596)
*Asier Bikandi-Noya,Miguel Fernandez-Cortizas,Muhammad Shaheer,Holger Voos,Jose Luis Sanchez-Lopez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出MVP-SLAM：一种基于双鱼眼相机与IMU的在线视觉惯性SLAM系统，利用地图中墙体与楼层平面图匹配来在线抑制漂移并实现建筑内定位，在Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026中取得领先的在线定位与SLAM成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内施工现场光照变化大、结构重复且纹理稀少，长轨迹下视觉SLAM易漂移；但墙体等结构仍可区分，且设计阶段楼层平面图可提供度量参考。已有方法多在离线阶段用平面图校正轨迹，在线方法则常依赖深度传感器，因此需要仅用相机实现在线漂移校正与平面图内定位。

Method: MVP-SLAM采用两个背对鱼眼相机和IMU进行在线视觉惯性SLAM；从地图中检测墙体并与楼层平面图匹配，通过漂移感知策略选择匹配；再以多阶段集成将每个匹配对增量转化为持久校正，使轨迹在建图过程中持续保持校正并定位在平面图中。

Result: 在Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026的多楼层施工场景中验证：Localization任务22支队伍中排名第2，平均RMSE为0.29 m；SLAM任务62支队伍中排名第5，RMSE为0.24 m；在同时满足在线运行、集成楼层平面图并定位其中的队伍中，两项任务均排名第1。

Conclusion: 结果表明，仅依靠双鱼眼相机与IMU、结合楼层平面图墙体匹配，即可在挑战性施工环境中实现在线漂移校正与度量定位，并达到竞赛领先水平。

Abstract: Indoor building construction sites are demanding environments for visual SLAM, where variable lighting and repetitive, low-textured structures make the system drift over long trajectories, though structural elements such as walls remain distinguishable despite these conditions. These buildings are constructed according to their as-planned floor plans, available from the design phase, and although the actual as-built site can differ from this design, floor plans still provide a metric reference, both to localize the system in the building and to correct drift. Existing methods often use the floor plan to correct an already-built trajectory offline, and those that instead correct it online typically rely on depth sensors. We instead present MVP-SLAM, an online visual-inertial SLAM on two opposite-facing fisheye cameras that corrects drift from cameras alone by matching walls detected in its map to the floor plan, through a drift-aware policy. A multi-stage integration then turns each matched pair incrementally into a persistent correction, so the trajectory stays corrected and localized within the floor plan as it is built. MVP-SLAM was validated on the multi-floor construction sites of the Hilti-Trimble SLAM Challenge 2026, ranking 2nd of 22 teams in the Localization task (0.29 m mean RMSE) and 5th of 62 teams in the SLAM task (0.24 m), the top-ranked one in both tasks among those that operate online, integrate the floor plan, and localize within it.

</details>


### [53] [Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39594)
*Zihan Ye,Jiayi Liu,Puze Liu,Jiayun Li,Georgia Chalvatzaki,Jan Peters,Kristian Kersting*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NEUPRO，一种神经符号谓词学习方法，用于语义安全机器人控制：将人类可理解的安全规则作为透明符号约束，并通过可微推理器学习可复用、可迁移的安全特征表示。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人日益进入日常环境，安全成为核心挑战。现有方法常将安全需求编码为不透明的数学/逻辑形式或稠密代价函数，虽在特定任务有效，但难以解释、与任务紧耦合，且难以说明机器人动作为何安全或不安全。

Method: NEUPRO利用可微推理器，从人类指定的安全知识中学习可复用的安全表示。实践者可用透明符号规则表达任务相关安全要求，梯度可经规则传播至特征提取器，将原始观测映射为安全相关概念，使学习到的特征提取器（软性）扎根于人类可理解语义，支持透明约束评估，并可跨任务迁移。

Result: 收集并发布了REASON——首个用于可解释机器人安全规约的真实机器人基准数据集。在REASON上的实验表明，NEUPRO学习到的安全关键特征可跨任务泛化，缓解传统黑盒代价公式的可解释性局限，并能对安全违规给出显式解释。

Conclusion: NEUPRO将可解释性与可微性结合，超越不透明代价设计，迈向可复用的安全推理。

Abstract: As robots are increasingly deployed in everyday environments, ensuring their safety has become a central challenge. Existing methods often encode safety requirements as opaque mathematical/logical formulations or dense cost functions. While effective in specific tasks, they remain difficult to interpret, tightly coupled to individual tasks, and offer limited insight into why a robot action is considered safe or unsafe. To address this limitation, we propose ``Neuro-Symbolic Predicate Learning for Semantic Safe Robot Control'' (NEUPRO), which leverages a differentiable reasoner that can learn reusable safety representations from human-specified safety knowledge. NEUPRO allows practitioners to express task-related safety requirements as transparent symbolic rules, while enabling gradients to propagate through these rules to a feature extractor that maps raw observations to safety-relevant concepts. As a result, the learned feature extractor is (softly) grounded in human-understandable semantics, supports transparent constraint evaluation, and is transferable across tasks. By coupling interpretability with differentiability, NEUPRO moves beyond opaque cost design toward reusable safety reasoning. To evaluate NEUPRO's capability, we collect and release REASON, the first real robot benchmark dataset for interpretable robot safety specification. Experiments on REASON show that NEUPRO learns safety-critical features that generalize across tasks, mitigate the interpretability limitations of conventional black-box cost formulations, and provide explicit explanations of safety violation.

</details>


### [54] [ECHO-G: Embodied Co-speech Humanoid mOtion Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39575)
*Yizhao Li,Pusen Gao,Ming Wang,Shaojie Shen,Shuo Yang,Hao Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 ECHO-G，一个联合语音音频与时间戳文本条件的人形机器人全身 co-speech 运动生成框架，直接在机器人空间建模并完成数据集、基准与实机验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人全身 co-speech 运动需协调语音韵律、语言内容与具身特定运动；同时需验证直接机器人空间生成相较人类运动生成再重定向流程的优势。

Method: ECHO-G 使用 Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer（SGDiT），融合帧对齐声学特征与 token 级语言上下文并保持二者粒度差异；采用 rectified flow matching 训练，直接在机器人空间建模一对多 utterance-motion 关系。构建基于 BEAT2 的 audio-text-robot 数据集及覆盖 co-speech 特性、机器人运动质量与运行时效率的基准。

Result: 对比评估表明，直接机器人空间生成优于所评估的人类运动生成与重定向管线；模态消融显示音频-文本联合条件化带来收益；在物理人形机器人上完成部署，补充视频评分研究也偏好联合条件化。

Conclusion: ECHO-G 能有效生成人形机器人全身 co-speech 运动，验证了直接机器人空间生成与联合音频-文本条件化的优势，并公开数据集及训练、推理、评估代码。

Abstract: Generating full-body co-speech motion for humanoid robots requires coordinating speech prosody, linguistic content, and embodiment-specific motion. To this end, we present ECHO-G, a framework jointly conditioned on speech audio and timed transcripts. Its Speech-Grounded Diffusion Transformer (SGDiT) combines frame-aligned acoustic features with token-level linguistic context, preserving their distinct granularities. Trained with rectified flow matching, it models one-to-many utterance-motion relationships directly in robot space. To support training and evaluation, we introduce a BEAT2-derived audio-text-robot dataset and a benchmark covering co-speech characteristics, robot-motion quality, and runtime efficiency. Comparative evaluation supports direct robot-space generation over the evaluated human-motion generation and retargeting pipelines, while modality ablations highlight the benefits of joint audio-text conditioning. We further demonstrate deployment on a physical humanoid robot. A complementary video-rating study also favors joint conditioning over the alternatives. The dataset and training, inference, and evaluation code are available through our project page.

</details>


### [55] [Sparse Planner: A Hybrid Planner for Efficient Sampling via a Conditional Variational Autoencoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39570)
*Wenguang Xu,Giovanni Lucente,Karem Mohamed,Richard Membarth*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于CVAE的稀疏规划器SP，学习场景上下文与有效轨迹参数的条件关系，以仅1/8的采样密度生成高质量轨迹，降低轨迹代价并提升运行稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶轨迹规划需兼顾实时性与解质量，SBMP虽能通过采样逼近近优解，但高质量轨迹通常依赖密集采样，导致复杂交通场景下计算开销大、运行时间波动显著。

Method: 使用条件变分自编码器CVAE学习场景上下文与有效轨迹参数之间的条件关系，建模高质量采样分布的结构，直接在参数空间生成高性价比样本，从而降低采样密度并保持解质量。

Result: 相比SOTA FISS+规划器，SP轨迹代价更低且仅需其1/8采样密度；在障碍密集场景中距离保持能力提升，运行时间更短且更稳定。

Conclusion: SP通过CVAE引导稀疏采样，在保持或提升轨迹质量的同时显著降低计算量，并改善运行时可预测性，增强自动驾驶规划系统的效率与可靠性。

Abstract: Trajectory planning is a core component of autonomous driving systems, where real-time performance and solution quality directly affect safety and reliability. Sample-Based Motion Planning (SBMP) is widely adopted for its ability to approximate near-optimal solutions through parameter space sampling. However, achieving high-quality trajectories typically requires dense sampling, leading to substantial computational overhead and significant runtime variability in complex traffic scenarios. To address this limitation, we propose a Sparse Planner (SP) that improves sampling efficiency by learning the conditional relationship between scene context and effective trajectory parameters using a Conditional Variational Autoencoder (CVAE). By modeling the structure of high-quality sampling distributions, SP directly generates cost-effective samples in the parameter space, significantly reducing the required sampling density while preserving solution quality. Experimental results show that SP achieves lower trajectory cost than the state-of-the-art FISS+ planner while using only one-eighth of the sampling density. In addition, SP demonstrates improved distance-keeping capability in obstacle-rich scenarios and maintains reduced and more stable runtime characteristics, indicating enhanced computational efficiency and predictable runtime behavior.

</details>


### [56] [General Performance Guarantee for Human Torque Estimation-Based Task-Agnostic Assistive Exoskeleton Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39558)
*Duy Hoang,Bastien Berret,Olivier Bruneau,Laurent Fribourg*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对外骨骼扭矩估计误差导致辅助与人体意图不匹配的问题，提出一种可保证匹配辅助概率下界的交互扭矩设计框架，并在ABLE上肢外骨骼多任务实验中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确估计人体扭矩对实现任务无关的外骨骼控制至关重要；估计误差会造成机器人辅助与人体意图不匹配，降低可控性与任务表现。

Method: 形式化定义“匹配辅助”为机器人对人体运动有正向贡献；基于该定义构建理论框架，设计期望交互扭矩，使其在整个扭矩分布（包括训练任务之外未见数据）上保证匹配辅助概率下界。

Result: 在ABLE上肢外骨骼多任务设置中验证理论保证；实现跨任务有效泛化，保证运动平滑并降低人体体力消耗。

Conclusion: 该方法具有强可靠性与泛化性，适用于外骨骼控制，能提升辅助匹配性、任务表现和人机协同。

Abstract: Accurate human torque estimation is crucial for enabling task-agnostic control in robotic exoskeleton systems. However, estimation errors may cause mismatches between the robot assistance and the human intention, degrading controllability and task performance. In this paper, we address this issue by formally defining matched assistance as scenarios in which the robot positively contributes to human movement. Based on this definition, we develop a theoretical framework to design the robot's desired interaction torque that guarantees a lower bound on the matched assistance probability. Importantly, the proposed guarantee holds over the entire torque distribution, including unseen data beyond the training tasks. This provides our method with strong reliability and generalization, both of which are critical for effective exoskeleton control. The proposed strategy is implemented on the ABLE upper-limb exoskeleton and evaluated in a multi-task setup. Experimental results validate the theoretical guarantees and demonstrate that the proposed strategy achieves effective general performance across several tasks, guaranteeing movement smoothness while reducing human physical effort.

</details>


### [57] [Discrete Forcing: Infusing Discrete Guidance into Continuous Denoising for Few-Step Action Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39526)
*Jingbo Wang,Wenxuan Song,Wenhao Yu,Han Zhao,Xi Wang,Jiayi Chen,Donglin Wang,Yan Wang,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Discrete Forcing 流匹配框架，通过“先离散粗动作 token，再连续细化”的显式由粗到细生成过程，在视觉-语言-动作模型中兼顾动作结构性与精度，并提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型的高效动作生成需同时建模粗粒度动作结构与细粒度细节；离散动作 token 结构紧凑但精度不足，连续动作 token 精度高却常需多步去噪，二者难以兼得。

Method: Discrete Forcing 在流匹配框架中先预测离散动作 token 以建立粗动作结构，再以其引导连续动作细化；离散与连续分支共享同一 diffusion transformer 主干并设专门分支，参数量与常规单分支模型相当，且每个分支仅需一次前向。

Result: 在多个基准上优于参数量匹配的连续动作专家，推理更快，并随模型容量增加保持稳定增益；真实世界实验在高精度与动态操作任务上也取得提升。

Conclusion: Discrete Forcing 通过离散粗结构引导连续细化的统一架构，有效提升 VLA 动作生成的性能、精度与推理效率，并具备真实场景适用性。

Abstract: Efficient action generation in vision-language-action (VLA) models requires capturing both coarse action structure and fine-grained details. Discrete action tokens provide compact structural representations but sacrifice precision, while continuous action tokens offer high precision but often require multiple denoising steps. We introduce Discrete Forcing, a flow-matching framework that combines these representations through an explicit coarse-to-fine generation process. It first predicts discrete action tokens to establish a coarse action structure, then uses them to guide continuous action refinement. The discrete and continuous components share a common diffusion transformer backbone with specialized branches, maintaining a parameter count comparable to a conventional single-branch model while requiring only one forward pass per branch. Extensive evaluations across multiple benchmarks demonstrate improved performance and faster inference over a parameter-matched continuous action expert, with consistent performance gains as model capacity increases. Real-world experiments further demonstrate improvements on high-precision and dynamic manipulation tasks.

</details>


### [58] [LIBERO-Agent: Evaluating General-Purpose Agents for Direct Embodied Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39507)
*Zijie Diao,Yitong Chen,Sicheng Xie,Tianyi Lu,Wujian Peng,Guojin Zhong,Houze Xu,Ziyi Ye,Zuxuan Wu,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 LIBERO-Agent，一个用于评估通用智能体在机器人操作任务中表现的 agent-native 基准，揭示其在具身操作中存在显著可靠性差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用智能体已能规划、使用工具并根据反馈修正行为，但这些能力能否从数字环境迁移到具身操作仍不明确，因此需要面向智能体的交互式机器人操作评测基准。

Method: LIBERO-Agent 提供交互式机器人环境，智能体可自主选择观测、用自身工具处理观测并发出原生动作命令，而非提交任务级 Python 控制程序或调用高层机器人技能。它集成 200 个任务，并提供 30 个任务的主套件，分离感知、短时程执行和长时程组合能力。

Result: 结果显示明显可靠性差距：智能体在感知和简单短时程任务上表现良好，但在困难短时程和长时程任务上性能大幅下降。更丰富的观测可改善短时程操作，而示范带来的收益取决于智能体和格式。GPT-6 Astra 总体表现最强，其优势主要在机制交互，尤其是需要持续物理接触时；剩余失败主要来自跨阶段干扰和几何误差。

Conclusion: 该基准表明通用智能体尚不能可靠迁移到具身操作，尤其在困难短时程与长时程任务上存在明显瓶颈；未来需重点提升机制交互、跨阶段协调和几何精度。

Abstract: General-purpose agents can plan, use tools, and revise their behavior from feedback, but it remains unclear whether these capabilities transfer from digital environments to embodied manipulation. To investigate this question, we introduce LIBERO-Agent, an agent-native benchmark for evaluating these agents in robot manipulation tasks. Rather than asking agents to submit task-level Python control programs or operate through high-level robot skills, LIBERO-Agent provides an interactive robotic environment where agents can select which observations to inspect, process them with their own tools, and issue native action commands. LIBERO-Agent integrates 200 tasks into a common interaction framework and provides a 30-task primary suite that separates perception, short-horizon execution, and long-horizon composition. Results reveal a pronounced reliability gap: while agents perform well on perception and easy short-horizon tasks, their performance degrades substantially on hard short-horizon and long-horizon tasks. Richer observations improve short-horizon manipulation, while demonstration benefits depend on the agent and format. Among these agents, GPT-6 Astra achieves the strongest overall performance. Further analysis shows its major advantage lies in mechanism interaction, especially when sustained physical contact is needed, while its remaining failures stem from cross-stage interference and geometric errors.

</details>


### [59] [Communication-Free Distributed Multi-Robot Task Allocation under Partial Observations Using Labeled Multi-Bernoulli Filtering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39416)
*Takumi Ito,Akiya Kamimura*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅依赖局部观测、无需机器人间显式通信的分布式多机器人任务分配框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多机器人系统中，显式通信可能受限、不可靠或代价高，因此需要实现无需通信的去中心化协调与任务分配。

Method: 将任务定义为到达目标位置；每个机器人使用标签多伯努利（LMB）滤波器估计邻居机器人位置，并基于贪婪拍卖策略独立分配任务；在执行过程中持续更新状态估计并重新分配任务，从而实现无显式通信的分布式协调。

Result: 蒙特卡洛仿真表明，该方法无需机器人间通信即可实现有效的协同任务分配，并对测量杂波和观测不确定性具有鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 基于局部观测与LMB滤波的无通信任务分配框架能够支持去中心化多机器人协同，并在复杂观测条件下保持稳健性能。

Abstract: This paper proposes a communication-free multi-robot task allocation framework based solely on local observations. In this study, tasks are defined as reaching target locations. Each robot estimates the positions of neighboring robots using a Labeled Multi-Bernoulli (LMB) filter and independently assigns tasks through a greedy auction-based strategy. By continuously updating state estimates and reallocating tasks during execution, the proposed method enables decentralized coordination without explicit communication. Monte Carlo simulations demonstrate that the proposed method enables effective cooperative task allocation without inter-robot communication while remaining robust to measurement clutter and observation uncertainty.

</details>


### [60] [IronMind: Scaling Humanoid Dexterous Manipulation via Camera-Space Ego-Centric Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39403)
*Huimin Pan,Yufan Ren,Kunpeng Song,Siyang Wang,Xiwen Zhang,Xiaoyun Hu,Zhuoxu Duan,Hanrui Zheng,Jialeng Ni,Nathan Zhao,Sibo Ma,Zhenxuan Fan,Zhongyang Che,Danny Bao,Jiacheng Wei,Jerry Bai,Xiaoyu Yue,Xiaoyang Guo,Chenyi Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: IronMind 提出一种基于相机空间动作表示的 VLA 模型，利用大规模第一视角人类视频与异构机器人数据预训练人形机器人灵巧操作策略，并在真实机器人任务上验证了数据规模扩展的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角人类视频是可扩展的灵巧操作数据源，但用于训练人形机器人面临两个挑战：一是人类手部与机器人末端执行器存在具身差距，且低成本第一视角视频缺乏传统重定向所需的躯干运动学信息；二是数据质量异质，包括手部姿态跟踪噪声和文本标注弱对齐。

Method: IronMind 绕过显式身体重定向，采用第一视角视频原生参考系中的相机空间动作表示，并在语义上对齐机器人与人类动作维度；该视觉-语言-动作模型使用第一视角人类视频和异构机器人数据预训练人形机器人灵巧操作策略。

Result: 在 250 至 10,000 小时预训练预算下，验证损失随数据规模近似对数线性下降；10,000 小时模型在六个具有未见物体、可供性和推理提示的挑战性任务上达到 55.0% 成功率，而不超过 5,000 小时的各预算最高仅为 11.7%，无预训练为 5.0%；相同预算下相机空间动作表示也优于躯干坐标系基线。

Conclusion: 结果表明，基于相机空间动作表示、利用大规模第一视角人类数据预训练，可作为人形机器人操作的可扩展基础。

Abstract: Egocentric human video offers a scalable data source for dexterous manipulation, yet using it to train humanoid robots presents two challenges: (1) an embodiment gap, as human hands differ structurally from robot end-effectors and low-cost egocentric recordings lack the torso kinematics required by conventional retargeting; and (2) heterogeneous data quality, including noisy hand-pose tracking and weakly aligned text annotations. We introduce IronMind, a vision-language-action (VLA) model that uses egocentric human video and heterogeneous robot data to pretrain policies for humanoid dexterous manipulation. To bridge the embodiment gap, IronMind bypasses explicit body-retargeting by using a camera-space action representation, the native reference space of egocentric video, and semantically aligning robot and human action dimensions. Across total pretraining budgets from 250 to 10,000 hours, validation loss decreases approximately log-linearly with data scale. Larger pretraining budgets also improve out-of-distribution real-robot manipulation after post-training: across six challenging tasks with unseen objects, affordances, and reasoning prompts, the 10,000-hour model achieves a 55.0% success rate, compared with at most 11.7% for every pretraining budget up to 5,000 hours and 5.0% without pretraining. At the same pretraining budget, the camera-space action representation also outperforms the torso-frame baseline. Together, these findings support pretraining with a camera-space action representation on large-scale human egocentric data as a scalable foundation for humanoid robot manipulation.

</details>


### [61] [UniWAM Technical Report: Unified Mobile Manipulation via Mixed-Stream World-Action Modeling and Manipulation Anchor Pose Supervision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39388)
*Wei Xue,Keliang Liu,Mingzhang Cui,Jinhua Xie,Jinjie Wei,Jianan Hou,Jingcheng Lu,Lintao Wang,Kaixiang Qiu,Yizhou Liu,Xinghai Ye,Jinghang Han,Mingcheng Li,Jie Gu,Shunli Wang,Lihua Zhang,Dingkang Yang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: UniWAM 是一种统一的混合流世界-动作模型，通过共享骨干网络和分离的动作编码器/输出头，同时学习导航与操作；并利用自动构建的 MAP-Data 提供 Manipulation Anchor Pose 监督，在导航到可操作位姿及移动操作任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动操作需要先精确导航到适合操作的位姿，再进行可靠物体交互。但导航与操作在动作空间和视觉需求上不同，难以用统一策略学习；同时，带显式“可操作位姿”监督的真实世界导航数据采集成本高、难以规模化。

Method: 提出 UniWAM：统一混合流世界-动作模型，导航与操作分别使用动作编码器和输出头，但共享公共骨干，从而支持在独立采样的导航与操作数据上进行联合表示学习，并支持单流独立推理与双流批并行推理。进一步提出 Manipulation Anchor Pose（MAP）监督，用于学习“在哪里停、如何朝向”以进行操作。通过自动化流水线从大规模 3D 场景构建 MAP-Data，包含超过 150 万 episode、7500 小时数据，提供逐帧目标物体边界框和图像平面 MAP 坐标作为导航辅助监督；结合投影后的末端执行器轨迹作为操作监督，从第一视角观测中形成流特定的图像平面监督。

Result: 在大规模 MAP-Data 上，UniWAM 在 MAP-Bench 上位置误差较最强外部基线降低 30.1%，朝向误差降低 44.0%。在 24 个真实机器人任务中，UniWAM 在 MAP 导航和移动操作方面取得领先结果，操作性能也具有竞争力。

Conclusion: UniWAM 通过统一混合流架构与 MAP 监督，有效缓解了移动操作中导航与操作统一学习及可操作位姿数据稀缺的问题，并展示了大规模自动构建数据对策略学习的价值；代码、数据和 benchmark 已开源。

Abstract: Mobile manipulation requires precise navigation to a manipulation-ready pose followed by reliable object interaction. These two stages differ in action spaces and visual requirements, which complicates unified policy learning. In addition, collecting diverse real-world navigation data with explicit manipulation-ready pose supervision remains costly and difficult to scale. We introduce UniWAM, a unified mixed-stream world-action model with separate action encoders and output heads for navigation and manipulation, sharing a common backbone. This design supports joint representation learning on independently sampled navigation and manipulation data. UniWAM supports independent inference for either stream and batch-parallel inference for both. We further introduce Manipulation Anchor Pose (MAP) supervision for where to stop and how to orient for manipulation. An automated pipeline constructs MAP-Data from large-scale 3D scenes, yielding over 1.5 million episodes and 7,500 hours. MAP-Data provides per-frame target-object bounding boxes and image-plane MAP coordinates as auxiliary navigation supervision. Together with projected end-effector trajectories for manipulation, these prediction targets provide stream-specific image-plane supervision for action learning from egocentric observations. With large-scale MAP-Data, UniWAM outperforms the strongest external baselines on our MAP-Bench by 30.1\% in position error and 44.0\% in heading error. Across 24 real-robot tasks, UniWAM achieves leading results in MAP navigation and mobile manipulation, with competitive manipulation performance. We have released code, data, and benchmark.

</details>


### [62] [RoboAssist: Interactive Human-Humanoid Planning for Long-Horizon Surgical Assistance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39384)
*Jingwei Jia,Keyu Zhou,Jiewei Wang,Peisen Xu,Xingyuan Zhou,Liang Wang,Jiming Chen,Gaofeng Li,Jin Wang,Shunlei Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 RoboAssist：面向长时程手术辅助的人-人形机器人交互规划智能体框架，统一工作流推理、任务协调与跨层安全，并在 Unitree G1 上完成多阶段仿真验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程手术辅助要求人形机器人与动态人类活动协同，同时保证规划与执行全过程安全；现有方法难以兼顾部分可观测人类流程、可执行机器人任务序列以及跨层安全约束。

Method: 核心是非对称双轨表示，将部分可观测的人类流程状态与可执行机器人任务序列解耦；在线更新人类流程估计、场景上下文与任务依赖，并在工作流请求变化时重验证剩余任务序列，仅重规划受影响后缀。跨层安全架构集成预防性导航调节、近距离交接反应式调节与独立全身运行时监督，实现任务协调与安全约束的执行期耦合。

Result: 在 Unitree G1 人形机器人上，面向长时程、多阶段模拟手术辅助场景（多模态交互、器械操作、医疗物资运输、导航与安全人-机器人交接）完成多阶段任务并适应工作流请求变化。针对全量重规划消融显示，残差重规划降低规划更新延迟与更新后 token 消耗；独立安全实验表明导航、交接与运行时监督提供互补保护。

Conclusion: RoboAssist 通过非对称双轨表示与跨层安全架构，实现了可适应工作流变化的长时程人-人形机器人手术辅助规划，并以残差重规划提升效率、以多层机制保障安全。

Abstract: Long-horizon surgical assistance requires humanoid robots to coordinate with evolving human activities while maintaining safety across planning and execution. We present RoboAssist, an agent-based framework for interactive human-humanoid planning that integrates workflow reasoning, task coordination, and cross-layer safety. At its core is an asymmetric dual-track representation that separates partially observed human process states from executable robot task sequences. By updating human-process estimates, scene context, and task dependencies online, RoboAssist revalidates the remaining task sequence and replans only the affected suffix when workflow requests change. A cross-layer safety architecture combines preventive navigation regulation, reactive regulation during close-range handover, and independent whole-body runtime supervision. This design couples online task coordination with safety constraints throughout execution. We demonstrate the framework on a Unitree G1 humanoid robot in long-horizon, multi-stage simulated surgical assistance scenarios encompassing multimodal interaction, instrument handling, medical material transport, navigation, and safe human-robot handover. Experiments show multi-stage task completion and adaptation to workflow-request changes. A targeted full-replanning ablation shows that residual replanning reduces plan-update latency and post-update token usage. Separate safety experiments demonstrate complementary protection across navigation, handover, and runtime supervision. Additional results and demonstrations are available online at this https URL .

</details>


### [63] [Beyond the Current Scene: Event-Referential Grasping with Active View Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39375)
*Hyunjoon Lee,Haebeom Jung,Eunsung Cha,Daeun Lee,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Jaesung Choe,Jaesik Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BeyondSCe，一个面向事件指代式抓取的零样本机器人抓取系统：利用历史事件与当前场景识别并定位目标；目标被遮挡时，结合事件先验与场景几何选择视角以揭示目标，在真实机器人实验中显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人观察人-物交互后，应能响应以过去事件中的角色而非名称或外观来指代抓取目标的后续请求；且请求发生时目标可能已不可见或被遮挡，因此需要事件指代与主动感知能力。

Method: BeyondSCe 是零样本系统，无需额外任务特定训练，仅使用预训练模型。输入为事件历史与当前场景，系统识别请求的对象或部件并定位以用于抓取；若目标被遮挡，则从历史中恢复事件先验，并结合当前场景几何选择可能揭示目标的相机视角。实验使用单个腕部安装的 RGB-D 相机。

Result: 在真实机器人实验中，目标初始可见与初始遮挡条件下抓取成功率分别为 76% 和 77%，而各条件下最强基线分别为 40% 和 55%。在四个额外重遮挡场景中，相比给定目标真实 3D 边界框的主动感知基线，成功率从 75% 提升至 95%，平均视角数从 3.35 降至 2.20。

Conclusion: BeyondSCe 能在零样本条件下完成事件指代式抓取，并通过事件先验与主动视角选择有效应对遮挡，在成功率和感知效率上均优于强基线。

Abstract: A robot that observes people interacting with objects should be able to carry out later requests that refer back to those interactions. Such requests may specify a grasp target by the role it played in a past event rather than by its name or appearance. Moreover, the target may no longer be visible when the robot is asked to act. We present BeyondSCe, a zero-shot robotic grasping system for this event-referential setting. Given the event history and the current scene, the system identifies the requested object or part and localizes it for grasping. If the target is occluded, it combines an event prior recovered from the history with current scene geometry to select camera viewpoints likely to reveal the target. The system uses pretrained models without additional task-specific training. In real-robot experiments with a single wrist-mounted RGB-D camera, it achieves grasp success rates of 76% and 77% for initially visible and occluded targets, respectively, compared with 40% and 55% for the strongest baseline in each condition. On four additional scenes with heavy occlusion, it increases grasp success rates from 75% to 95% while reducing the mean number of views from 3.35 to 2.20, compared with an active-perception baseline given the target's ground-truth 3D bounding box.

</details>


### [64] [MotionWeave: Learning Motion-Centered Future Dynamics for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39324)
*Jingqiu Wang,Yan Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出以运动为中心的未来动态框架 MotionWeave，用于动作块预测，在六个 MetaWorld 任务上达到 75.3% 平均成功率，较 π0 绝对提升 8.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 模型引入世界模型提供动态监督，但显式预测未来图像/视频会包含与控制无关的外观；基于整体未来视觉表征与共享全局动作特征的引导，难以建立时间步特定的动作与局部视觉变化对应关系。

Method: MotionWeave 包含两个模块：动作诱导运动定位器 AIMG 以动作和本体感受表示为条件，构造时间视野特定查询，从当前视觉 token 中定位每个未来动作时间步对应的交互区域；时间视野残差合成器 HRC 提取相邻时间视野交互表征的差异，编码为时序运动线索，并通过门控残差注入动作 token。训练时使用未来帧渲染的机械臂掩码构建 KL 运动定位监督；推理时仅使用当前观测。

Result: 在六个 MetaWorld 任务上，MotionWeave 平均成功率为 75.3%，相比 π0 的 66.7% 绝对提升 8.6%，在持续交互任务上尤其有效。

Conclusion: 以运动为中心、时间步特定运动定位与残差运动注入可有效提升动作块预测，无需未来观测即可改善策略性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have recently incorporated world models to provide richer dynamic supervision beyond sparse action labels. However, explicitly predicting future images or videos may include control-irrelevant appearance, while guidance derived from holistic future visual representations and shared global action features may fail to establish timestep-specific correspondence between actions and local visual changes. To address this issue, we propose MotionWeave, a motion-centric future-dynamics framework for action-chunk prediction with two modules: the Action-Induced Motion Grounder (AIMG) and the Horizon Residual Composer (HRC). Specifically, AIMG conditions on action and proprioceptive representations to construct horizon-specific queries that localize interaction regions associated with each future action timestep from current visual tokens. HRC extracts differences between interaction representations at adjacent horizons, encodes them as temporal motion cues, and injects them into action tokens through a gated residual. During training, robot-arm masks rendered from future frames are used to construct KL-based motion-grounding supervision, while inference uses only the current observation. On six MetaWorld tasks, MotionWeave achieves a 75.3% average success rate, an absolute gain of 8.6% over {\pi}0 (66.7%), especially on sustained-interaction tasks. Our code is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [65] [HiWE: Hierarchical World Knowledge Model with Visual Keypoint Enhancement for Zero-Shot 3D Path Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39323)
*Guoqing Ma,Mingqi Yuan,Chen Gao,Jiayu Chen,Shan Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HiWE 通过基于点的视觉定位与语言规划接口，将交互位置识别、可行运动规划与接触执行串联起来，用于机器人演示生成；PointVLM 将任务相关物体关联到图像坐标，深度提升为语义 3D 表示，3DLLM 规划末端执行器路点与夹爪指令，混合抓取模块求解局部抓取姿态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人演示生成需要系统判断交互应发生在何处、规划可行运动并执行所需接触，因此需要统一视觉定位与语言规划中的关键决策。

Method: 提出基于点的框架 HiWE：PointVLM 经指令微调，混合点标注、分割衍生样本、机器人观测和 VQA 数据，将任务相关物体关联到图像坐标；深度测量将预测提升为语义 3D 表示；语言规划器 3DLLM 基于该表示指定末端执行器路点和夹爪命令；混合抓取模块解析局部抓取姿态。

Result: 在 14 个仿真操作任务和 4 个实体机器人任务上评估，并对视觉训练数据、空间输入和抓取选择进行消融。零样本执行指不进行任务特定演示训练的部署；视觉模型微调时使用现有机器人数据。

Conclusion: 本文描述了该框架最初的基于点表述；其与后续 GeneralVLA 扩展的关系在引言中详述。

Abstract: Robot demonstration generation requires a system to identify where an interaction should occur, plan a feasible motion, and execute the required contact. HiWE connects these decisions through a point-based interface between visual grounding and language-based planning. PointVLM is instruction-tuned to associate task-relevant objects with image coordinates using a mixture of point annotations, segmentation-derived samples, robot observations, and visual question answering data. Depth measurements lift these predictions into a semantic 3D representation. A language planner, 3DLLM, uses this representation to specify end-effector waypoints and gripper commands, while a hybrid grasping module resolves local grasp poses. The evaluation covers 14 simulated manipulation tasks and four physical-robot tasks, together with ablations of the visual training data, spatial inputs, and grasp selection. Here, zero-shot execution refers to deployment without task-specific demonstration training; the visual model uses existing robot data during fine-tuning. This paper describes the original point-based formulation of the framework; its relationship to the subsequent GeneralVLA extension is detailed in the introduction.

</details>


### [66] [A Biophysically Detailed C. elegans Circuit as a Task-Agnostic Dynamical Core for Visually Robust Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39322)
*Linrui Qian,Jiajia Zhang,Gan He,Bohan Sun,Zhiwei Lin,Qianhao Wang,Zewu Cai,Nianyu Yi,Mengdi Zhao,Kai Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将生物物理详尽的秀丽隐杆线虫感觉运动回路作为视觉运动策略的动态核心，仅训练轻量任务适配器，在固定突触权重下实现跨任务与抗视觉扰动的机器人控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略通常局限于单一任务、单一身体和单一视觉环境，泛化能力差；尚不清楚神经系统能否通过演化形成的连接和生物物理特性提供可泛化的感觉运动计算。

Method: 嵌入包含136个多房室神经元、具有真实形态和电生理特性的线虫感觉运动回路作为策略动态核心；固定核心突触权重，仅训练薄的任务特定适配器，膜电压自由演化；在MetaWorld多任务及真实机械臂上评估，并与扩散策略、动作分块Transformer、神经回路策略及MLP/LSTM/Transformer/储备池等替换核心模型对比。

Result: 在多个MetaWorld任务中，该核心匹配或超越扩散策略、动作分块Transformer和神经回路策略基线，且视觉扰动下退化更小；替换为MLP、LSTM、Transformer或储备池网络后优势消失；在真实机械臂上，该核心能承受使基线崩溃的多种视觉扰动。

Conclusion: 视觉鲁棒性可继承自生物物理详尽的回路动力学，而非仅由任务特定控制器学习得到。

Abstract: Robot policies are usually trained for one task, one body and one visual environment, and generalize poorly beyond these conditions. Whether a nervous system can instead supply the sensorimotor computation through its evolved wiring and biophysics remains unresolved. Here we embed a biophysically detailed Caenorhabditis elegans sensorimotor circuit - 136 multicompartment neurons with realistic morphologies and electrophysiological characteristics - as the dynamical core of a visuomotor policy. Only thin task-specific adapters are trained; the core's synaptic weights stay fixed while its membrane voltages evolve freely. Across different MetaWorld tasks the core matches or exceeds diffusion-policy, action-chunking-transformer and neural-circuit-policy baselines, and degrades less under visual perturbations. Replacing the core with generic network models such as MLP, LSTM, transformer or reservoir networks removes the advantage. Furthermore, on a real robotic arm the core withstands diverse visual perturbations that collapse the baselines. Our results suggest that visual robustness can be inherited from biophysically detailed circuit dynamics rather than learned by task-specific controllers.

</details>


### [67] [FORTE: Forecasting Occupancy for Spatiotemporal Risk-Aware Planning in Dynamic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39305)
*Hahjin Lee,Young J. Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: FORTE 是一种面向动态环境的导航框架，利用预测占据栅格地图的时空演化进行规划；其潜扩散预测器可非自回归生成完整预测时域并保持时序一致性，预测与导航性能均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态环境中的安全导航需要前瞻未来环境状态，以评估碰撞发生的时空风险。现有基于占据栅格地图（OGM）的导航方法存在预测精度与效率不足，且规划阶段未充分利用预测 OGM 中时序信息的问题。

Method: 提出 FORTE 导航框架，直接从时空占据重叠与占据方向性出发利用预测占据的时空演化；据此评估多条拓扑不同的路径并选择合适路径，无需显式目标检测或跟踪。为支持在线规划，提出基于潜扩散模型的 OGM 预测器，以非自回归方式生成整个预测时域，并通过 temporal shift 模块保持时序一致性。

Result: 大量评估表明 FORTE 优于最先进基线：预测方面 IoU 最高提升 215.3%，推理速度最高提升 5.24 倍；导航方面成功率最高提升 3.5 倍。

Conclusion: FORTE 通过直接利用预测 OGM 的时空演化并结合高效非自回归潜扩散预测，实现了更准确、高效的动态环境安全导航。

Abstract: Safe navigation in dynamic environments requires anticipating future environmental states to account for spatiotemporal risks, specifically when and where collisions may occur. To this end, occupancy grid map (OGM) prediction has been widely adopted as an effective approach. However, existing OGM-based navigation methods often struggle to achieve accurate and efficient forecasting and fail to fully exploit the temporal information in predicted OGMs during planning. To address these challenges, we propose FORTE, a navigation framework that directly exploits the spatiotemporal evolution of predicted occupancy from the perspectives of spatiotemporal occupancy overlap and occupancy directivity. Based on these properties, FORTE evaluates multiple topology-distinct paths and selects the suitable one without explicit object detection or tracking. To support online planning, we formulate a latent diffusion model-based OGM predictor that generates the entire forecast horizon in a non-autoregressive manner while maintaining temporal consistency through temporal shift modules. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that FORTE outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. For prediction, FORTE achieves up to 215.3% higher IoU and 5.24x faster inference; for navigation, it yields up to a 3.5x higher success rate.

</details>


### [68] [Scale and Selection: What Makes Automatic Harness Evolution Work for Visual-Interface Robot Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39304)
*Zhijie Wei,Ferris Tan,Jinghui Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 将现成编码智能体直接用作机器人策略，通过截图观察浏览器 3D 界面并用少量工具摆虚拟目标夹爪时，其 harness（提示、工具、控制规则）决定成败；本文用另一个编码智能体（优化器）自动改进该 harness，并发现两个关键因素：每轮 rollout 数量和 Champion-Challenger 选择机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动编写 harness 是瓶颈；需验证自动演化 harness 对视觉界面机器人智能体是否可行，以及哪些因素决定其可靠增益。

Method: 以编码智能体作为优化器，自动修改作为机器人策略的编码智能体的 harness；通过多轮提出并评估修订。实验比较每轮不同 rollout 数（5 到 100）以及是否无条件接受所有修订 vs Champion-Challenger：仅当修订在固定评估集上严格优于当前版本才晋升。

Result: 固定轮数下，训练 rollout 从 5 增至 100，held-out 成功率从 47% 升至 67%；小训练集过拟合，训练 70% 但 held-out 仅 54%。无条件接受所有修订时，性能在 10 轮内下降；加入 Champion-Challenger 后，30 轮内 held-out 成功率从 51% 升至 67%。

Conclusion: 视觉界面机器人智能体的 harness 自动演化可行，但增益取决于每次决策背后的 rollout 规模，以及优化器修订的选择/晋升机制。

Abstract: When an off-the-shelf coding agent is used directly as a robot policy, observing a browser-based 3D interface through screenshots and acting by posing a virtual target gripper through a few tools, the agent's harness, its prompts, tools, and control rules, largely determines success, and until now it has been written by hand. We show that this harness can be improved automatically by another coding agent, the optimizer agent, and report two findings about what makes it work. First, the number of rollouts the optimizer agent sees per round governs whether the evolved harness is trustworthy, generalizes, and improves steadily. A single rollout is a noisy binary outcome, so with few rollouts per round a revision can be promoted on luck; enlarging the batch raises the signal-to-noise ratio of every promotion decision. Holding rounds fixed and growing the training set from 5 to 100 rollouts, held-out success rises from 47% to 67%, while small training sets overfit, reaching 70% on training tasks but only 54% held-out. Second, the optimizer agent must not be given free rein. With every revision it proposes accepted unconditionally, performance drifts downward within ten rounds as ill-judged edits accumulate; adding the most basic safeguard, Champion-Challenger selection that promotes a revision only if it strictly beats the incumbent on the same fixed evaluation set, turns the same loop into one that raises held-out success from 51% to 67% over 30 rounds. Automatic harness evolution for visual-interface robot agents is thus feasible, but its gains hinge on the rollout scale behind each decision and on how the optimizer agent's revisions are selected.

</details>


### [69] [ReWAM: Reciprocal World Action Models for Interactive Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39245)
*Benshan Ma,Pei Liu,Ruiguo Zhong,Lang Zhang,Mingyue Feng,Yaonong Wang,Jun Ma*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出互惠世界行动模型（ReWAM），通过博弈论框架和Level-k响应层级建模自车与其他智能体的相互影响，在NAVSIM数据集上取得最先进性能，交互场景提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
