Motivation: 现有世界行动模型（WAMs）将其他智能体视为世界模型的组成部分，而非能根本影响自车动作的决策者，导致密集交互场景性能受损；交互场景要求自车动作受其他智能体行为影响。

Method: 引入ReWAM，一种博弈论世界行动建模框架，将自车与其他智能体表示为相互影响的条件响应者。采用Level-k响应层级实例化，角色特定的自车与其他动作DiT通过跨智能体注意力交换紧凑策略令牌，并基于共享的未来驾驶世界表示。将专家动作视为最佳响应分布的样本，使用条件流匹配联合优化整个层级，以从演示中学习自车响应策略。

Result: 在NAVSIM数据集上评估，相比基线达到最先进性能，交互场景中提升尤为显著，验证了建模互惠响应为交互感知世界行动生成提供了更有效的基础。

Conclusion: 建模互惠响应是交互感知世界行动生成的有效基础，ReWAM框架在交互场景中表现优越。

Abstract: In interactive scenarios, an autonomous driving system is required to generate ego actions under the influence of other agents' behaviors. Existing World Action Models (WAMs) typically model other agents as components of the world model rather than as decision-makers that fundamentally shape the action of the ego agent, which impairs their performance in dense interaction scenarios. We introduce Reciprocal World Action Models (ReWAM), a game-theoretic world action modeling framework that captures the reciprocal influence between the ego agent and other agents by representing them as conditional responders whose actions are mutually influenced. We instantiate this framework with a Level-$k$ response hierarchy, where role-specific ego and other action DiTs exchange compact strategy tokens through cross-agent attention while remaining grounded in a shared representation of the future driving world. To learn the response policy of the ego agent from demonstrations, we formulate expert actions as samples from the best response distribution and jointly optimize the entire hierarchy using conditional flow matching. Our framework is evaluated on the NAVSIM dataset and achieves state-of-the-art performance compared to baselines. The improvement is particularly significant in interactive scenarios, validating that modeling reciprocal responses provides a more effective foundation for interaction-aware world action generation.

</details>


### [70] [The Planning Limits of Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39235)
*Ali Alrasheed,Basim Azam,Naveed Akhtar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 世界模型仅在目标位于其想象轨迹内或稍远处时可可靠指导动作选择；其可规划范围有限，需更长想象轨迹或更近子目标，且在范围内可提升VLA策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多展示世界模型能完成什么，却未明确其预测何时对规划有用、何处会失败。作者用冻结自监督视觉骨干测试可跨环境迁移的表征，以探究动作条件世界模型在规划中的可靠范围与失效边界。

Method: 在五个冻结自监督视觉骨干（V-JEPA 2、V-JEPA 2.1、VideoMAEv2、VideoPrism、DINOv2）上构建动作条件预测器，并在Meta-World操作任务和BridgeData V2真实机器人交互中评估。比较预测器规模、rollout训练长度与编码器影响；用真实模拟器检验完美预测下的极限；比较纯想象、MPC反馈规划、想象至目标、专家子目标等策略；并测试世界模型对VLA动作选择的改进。

Result: 五步rollout（训练长度）下，世界模型仅对前方5至10个控制步的目标可靠排序动作，而任务目标距16至53步。81倍更大预测器或更长rollout训练均未扩展该范围；编码器同时影响可靠范围与闭环成功率，V-JEPA 2.1表现最一致。完美预测下，目标从5步增至20步，成功率从92%降至41%。远距离目标中，纯想象成功23%，MPC提升至30%，想象至目标47%，附近专家子目标76%。在可规划范围内，从VLA提出的八个动作中选择可将16个任务成功率从65%提升至77%。

Conclusion: 世界模型的有效规划受限于其想象轨迹的时域范围；对远距离目标，需采用更长想象轨迹或更近子目标。若在可规划范围内使用，世界模型可作为动作筛选器改善VLA策略性能。

Abstract: World models offer a promising way to help robots understand how the physical world evolves and plan complex behaviours through imagination. Yet existing studies mainly demonstrate what these models can accomplish, leaving unclear when their predictions remain useful for planning and where they fail. We study this question using action-conditioned predictors built on five frozen self-supervised visual backbones: V-JEPA 2, V-JEPA 2.1, VideoMAEv2, VideoPrism, and DINOv2. We use frozen backbones to test representations intended to transfer across environments. We evaluate these models on diverse Meta-World manipulation tasks and real-robot interactions from BridgeData V2. We find that a world model guides action selection reliably only when the goal lies within, or slightly beyond, the trajectory it imagines during planning. With five-step rollouts, the length the predictor was trained on, the world model ranks actions reliably only for targets five to ten control steps ahead, whereas task goals lie 16 to 53 steps away. Neither an 81-fold larger predictor nor longer-rollout training extends this range; the encoder affects both range and closed-loop success, with V-JEPA 2.1 performing most consistently. More fundamentally, the limit persists under perfect prediction: using the real simulator, success falls from 92% to 41% as the target moves from five to twenty steps ahead of a five-step rollout. Planning therefore requires either longer imagined trajectories or closer subgoals. For distant goals, pure imagination succeeds in 23% of episodes, planning with feedback (MPC) raises success to 30%, imagining as far as the goal to 47%, and nearby expert subgoals to 76%. Used within its plannable range, a world model can also improve a vision-language-action (VLA) policy: choosing among eight actions the VLA proposes raises its success from 65% to 77% across 16 different tasks.

</details>


### [71] [ASENA: Self-evolving Agents for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39207)
*An-Chieh Cheng,Isabella Liu,Edmund Bu,Johan Bjorck,Hongxu Yin,Zhengyi Luo,Jan Kautz,Linxi "Jim" Fan,Yuke Zhu,Sifei Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ASENA 将通用编码智能体接入机器人感知、计算、监督执行与持久经验，并引入 4B 单目导航策略 ASENA-VLN，实现可编程、可修复、可积累经验的具身智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人系统通常局限于预定义技能或路线跟随，缺乏让通用编码智能体直接编写程序、检查结果、修复失败并复用可执行技能的能力；同时需要将学习型导航作为可选工具集成到此类可编程系统中。

Method: 提出 ASENA 具身智能体系统，连接编码智能体与机器人传感、计算、监督执行和持久经验，支持写程序、执行、检查记录、修复失败、复用笔记与技能，且不更新模型权重。进一步提出 ASENA-VLN：4B 单目导航策略，作为系统内可选工具，用共享视觉语言解码器预测机体坐标系轨迹，支持长路线与短时行为；训练数据包括路线指令、视觉问答及新构建的几何推导原子导航任务。

Result: ASENA-VLN 独立策略在 R2R 上成功率达 68.7%，RxR 上 70.2%，均为 SOTA。与编码智能体集成后，在两个智能体基准上成功率提升 11 个百分点并减少执行时间。通过持久工作区演化与模拟器反馈，在重复 100 任务子集上迭代十轮，R2R 成功率从 72% 提升至 98%，RxR 从 65% 提升至 89%。在具身问答上取得 SOTA 准确率且交互步数更少。Unitree G1 真实世界演示无需预建地图，结合搜索、视觉检查、空间推理与合成手势。

Conclusion: 在线编程与持久经验演化使机器人行为超越路线跟随和预定义技能，ASENA 展示了可编程具身智能体在仿真与真实世界中的有效性和可扩展性。

Abstract: We present ASENA, an embodied agent system that connects general-purpose coding agents to robot sensing, computation, supervised execution, and persistent experience. Agents can write and execute programs, inspect recorded outcomes, repair failures, and reuse notes and executable skills while keeping their model weights fixed. We further introduce ASENA-VLN, a 4B monocular navigation policy that serves as an optional tool within this programmable system. ASENA-VLN predicts body-frame trajectories for both extended routes and short-horizon behaviors using a shared vision-language decoder trained on route instructions, visual question answering, and a newly curated dataset of geometry-derived atomic navigation tasks. As a standalone policy, ASENA-VLN achieves state-of-the-art success rates of 68.7% on R2R and 70.2% on RxR. When integrated with a coding agent, learned navigation improves ASENA's success rate by 11 percentage points on both agentic benchmarks while reducing execution time. Through persistent workspace evolution and simulator feedback, ten passes over recurring 100-task subsets further improve success from 72% to 98% on R2R and from 65% to 89% on RxR. On embodied question answering, ASENA achieves state-of-the-art accuracy with fewer interaction steps. Finally, real-world demonstrations on a Unitree G1 combine search, visual inspection, spatial reasoning, and synthesized gestures without a pre-built map, illustrating how online programming extends robot behavior beyond route following and predefined skills.

</details>


### [72] [Benchmarking EMlog Calibration for Autonomous Surface Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39203)
*Samuel Cohen-Salmon,Itzik Klein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文针对DVL不可用时EMLog速度测量受系统误差影响、且缺乏动态海况下校准模型对比的问题，提出基于模型的EMLog校准比较方法，并在MARVEL实船221分钟数据上验证，最佳方案将速度估计提升71%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DVL因作业高度限制会不可用，EMLog可作为连续速度估计的鲁棒替代；但原始EMLog测量存在系统误差，需在任务前校准。现有文献缺少不同校准模型在快速变化动态海况下的基准对比评估。

Method: 提出一种比较性模型校准方法，评估四种不同校准模型，并使用两种估计流程；在MARVEL水面载具动态海试采集的221分钟连续真实遥测数据上严格验证，数据包含两个EMLog和DVL记录。

Result: 实验表明，采用卡尔曼滤波实现的偏置与尺度误差模型将速度估计提升71%；动态机动相比标准直线路径还能进一步提高精度。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种经真实海试验证的、面向自主水面车辆的实时在线EMLog校准方法，为DVL不可用时的连续速度估计提供了可靠替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate velocity measurement is a fundamental requirement for autonomous surface and underwater vehicles. Commonly, velocity is provided by a Doppler velocity log (DVL) sensor, yet it becomes unavailable due to operational altitude constraints. In such situations, electromagnetic logs (EMLogs) provide a critically robust alternative for continuous velocity estimation. However, raw EMLog measurements are inherently corrupted by systematic errors, which need to be calibrated prior mission begins. Currently, a benchmarking comparative evaluation of how different calibration models perform under rapidly changing dynamic sea conditions is missing in the literature. To bridge this gap, this paper presents a comparative model-based calibration methodology that evaluates four distinct calibration models using two different estimation pipelines. The proposed framework is rigorously validated on a unique 221 minutes of continuous real-world telemetry collected from the MARVEL surface vehicle during dynamic sea trials. The dataset contains two different EMLogs and DVL recordings. Experimental results demonstrate that the bias and scale error model implemented with the Kalman filter improves the speed estimation by 71%. We also demonstrate that dynamical manoeuvres further improve the accuracy compared to standard straight-line paths, ultimately delivering a validated, real-time online calibration EMLog approach for autonomous surface vehicles.

</details>


### [73] [DSDyn-VLA: A Dual-Stream Dynamic Manipulation Framework with Motion Perception, Future Awareness, and Realtime Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39198)
*Wenhao Li,Xiu Su,Yu Han,Yichao Cao,Shan You,Chang Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DSDyn-VLA慢-快双流动态操作框架，通过运动感知前瞻规划与实时残差修正，解决VLA在动态环境中的感知、延迟和控制三大瓶颈，并在动态基准和真实场景中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在静态任务中表现优异，但在物体运动场景（如传送带操作）中受限，主要存在三个根本问题：感知差距，静态视觉输入缺乏时序运动线索；延迟差距，推理延迟导致动作过时；控制差距，开环动作块执行缺乏实时调整。

Method: 提出DSDyn-VLA慢-快双流动态操作框架。慢速Flow-Planner作为宏观规划器，通过引入光流增强时序感知，并利用未来状态感知机制预判并补偿推理延迟，生成全局一致且运动感知的动作块；快速Res-Refiner采用轻量级强化学习策略，基于实时观测向规划动作块注入高频闭环残差修正。同时提出基于MuJoCo的动态物体操作基准DynBench，包含九项任务。

Result: 在高延迟设置的Kinetix动态基准上，DSDyn-VLA相比当前SOTA方法将失败率降低超过76%；在真实动态场景中成功率约为PI0.5的6倍，在DynBench上约为5倍。

Conclusion: DSDyn-VLA有效缓解了动态操作中的感知、延迟与控制瓶颈，显著提升动态环境下的操作鲁棒性与成功率，并将开源全部代码与权重。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models excel in static tasks, they struggle in dynamic environments where objects are in motion (e.g., conveyor belt manipulation). We identify three fundamental limitations hindering current VLAs in these scenarios: the \textbf{perception gap}, where static visual inputs lack temporal motion cues; the \textbf{latency gap}, where inference delays render actions obsolete; and the \textbf{control gap}, caused by the open-loop action chunk execution without real-time adjustment. In this work, we propose \textbf{DSDyn-VLA}, a Slow-Fast \textbf{D}ual-\textbf{S}tream \textbf{Dyn}amic manipulation framework that integrates motion-aware foresighted planning with real-time residual correction. The slow \textbf{Flow-Planner} serves as a macro-planner. By enhancing the VLA with optical flow for temporal perception and a future state awareness mechanism to preemptively offset inference latency, it produces globally consistent, motion-aware action chunks. Complementing this, the fast \textbf{Res-Refiner} employs a lightweight RL policy to inject high-frequency, closed-loop corrections into the planned action chunks based on real-time observations. In addition, we introduce \textbf{DynBench}, a MuJoCo-based benchmark for dynamic object manipulation that comprises nine tasks. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DSDyn-VLA reduces the failure rate by over 76\% compared to current SOTA method in high-latency setting on the Kinetix dynamic benchmark, while achieving about 6$\times$ the success rate of PI0.5 in real-world dynamic settings and about 5$\times$ on DynBench. We will open-source all the code and weights.

</details>


### [74] [LocoWM: High-Precision Locomotion through World-Model-Guided Residual Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39179)
*Zijie Zhao,Shengqian Chen,Xiaoxu Wang,Han Jiang,Yuanheng Zhu,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LocoWM 是一种世界模型引导的预主动残差自适应框架，通过预测未来物理状态序列来生成附加动作修正，从而实现高精度运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度运动需在跟踪运动指令的同时精确调节任务相关物理状态；但端到端联合优化会导致精度目标优化不足，而反应式残差控制只能在偏差可观测后才调整动作，存在滞后。

Method: 基座策略负责指令跟随运动；动作条件世界模型依据本体感受历史与拟采用的基座动作预测未来物理状态序列；残差适配器以该预测序列为条件，生成附加动作修正以补偿预期偏差。训练分两阶段：先学习运动与动作条件动力学，再冻结二者并单独训练适配器，从而将运动能力习得与精度自适应解耦。

Result: 在地形整平、加速度补偿与推力恢复实验中，相较端到端与反应式残差基线，该方法在控制精度与抗扰动鲁棒性上均取得提升。

Conclusion: 世界模型引导的预主动残差自适应能有效提升高精度运动控制的精度与鲁棒性，且演示与代码已公开。

Abstract: High-precision locomotion combines motion-command tracking with precise regulation of task-relevant physical states, enabling robots to interact reliably with their surroundings during motion. Joint end-to-end optimization can leave precision objectives insufficiently optimized, while reactive residual control adjusts actions only after deviations become observable. We present \textbf{LocoWM}, a world-model-guided preactive residual adaptation framework for high-precision locomotion. A base policy provides command-following locomotion, while an action-conditioned world model predicts a sequence of future physical states from proprioceptive history and the proposed base action. A residual adapter conditions on this predicted sequence to generate additive action corrections that compensate for anticipated deviations. Two-stage training first learns locomotion and action-conditioned dynamics, then freezes both modules while training the adapter, separating locomotion acquisition from precision adaptation. Experiments spanning terrain leveling, acceleration compensation, and push recovery demonstrate improved control precision and disturbance robustness over end-to-end and reactive residual baselines. Demos and code are available at: this https URL

</details>


### [75] [Exploiting Vulnerabilities: Universal Adversarial Attacks on Vision-Language-Action Models in Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39178)
*Songhua Yang,Ziyu Liu,Yuanwei Liu,Xuetao Li,Xuanye Fei,He Huang,Zheng Wang,Miao Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种表面纹理经过优化的通用对抗球体，可置于机器人视野中，通过多层级攻击显著降低VLA模型在机器人操作任务中的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型将视觉感知、语言理解和动作生成端到端结合，并直接与物理世界和人类交互，因此其安全性至关重要；即使是微小漏洞也可能导致灾难性失败。

Method: 设计名为Universal Adversarial Object的球体，优化其表面纹理以攻击VLA模型；采用多层级攻击框架，联合干扰轨迹规划、任务执行和动作控制；在仿真和真实机器人环境中进行验证。

Result: 实验表明，该对抗物体使两个代表性VLA模型Pi0和RDT的平均任务成功率降低31.2%-39.9%，在复杂场景中成功率降至接近零。

Conclusion: 该工作揭示了VLA模型对物理世界对抗物体的脆弱性，表明此类通用对抗攻击具有现实可行性，凸显了机器人安全与鲁棒性防护的重要性。

Abstract: Recently, Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have revolutionized robotic manipulation by seamlessly integrating visual perception, language understanding, and action generation in an end-to-end learning framework. However, since these models are designed to interact directly with the physical world and humans, their security is critical, and even small vulnerabilities can lead to catastrophic failures. In this work, we propose the Universal Adversarial Object, a sphere with optimized surface texture that significantly degrades task success rates when placed within the robot's field of view. Specifically, our approach introduces a multi-level attack framework that jointly disrupts trajectory planning, task execution, and action control. We validate our method in both simulated and real-world robotic settings. Experimental results demonstrate that the adversarial object reduces the average task success rates by 31.2%-39.9% for two representative VLA models (Pi0 and RDT), with success rates dropping to near zero in complex scenarios. Index Terms--Vision-Language-Action models, adversarial attack, robotic security, universal adversarial object

</details>


### [76] [Concurrent Semantic Search and Mission Execution for LTL Missions in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39153)
*Fernando Salanova Gaspar,David Morilla Cabello,Cristian Mahulea Poleuca,Eduardo Montijano Muñoz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种自适应高层规划方法，将任务驱动的语义搜索与LTLf任务执行交织进行，利用在线构建的度量-语义场景图和DFA，在未知室内环境中非短视地选择并排序路点，从而完成更多任务、覆盖更少环境并缩短路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知环境下复杂任务要求机器人同时推理应做什么与还需发现什么。现有LTLf方法多假设环境已知，或将探索与执行分离；语义探索方法一次只寻找单个目标且忽略当前任务，导致探索与任务执行脱节。

Method: 核心是任务驱动语义搜索与任务执行交织的自适应高层规划。在线构建两种表示：由视觉语言模型（VLM）生成的度量-语义场景图，用于提供定位任务涉及物体的证据；编码任务的确定性有限自动机（DFA），用于指示各任务状态下哪些物体重要。每个规划阶段选择对语义搜索和任务推进均最有价值的路点，并评估其对剩余任务阶段的价值以避免阻塞状态；将所选路点排序为单一高层计划，并随新信息到达重规划。

Result: 在真实感室内环境中针对五类任务，相比对比方法完成更多任务，同时覆盖更少环境，路径更短，并满足任务施加的约束。

Conclusion: 该方法通过非短视地交织语义搜索与任务执行，有效弥补未知环境下LTLf任务规划中探索与执行分离的不足，提升任务完成率与规划效率。

Abstract: Planning complex missions in unknown environments requires robots to reason simultaneously about what they should do and what they still need to discover. Existing approaches for solving LTLf missions typically assume a known environment, or separate the exploration of the environment from the execution of the mission, while semantic exploration methods look for one target at a time and ignore the mission being executed. To fill this gap, our main contribution is an adaptive high-level planning method that interleaves a task-driven semantic search with the execution of the mission, advancing both in a non-myopic manner. Our method leverages two representations built online, a metric-semantic scene graph, built with a Vision Language Model (VLM), that provides the evidence needed to locate the objects the mission refers to, and the deterministic finite automaton (DFA) encoding the mission, that indicates which of them matter at each mission state. At every planning stage, our planner selects the waypoints that are most valuable for both the semantic search and the advancement of the mission, valuing them over the remaining mission stages in order to avoid blocking states. The selected waypoints are then ordered in a single high-level plan, which is recomputed as new information arrives. In photorealistic indoor environments over five mission types, our method completes more missions than the compared approaches while having to cover less of the environment, and it does so with shorter paths and complying with the restrictions imposed by the mission.

</details>


### [77] [Linear Recurrent Memory Suffices to Distil a World-Model Policy for Robot Air Hockey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39151)
*F. Olivia Fan,Oliver Obst*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 在模拟冰球防守的追踪丢失任务中，将DreamerV3教师蒸馏为64维循环策略后，纯线性循环核心（k=0）与GRU基线及教师表现相当，提高非线性创新秩无增益，表明该记忆依赖控制任务未必需要非线性循环动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究记忆依赖控制是否必须依赖非线性循环动力学，还是简单线性记忆机制配合非线性表示学习已足够。

Method: 在追踪暂时丢失的模拟冰球防守中，以DreamerV3为教师，验证记忆必要性（无记忆策略较差，重置教师循环状态后性能骤降）。将教师蒸馏为紧凑循环策略：64维状态，对角线性循环并可选加入秩-k非线性创新，同时保留非线性观测编码器和动作头。与GRU基线及教师比较，使用5个匹配随机种子；最终在模型与分析冻结后才打开的新测试划分上评估。

Result: 在5个匹配种子下，纯线性循环模型（k=0）在整个测试的追踪丢失范围内匹配GRU基线和教师；提高非线性创新秩未带来可测收益；线性模型循环参数更少、计算量更低，但性能相当。

Conclusion: 对该模拟任务、教师、状态维度、黑障时长以及观测编码器与动作头仍为非线性的策略而言，围绕简单线性记忆机制进行非线性表示学习可能已足够，非线性循环动力学并非必需；上述结论受限于这些设定。

Abstract: Does memory-dependent control need nonlinear recurrent dynamics? We study simulated air-hockey defence under temporary loss of puck tracking. A DreamerV3 teacher outperforms a memoryless policy under tracking loss, while resetting the teacher's recurrent state sharply reduces performance, which demonstrates that the task requires memory. We distil this teacher into compact recurrent policies with a 64 dimensional state, with a combination of a diagonal linear recurrence and an optional rank-$k$ nonlinear innovation while retaining nonlinear observation encoders and action heads. Across five matched seeds, the purely linear recurrent model ($k=0$) matches both the GRU baseline and the teacher throughout the tested range of tracking loss. Increasing nonlinear innovation rank providing no measured benefits. This result is obtained on a fresh test split, which will be only opened after all models and analyses are frozen. The linear model requires fewer recurrent parameters and less computation than GRU, but performs comparably. These results suggest that, for this memory dependent control task, nonlinear representation learning around a simple linear memory mechanism can be sufficient, and that nonlinear recurrent dynamics are not necessarily required. These conclusions are limited to the simulated task, teacher, state dimension, and blackout horizon considered here, and to policies whose observation encoder and action head remain nonlinear.

</details>


### [78] [Blackout vs. Freeze: Analyzing Physical Failure Modes of VLAs under Camera Faults](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39145)
*Heejae Suh,Jongwook Han,Zahra Gholami,Yohan Jo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究分析 π0.5 和 GR00T 等 VLA 模型在图像黑屏、冻结等视觉输入故障下的物理失效模式，发现不同故障会导致不同风险，并提出应利用故障下仍可用的机器人与物体信息来限制危险运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可靠的视觉输入会损害视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型的任务表现，并可能带来物理安全风险；因此需要理解不同视觉故障如何影响动作与物理交互，以及现有缓解措施是否真正安全。

Method: 分析 π0.5 与 GR00T 在图像黑屏、冻结等输入故障下的表现；比较任务成功率、关节行为和物体掉落等物理失效模式；通过选择性干预检验本体感觉、机器人描绘、腕部视角物体信息和场景视角的作用；评估相机黑屏训练与免训练替换故障视觉嵌入两种缓解方法；并进行真机试验。

Result: 黑屏与冻结即使任务成功率相近，也产生不同物理失效：冻结导致更极端的关节行为，夹爪闭合后黑屏导致更多物体掉落，且在没有本体感觉时最明显。本体感觉可部分补偿被移除的机器人描绘并减少非目标接触，但当腕部视角中的物体信息缺失时，即使有剩余场景视角，也难以充分恢复任务成功。两种缓解方法在特定条件下提升成功率，但可能增加意外接触或对周围物体的扰动；真机试验还显示，相机故障下的成功执行仍可能伴随意外物理交互。

Conclusion: 应设计 VLA 策略，在相机故障时利用仍然可用的机器人与物体信息，以限制危险运动，从而提升物理安全，而不仅仅关注任务成功率。

Abstract: Unreliable visual inputs can harm task performance and cause potential physical safety risks for vision-language-action (VLA) models. We analyze how $\pi 0.5$ and GR00T models act under input faults such as image blackouts and freezing. We find that blackout and freezing produce distinct physical failure modes even when task-success rates are similarly low: freezing causes more extreme joint behavior, whereas blackout after gripper closure can cause more object drops, most markedly without proprioception. Selective intervention studies reveal that proprioception (current robot state) partly compensates for the removed robot depictions and reduces non-target contact. However, it cannot sufficiently restore task success when wrist-view object information is removed, even when aided by the remaining scene view. We then evaluate two mitigation approaches: camera-blackout training and training-free replacement of faulty visual embeddings. Both improve task success in selected conditions, but can increase unintended contact or disturbance to surrounding objects. Real-robot trials further show that successful execution under camera faults can still involve unintended physical interactions. These findings motivate designing VLA policies that use the robot and object information still available under camera faults to limit hazardous motion.

</details>


### [79] [Drape-Compatible Tool-Tip Localization for Hand-Held Laparoscopic Instruments via UWB Carrier-Phase Ranging and Trocar-Constrained Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39129)
*Jinseok Lee,Dongho Yee,Minsung Kim,Younghoon Noh,Seonho Shim,Juahn Oh,Yechan Seo,Jiyul Lee,Seong Jeong,Hyuk Choi,Hyoun-Joong Kong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种仅用超宽带载波相位测距和IMU的腹腔镜工具尖端定位方法，借助trocar的已知位置与IMU轴向约束，将欠定问题化为可解，无需第二载频即可处理整周模糊度，并在体模与活体兔实验中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手术机器人需要知道每个器械工作端相对内窥镜相机和其他器械的位置，但腹腔镜手术通常只有内窥镜视频，且铺盖无菌铺巾的器械会遮挡自身所有光学路径，使纯视觉或光学追踪不可行。

Method: 在手持器械手柄侧和内窥镜上各放置一个位于无菌屏障外的超宽带天线节点，以内窥镜天线为参考；两个器械天线提供6个坐标但只有3对距离，原始问题欠定3个自由度。载波相位每对还引入一个整周模糊度，使距离仅确定到λ/2=23.1 mm。利用trocar：每根器械轴穿过位置已知的端口，其方向由板上IMU测得，因此每个天线只剩插入深度这一单自由度。两个未知深度对应3个距离，其中两个用于求解深度，第三个剩余方程用于检测错误整周模糊度或错误安装偏移。同一求解过程在内窥镜坐标系中返回两个工具尖端，且链路始终保持在单个PLL锁定内，无需通常解整周模糊度所需的第二载波频率。

Result: 在含金属器械的11孔体模中，三对距离的标准差为0.10–0.33 mm，更新率为35–46 Hz；将无菌铺巾压在天线上时链路保持不变，而光学路径会被阻断；记录器还被带入活体兔阑尾切除术中试用。

Conclusion: 该方法能够在无菌铺巾遮挡和金属器械环境下实现高精度、较高频率的工具尖端定位，并具备活体可行性，为手术机器人提供了一种不依赖光学视线且无需额外载频的定位方案。

Abstract: A surgical robot policy needs to know where each instrument's working end sits relative to the camera and to the other instrument, yet a laparoscopic operation leaves only the endoscope video, and a draped instrument hides every optical path on itself. We estimate the tool tips from distances and an IMU alone. The distances are measured by ultra-wideband carrier phase between antenna nodes on the handle side of the instruments: one per hand-held instrument, one on the endoscope, all outside the sterile barrier. Take the endoscope antenna as the reference. The two instrument antennas carry six coordinates while the three pairs give three distances, so the problem is short by three, and averaging cannot close that gap because what is missing is information, not precision. Carrier phase adds one more unknown per pair, an integer that leaves distance fixed only modulo lambda/2 = 23.1 mm. The trocar settles both difficulties: each shaft passes through a port whose position is known and whose direction the on-board IMU measures, so its antenna keeps a single degree of freedom, the insertion depth. Two unknowns now face three distances -- two fix the depths and the third is left over, and that spare equation exposes a wrong integer or a wrong mounting offset instead of absorbing it. The same solve returns both tool tips in the endoscope frame, without the second carrier frequency that integer resolution usually needs, so the link never leaves a single PLL lock. In an 11-hole phantom among metal instruments the three distances hold sigma = 0.10-0.33 mm at 35-46 Hz, a sterile drape pressed onto the antennas leaves the link unchanged where an optical path would be blocked, and the logger was carried into a live rabbit appendectomy.

</details>


### [80] [LBDU-VIO: Learned Bias Dynamics and Uncertainty for Visual-Inertial Odometry with Unreliable Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39125)
*Qizhi Guo,Junning Lyu,Defu Lin,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LBDU-VIO：一种学习增强的MSCKF视觉惯性里程计，利用神经常微分方程建模连续时间零偏动态，并学习IMU不确定性模型，以提升视觉信息不可靠时的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多状态约束卡尔曼滤波（MSCKF）采用随机游走零偏假设和固定噪声参数，当视觉信息不可靠时鲁棒性受限。

Method: LBDU-VIO在MSCKF框架中引入两个学习模块：用神经常微分方程（Neural ODE）建模连续时间零偏动态，替代随机游走模型来传播滤波器零偏状态；用IMU不确定性模型预测运动自适应量测噪声协方差，用于协方差传播。两个模型仅通过位姿监督训练，无需直接标签。

Result: 在真实世界EuRoC和TUM-VI基准上，误差低于代表性视觉惯性基线；在EuRoC中10秒视觉中断序列上，相比S-MSCKF平均相对位置误差降低25.1%。

Conclusion: 学习连续时间零偏动态与运动自适应IMU噪声协方差可有效增强MSCKF在视觉退化或中断场景下的鲁棒性和定位精度。

Abstract: Visual-inertial odometry (VIO) for aerial robots relies on high rate inertial measurement unit (IMU) propagation between visual updates. However, conventional multi state constraint Kalman filters (MSCKFs) use random walk bias assumptions and fixed noise parameters, which can limit robustness when visual information is unreliable. To address this problem, we propose LBDU-VIO, a learning-augmented MSCKF with learned continuous time bias dynamics and an IMU uncertainty model. A neural ordinary differential equation (ODE) models continuous time bias dynamics to propagate the filter's bias states, replacing their random walk model. The IMU uncertainty model predicts motion adaptive measurement noise covariances for covariance propagation. Both models are trained with pose supervision without direct labels. Experiments on real world EuRoC and TUM-VI benchmarks show lower errors than representative visual-inertial baselines, including a 25.1% reduction in mean relative position error compared with S-MSCKF on EuRoC sequences with 10s visual outage.

</details>


### [81] [Occlusion-Aware, Quasi-Static, Stability-Oriented Trajectory Planning on Uneven Terrain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39105)
*Amith Manoharan,Chinmay Mundane,Aayush Bahukhandi,K. Madhava Krishna,Karel Zimmermann,Arun Kumar Singh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向崎岖非结构化越野地形的基于模型轨迹生成框架，通过地形遮挡不确定性建模、不确定性传播和CEM轨迹优化，并结合Flow Matching热启动地形拟合，提升刚性四轮车准静态稳定性与通过成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非结构化越野自主导航需同时推理车辆-地形交互与环境未知；地形遮挡形成盲区，导致地形表示和姿态/接触估计存在认知不确定性，进而影响稳定性导向参考轨迹生成。

Method: 三项核心贡献：1）将地形遮挡盲区建模为固定特征傅里叶地形表示中的覆盖诱导认知不确定性，并用正则化逆Hessian估计量化；2）通过隐式微分将不确定性传播到非线性最小二乘姿态/接触模型，并将姿态、接触点及各轮表面法向不确定性项纳入基于交叉熵方法CEM的轨迹优化；3）引入Flow Matching模型热启动地形拟合，在保留基于模型细化的情况下评估拟合精度与时延权衡。

Result: 在六种合成地形、每地形30组匹配起终点上，完整框架观测失败率为18.9%，两个代表性基线分别为46.1%和41.7%，移除传播不确定性评分的消融为34.4%；硬件评估覆盖六个不同户外环境，正文展示两个代表性执行，补充视频包含另外四个执行，并报告Flow Matching热启动的精度-时延权衡。

Conclusion: 该框架可有效降低越野环境下轨迹生成失败率，其中不确定性传播与评分是关键；Flow Matching可加速地形拟合且保留模型细化，整体具备真实户外部署潜力。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in unstructured off-road environments requires reasoning about both vehicle--terrain interaction and environmental unknowns. We propose a model-based framework for generating quasi-static, stability-oriented reference trajectories for rigid, non-articulated four-wheeled vehicles on highly uneven terrain. Our work makes three primary contributions. First, we model blind spots caused by terrain occlusion as coverage-induced epistemic uncertainty in a fixed-feature Fourier terrain representation, quantified through a regularized inverse-Hessian estimate. Second, we propagate this uncertainty through the Nonlinear Least-Squares (NLS) pose/contact model using implicit differentiation and incorporate the resulting pose, contact-point, and per-wheel surface-normal uncertainty terms into trajectory optimization based on the Cross-Entropy Method (CEM). Third, we introduce a Flow Matching model that warm-starts terrain fitting, and we evaluate its fitting-accuracy--latency trade-off while retaining model-based refinement. Across six synthetic terrains with 30 matched start--goal pairs per terrain, the complete framework produced an observed failure rate of 18.9%, compared with 46.1% and 41.7% for two representative baselines and 34.4% for an ablation that removed the propagated-uncertainty scoring. Hardware evaluations span six distinct outdoor environments, with two representative executions presented in the paper and four additional executions included in the supplementary video. The evaluation also reports the accuracy--latency trade-off for the Flow Matching warm start.

</details>


### [82] [DiFF: Doppler-informed Flow Matching for Human Motion Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39098)
*Kai Wang,Mingle Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 DiFF，一种结合多普勒先验与基于 KAN 的条件流匹配的生成式框架，用于 4D 毫米波雷达点云的非刚性人体运动场景流估计，并在真实数据集上取得 SOTA，mmBody 上 3D 端点误差降至毫米级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 4D 毫米波雷达可在隐私保护下感知人体运动，对下一代人机交互至关重要；但雷达点云极度稀疏且噪声大，使非刚性运动流估计严重病态，现有以刚性运动为中心的方法和先前工作未充分利用 4D 雷达固有的丰富多普勒速度线索。

Method: 提出 DiFF 生成式框架，将多普勒信息驱动的运动先验与基于 Kolmogorov-Arnold Network（KAN）的条件流匹配模型结合。核心包括 KAN-attention 机制以增强特征提取表达力，以及先验引导的生成过程，利用多普勒线索正则化病态解空间。

Result: 在多个真实世界数据集上取得 SOTA 性能；在 mmBody 基准上，将 3D 端点误差降低至毫米级。

Conclusion: DiFF 通过显式利用 4D 雷达多普勒线索并以生成式流匹配建模非刚性运动，有效缓解雷达点云稀疏噪声导致的病态问题，推动隐私保护人体运动感知与人机交互应用。

Abstract: Perceiving human motion via privacy-preserving 4D millimeter-wave (mmWave) radar is critical for next-generation human-robot interaction (HRI), where point cloud scene flow serves as a foundational motion representation. Yet the extreme sparsity and noise of 4D radar point clouds make non-rigid motion flow estimation severely ill-posed--a challenge that existing rigid-centric methods and prior works fail to adequately address, largely because they neglect the rich Doppler velocity cues inherent in 4D radar. We propose DiFF, a generative framework that marries Doppler-informed motion priors with a Kolmogorov-Arnold Network (KAN)-based conditional flow matching model. At its core, a KAN-attention mechanism enables expressive feature extraction, while a prior-guided generative process harnesses Doppler cues to regularize the ill-posed solution space. Extensive experiments show that DiFF achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across diverse real-world datasets, reducing 3D endpoint error to the millimeter scale on the mmBody benchmark.

</details>


### [83] [SteerQuant: Steering Quantization Error with Action-Guided Scaling in World-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39056)
*Yunhan Wang,Haodong Wang,Zhiming Liu,Zicong Hong,Qianli Liu,Xiaoyi Pang,Yangjia Hu,Quanxin Shou,Yikun Miao,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出面向世界-动作模型（WAMs）的4-bit量化框架SteerQuant与推理引擎Rudder，通过将量化误差导向对最终动作影响较小的计算，实现低比特下可靠控制与显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAMs以共享权重通过迭代去噪联合生成未来世界状态与动作，需处理视频、本体感觉和动作等异构语义流。量化虽可降低推理成本，但不同流中相近的数值误差对最终动作影响差异显著，仅以数值精度衡量不足以保障可靠控制。

Method: SteerQuant先刻画各流量化误差对最终动作的影响，并据此指导共享通道缩放，使误差偏向影响较小的计算；再按流和去噪步分别校准激活缩放，以适应激活范围与动作影响的变化，且无需复制权重或提高特定流位宽。为降低缩放带来的额外内核启动与内存流量，提出Rudder 4-bit推理引擎，将缩放与输出补偿融合进低比特内核。

Result: 在W4A8和W4A4下，SteerQuant在三个WAMs上使LIBERO平均成功率与全精度差距保持在0.8个百分点以内，去噪速度较BF16最高提升2.23倍，并降低峰值GPU内存。在真实双臂机器人上，W4A8部署实现1.35倍端到端推理加速，同时保持与BF16相当的平均任务成功率。

Conclusion: SteerQuant与Rudder表明，针对WAMs的流敏感量化与低比特内核融合可在不复制权重、不提高特定流位宽的前提下，兼顾控制可靠性、推理速度与显存效率，适用于真实机器人部署。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) jointly generate future world states and actions through iterative denoising, using shared weights to process heterogeneous semantic streams of video, proprioceptive, and action tokens. Quantization reduces inference cost, but comparable numerical errors in different streams can have markedly different effects on final actions, making numerical accuracy alone insufficient for reliable control. We introduce SteerQuant, a 4-bit quantization framework for WAMs that steers errors toward computations with less influence on final actions. It maps how each stream's quantization errors affect final actions and uses this map to guide shared channel scaling. Activation scaling is further calibrated for each stream and denoising step to accommodate changes in activation ranges and action impact. This adapts quantization to different stream requirements without duplicating weights or increasing bit-widths for selected streams. To reduce the extra kernel launches and memory traffic introduced by scaling, we develop Rudder, a 4-bit inference engine for WAMs that fuses scaling and output compensation into low-bit kernels. Under W4A8 and W4A4, SteerQuant maintains mean LIBERO success within 0.8 percentage points of full precision, while delivering up to $2.23\times$ denoising speedup over BF16 across three WAMs with reduced peak GPU memory usage. On a real dual-arm robot, W4A8 deployment achieves a $1.35\times$ end-to-end inference speedup while maintaining average task success relative to BF16.

</details>


### [84] [CLIPPER Beyond Shortlisting: Auditable Decision Support for Changing Municipal Micromobility Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39054)
*Julian Teusch,Jörg Philipp Müller,Monika Sester*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 CLIPPER，一种面向共享微出行停车规划的低延迟贪心优化器，通过有界候选池与可选审计，在保持约束精确和覆盖率损失极小的同时大幅加速多方案重优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 市政规划工作坊中，规划者需频繁调整禁停区、保留站点、间距或面积分配等政策输入；每次编辑都会改变可行站点及其需求覆盖，因此必须在同一空间数据上重新优化。全集合贪心虽是透明参考方法，但在城市尺度下每个备选方案需数十秒，难以支持交互式规划。

Method: CLIPPER（Constraint-exact Low-latency Iterative Planning with Pooled Evaluation and Replay）是一种带审计功能的优化器，需求由与 Braunschweig 市合作引出。每轮贪心构建确定性的有界候选池，计算各候选可新增的未覆盖需求，并拒绝违反活动约束的候选；可选离线审计扫描所有剩余可行候选，记录受限候选池的遗漏。评估覆盖 Braunschweig、Munich、Berlin 的完整十一状态编辑链 E0 至 E10，并采用 K=1024 候选/组及固定宽度模式 CLIPPER-F。

Result: CLIPPER-F 相对同政策下全集合贪心的平均覆盖差距分别为 0.245、0.003、0.001 个百分点（Braunschweig、Munich、Berlin），平均 rollout 时间降低 13.6 至 28.9 倍；审计运行中没有任何一次在池外仍存在可增加覆盖的候选时终止。Braunschweig 官方禁停区数据的两个校验版本所得方案在约 540 个选中站点中有 30 个不同，但覆盖仅变化约 0.1 个百分点。这些变化仍需市政评估与实施。

Conclusion: 研究支持提出一种市政流程：对政策输入进行版本化；在报告覆盖指标的同时报告站点变化；最终决策前扫描完整候选集。CLIPPER 可支撑交互式规划，但最终方案仍需市政评估与实施。

Abstract: In municipal planning workshops, planners and other stakeholders compare shared-micromobility parking policies by varying no-parking zones, retained sites, spacing, or area allocations. Each edit changes feasible sites and how much demand they cover, so the alternative must be reoptimized on the same spatial data. Full-set greedy, the transparent reference for this task, takes tens of seconds per alternative at city scale. We present Constraint-exact Low-latency Iterative Planning with Pooled Evaluation and Replay (CLIPPER), an optimizer with audit functions developed for requirements elicited with the City of Braunschweig. In each greedy round, it forms a deterministic candidate pool of bounded size, computes how much still-uncovered demand each candidate would add, and rejects candidates that violate an active constraint. An optional offline audit scans every remaining feasible candidate and records what the restricted pool omitted. We evaluate these functions on complete eleven-state edit chains ($E_0,\ldots,E_{10}$) in Braunschweig, Munich, and Berlin. With $K=1024$ candidates per group, the fixed-width mode CLIPPER-F has mean coverage gaps to full-set greedy under the same policy of 0.245, 0.003, and 0.001 percentage points in Braunschweig, Munich, and Berlin, respectively, while mean rollout time falls by factors of 13.6--28.9; no audited run terminates while a candidate outside the pool could still increase coverage. Plans computed from two checksummed versions of Braunschweig's official no-parking-zone data differ in 30 of about 540 selected sites although coverage moves by only about 0.1 percentage points. These changes still require municipal assessment and implementation. The findings inform a proposed municipal process that versions policy inputs, reports site changes beside coverage, and scans the full candidate set before a final decision.

</details>


### [85] [Looking Back to Move Forward: Temporal Verification for Generative Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39038)
*Haoxuan Wang,Wayne Wu,Yan Yan,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TeV 是一种面向 flow-matching VLA 的高效时序感知动作验证框架，通过时序 token 和能量式验证器提升生成式策略的动作选择与执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式策略虽能结合表达性动作建模与大规模模仿学习，但异构示教易导致次优动作块，误差随时间累积并将机器人推向难以恢复的分布外状态。现有动作验证方法通常仅基于当前观测评估候选动作，忽略轨迹连续性，且依赖大型验证器和额外专家示教，训练成本高。

Method: TeV 首先学习一个时序 token，用于总结近期观测-动作历史，使候选动作块作为执行轨迹的延续而非孤立预测被评估。基于该 token，TeV 无需额外专家示教或偏好标注即可构造正负样本对，并以对比方式训练基于能量的验证器，使更高质量、轨迹一致的动作块获得更低能量。除事后排序外，TeV 还利用学到的能量景观引导中间 flow 样本向低能量区域移动，在最终选择前改进候选动作。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，TeV 能可靠地对动作候选进行排序，提高任务成功率，并产生更平滑的执行轨迹。

Conclusion: TeV 通过时序感知的动作验证与能量引导，为生成式机器人策略提供了一种高效测试时扩展方法，可缓解误差累积和分布外失败，提升动作质量与执行稳定性。

Abstract: Generative policies have emerged as a promising paradigm for robot learning, combining expressive generative action modeling with scalable imitation learning from large demonstration corpora. However, heterogeneous demonstrations can induce suboptimal action chunks whose errors compound over time, eventually driving the robot into out-of-distribution states from which recovery is difficult. Action verification offers a test-time scaling strategy for mitigating this failure mode by sampling multiple candidate actions and using a verifier to select one for execution. Existing approaches, however, remain temporally myopic and costly to train, evaluating candidates from the current observation alone without accounting for trajectory continuity and often relying on large verifiers and additional expert demonstrations. In this paper, we introduce Temporal Verification (TeV), an efficient temporally aware action verification framework for flow-matching VLAs. TeV first learns a temporal token that summarizes recent observation--action history, enabling candidate chunks to be evaluated as continuations of the execution trajectory rather than as isolated predictions. Conditioned on this token, TeV constructs positive--negative pairs without additional expert demonstrations or preference annotations and trains an energy-based verifier contrastively to assign lower energy to higher-quality, trajectory-consistent action chunks. Beyond post-hoc ranking, TeV further uses the learned energy landscape to guide intermediate flow samples toward lower-energy regions, improving candidates before final selection. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that TeV provides reliably ranks action candidates, improves task success rates, and produces smoother execution trajectories.

</details>


### [86] [Make Code as Policy Great Again: Frontier Agents Write, Call, and Evolve Robot Tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39018)
*Shijia Ge,Alex Zhou,Jianshu Zeng,Yexing Wan,Di Wu,Zelin Zheng,Yazhe Wang,Zhiqi Jia,Xuan Shangguan,Jay Zhu,Yijun Liu,Lingyu He,Sihang Wu,Xiao He,Hongcheng Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 URAI：让前沿模型编写和修订可执行机器人工具，由代码执行多阶段运动，模型仅在工具调用之间做决策，从而提升机器人操作成功率与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型虽能控制机器人，但逐步推理每次伸、抓、退等动作会使操作缓慢且 token 消耗大，因此需要重新划分模型决策与代码执行的职责。

Method: URAI（Universal Robot-Agent Interface）耦合编程智能体与执行智能体：编程智能体根据任务意图编写可复用和任务专用工具，并通过执行反馈和人工指导进行修订与验证；执行智能体根据当前观测选择并参数化工具，每次调用本地执行完整动作后再交还控制权。验证后的工具修订可跨回合保留，无需更新基础模型权重；共享 GUI 和 API 使人和智能体都能使用同一套工具。

Result: 在 5 个 RoboDojo 任务和 4 个冻结执行智能体上，URAI 将总体成功率从直接指尖控制的 18.0% 提升至 53.0%，Swap Blocks 增益最大；使用相同工具时，预先写好的程序仅达 24%，而两个智能体在每次调用后决策可达 56%。四个智能体中有三个完成回合快 1.3–1.5 倍，执行智能体输出 token 少 1.5–1.7 倍；DeepSeek-V4-Flash 成本几乎不变。另在 7 个真实 AgileX 双臂任务上验证，涵盖物体操作、叠衣和人机交互井字棋。

Conclusion: URAI 连接前沿智能体的编程与决策能力，以可调用、可修订的可复用工具组织机器人控制，在多种任务和真实平台上同时改善成功率与执行效率。

Abstract: Frontier models can control robots, but reasoning through every reach, grasp, and retreat makes manipulation slow and token-intensive. We revisit code as policy with a different division of labor: models build executable tools, code handles multi-phase motions, and models decide what to do next. We introduce URAI (Universal Robot-Agent Interface), which couples a programming agent that constructs robot tools with an execution agent that uses them in a feedback loop. The programming agent writes reusable and task-specific tools from task intent and refines them through execution feedback and human guidance. The execution agent selects and parameterizes these tools from current observations; each call runs a complete motion locally before returning control to the agent. Unlike delegating subsequent decisions to a generated program, this design retains model-level decision-making between tool executions. Validated tool revisions persist across episodes without updating foundation-model weights, and a shared GUI and API make the same tools available to humans and agents. Across five RoboDojo tasks and four frozen execution agents, URAI raises aggregate success from 18.0% to 53.0% relative to direct fingertip control, with the largest gain on Swap Blocks; with the same tools, a program written in advance reaches only 24% against 56% for two agents deciding after each call. Three of the four agents also finish episodes 1.3-1.5 times faster with 1.5-1.7 times fewer execution-agent output tokens; DeepSeek-V4-Flash's cost barely changes. We further evaluate URAI on seven real-world AgileX dual-arm tasks, spanning object manipulation, cloth folding, and human-interactive tic-tac-toe. URAI connects the coding and decision-making capabilities of frontier agents, organizing robot control around reusable tools that agents can both invoke and revise.

</details>


### [87] [OccluDex: Hierarchical 3D Visuo-Tactile Representation Learning for Egocentric Dexterous Manipulation under Self-Occlusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39017)
*Ziheng Xu,Yueyuan Chen,Xinyuan He,Guoxing Liu,Yuanshuo Tan,Huiming Pan,Bin He,Shuo Jiang,Peter B. Shull*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出OccluDex，一种分层3D视触觉表征学习框架，用于在自遮挡下实现稳健的灵巧操作，通过融合全局几何与局部触觉接触信息，并在仿真和真实Shadow Hand上验证了性能与零样本sim-to-real泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第一视角/自中心传感中，机械手常遮挡任务相关物体表面与接触区域，导致状态估计可用视觉证据减少，进而使闭环控制和未见物体几何泛化困难。

Method: OccluDex采用多尺度掩码自编码逐步编码部分3D几何，并通过跨模态注意力将触觉接触token与高层几何特征融合；编码器从同步的人类视触觉演示中预训练，并作为冻结感知骨干迁移至下游强化学习。

Result: 在 faucet rotation（水龙头手柄整圈顺时针旋转）和 tabletop object reorientation（桌面物体180度重定向且不倾倒）任务中，仿真实验对未见物体准确率比最强SOTA基线高12.6%，对已见物体高8.3%；并用Shadow Hand完成物理实验，在未见物理物体上实现成功的零样本sim-to-real泛化。

Conclusion: 该方法能在机械手遮挡视觉时支持对已见和未见物体的类人自中心操作，有望推动遮挡条件下的灵巧操作与泛化。

Abstract: Reliable dexterous manipulation requires continuous estimation of object geometry and hand-object contact throughout interaction. With egocentric sensing, however, the manipulating hand frequently occludes task-relevant object surfaces and contact regions, reducing the visual evidence available for state estimation and thereby making robust closed-loop control and generalization to unseen object geometries particularly challenging. To address this, we present OccluDex, a hierarchical 3D visuo-tactile representation learning framework that integrates global geometric structure with local contact information for robust manipulation under dynamic self-occlusion during hand-object interaction. OccluDex adopts multi-scale masked autoencoding to progressively encode partial 3D geometry and fuses tactile contact tokens with high-level geometric features through cross-modal attention. The encoder is pretrained from synchronized human visuo-tactile demonstrations and transferred as a frozen perceptual backbone for downstream reinforcement learning. We evaluate OccluDex on a faucet rotation task, requiring one full clockwise handle revolution, and a tabletop object reorientation task, requiring a 180-degree tabletop object reorientation without toppling. In simulation experiments, OccluDex demonstrated 12.6% higher accuracy for unseen objects and 8.3% higher accuracy for previously seen objects than the strongest state-of-the-art baseline models. Physical experiments were further performed with a Shadow Hand to demonstrate successful zero-shot sim-to-real generalization on unseen physical objects. This results could enable humanoid egocentric object manipulation for seen and unseen objects even when the manipulating robotic hand occludes vision.

</details>


### [88] [Function beyond Form: Functional Correspondence for Cross-Embodiment Dexterous Grasp Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39006)
*Bolin Zou,Wenlong Dong,Mu Ai,Chao Tang,Aoxiang Gu,Lipeng Chen,Hong Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FunCo-Grasp，通过功能部件对齐与规范坐标系对齐建立跨异构机械手的功能对应，实现无需目标手抓取数据或微调的灵巧抓取生成与迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨本体灵巧抓取因机械手在几何、拓扑和运动学上差异显著而困难。现有方法缺少对不同结构中承担相似抓取功能区域之间的显式功能对应，导致模型学习手部特定交互模式，难以泛化到未见手。

Method: FunCo-Grasp建立跨异构手的功能对应：功能部件对齐将各手物理连杆按抓取角色映射到共享功能部件，形成规范功能模式；规范坐标系对齐在规范局部坐标系中表达这些部件。二者提供一致的部件间与手-物交互表示。以对齐手部表示和物体几何为条件，扩散模型生成功能部件目标空间排布，再转换为可执行关节配置。适应新手机仅需其几何与运动学模型及一次性轻量功能标注，无需目标手抓取数据、微调或学习型重定向。

Result: 在过滤CMapDataset的保留物体仿真中，三个已见手平均成功率为92.40%，四个未见手为74.02%。真实实验中，同一模型在两个未见手上无额外训练或微调达到76.00%总体成功率。

Conclusion: FunCo-Grasp通过功能对应与规范对齐有效学习可迁移抓取知识，并能泛化到未见手，验证了跨本体灵巧抓取生成的可行性。

Abstract: Cross-embodiment dexterous grasp generation remains challenging because robotic hands differ substantially in geometry, topology, and kinematics. Existing approaches often lack explicit correspondences between structurally different hand regions that play similar functional roles in a grasp, a concept we refer to as functional correspondence. Consequently, their models tend to learn hand-specific interaction patterns rather than transferable grasp knowledge, limiting generalization to unseen hands. To address this limitation, we introduce FunCo-Grasp, which establishes functional correspondences across heterogeneous hand embodiments. Specifically, Functional Part Alignment aligns each hand to a canonical functional schema by mapping physical links to shared functional parts according to their grasping roles, while Canonical Frame Alignment expresses these parts in canonical local frames. These two alignments provide a consistent representation for inter-part and hand-object interactions, allowing the model to learn transferable grasp knowledge across hands. Conditioned on the aligned hand representation and object geometry, a diffusion model generates the target spatial arrangement of the functional parts, which are then converted into an executable joint configuration. Adapting FunCo-Grasp to an unseen hand requires only its geometric and kinematic models and a one-time lightweight functional annotation, without target-hand grasp data, fine-tuning, or learned retargeting. In simulation on held-out objects from the filtered CMapDataset, we achieves average success rates of 92.40% on three seen hands and 74.02% on four unseen hands. In real-world experiments, the same model achieves an overall success rate of 76.00% on two unseen hands without additional training or fine-tuning. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of FunCo-Grasp in transferring grasp knowledge to unseen hands.

</details>


### [89] [NEXUS: Perceptive Whole-Body Control for Terrain-Adaptive Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39000)
*Xiangyu Miao,Junsong Wu,Jiyuan Shi,Weiji Xie,Jinrui Han,Xingyi Wang,Weinan Zhang,Chenjia Bai,Xuelong Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出NEXUS感知式全身控制框架，将人类运动指令与机载感知反馈结合，实现跨不匹配地形的全身遥操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全身遥操作要求人形机器人在地形与操作者不同时，感知局部地形并调整姿态与接触，而非逐帧复制人类动作；但平地与不同地形下同一行为的配对运动数据稀缺，限制地形自适应控制的学习监督。

Method: 开发可扩展的地形自适应算法，无需逐动作或逐地形调参即可高效生成高质量跨动作、跨地形运动配对；构建近1,000小时配对运动语料；通过教师-学生学习训练感知式全身控制器，使其融合人类运动命令与本体感知反馈，在不同地形上复现指令行为。

Result: 实验表明该方法能高效、可扩展地生成高质量运动数据；NEXUS兼具广泛行为覆盖、地形适应性与跟踪保真度，在评估基准上优于现有全身控制器；零样本真实世界部署实现多样未见过地形上的实时全身遥操作，验证了泛化能力。

Conclusion: NEXUS通过配对运动语料与教师-学生训练，实现了跨不匹配地形的感知式全身遥操作，并展现出良好的地形适应性、跟踪性能与真实世界泛化能力。

Abstract: Whole-body teleoperation requires a humanoid robot to reproduce a human operator's behavior even when their terrains differ. This demands that the robot perceive local terrain and adapt its posture and contacts accordingly, rather than copy the operator's motion frame by frame. However, paired motion data linking the same behaviors across flat ground and different terrains remain scarce, limiting supervision for learning terrain-adaptive control. To enable whole-body teleoperation across mismatched terrains, we introduce NEXUS, a perceptive whole-body control framework that combines human motion commands with onboard sensory feedback. We first develop a scalable terrain-aware adaptation algorithm that efficiently generates high-quality motion pairs across motions and terrains without per-motion or per-terrain tuning. Using a paired motion corpus totaling nearly 1,000 hours, we train a perceptive whole-body controller through teacher-student learning to reproduce commanded behaviors across terrains. Experiments demonstrate efficient, scalable generation of high-quality motion data and show that NEXUS combines broad behavioral coverage with terrain adaptability and tracking fidelity, outperforming existing whole-body controllers on the evaluated benchmarks. Zero-shot real-world deployment enables real-time whole-body teleoperation on diverse unseen terrains, further validating the generalization of our method. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [90] [Cue the Flow: Steering Flow-Matching Policies for Open-World Delivery Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38989)
*Haoxuan Wang,Griffin Galimi,Junhua Huang,Selina Song,Wayne Wu,Yan Yan,Bolei Zhou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于预训练流匹配VLA与轻量级空间提示适配器的双系统移动操作框架，用接地输出作为提示引导策略，无需替换底层控制器，提升开放世界指令跟随与新颖物体操作成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放世界货物配送要求移动机械臂遵循自由文本指令并操作未知物体；现有多系统方法虽能用高层grounding模型将语言转为视觉提示，但底层控制器在噪声感知、动态场景和富接触交互中仍脆弱。作者认为预训练VLA已具备强操作先验，缺少的是在新语言-物体映射下引导动作的目标空间信息。

Method: 以预训练流匹配VLA作为低层控制接口，将grounding输出视为空间cue进行策略引导。设计轻量cue-conditioned适配器：先用对比学习目标训练，获得显著且空间可区分的cue表示；再以监督学习预测作用于生成动作块的对角仿射变换，使策略动作对齐到cue指示的目标。

Result: 在桌面和移动底座场景中，方法在域内与域外物体上均提升指令跟随和操作成功率，平均任务成功率最高提升近2倍，且推理开销可忽略。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，将预训练VLA的强操作先验与接地空间提示结合，通过轻量适配器进行策略引导，是提升开放世界移动操作鲁棒性和泛化性的有效途径。

Abstract: Open-world goods delivery requires mobile manipulators to follow free-form user instructions and manipulate potentially novel objects. Existing dual-system approaches use high-level grounding models to convert language into grounded visual prompts, but their low-level controllers can remain brittle under noisy perception, dynamic scenes, and contact-rich interactions. We instead use a pretrained flow-matching vision-language-action model as the low-level control interface, leveraging its reactivity and robustness to environmental changes while treating the grounding output as a spatial cue for policy steering. Our key insight is that the pretrained VLA already provides a strong manipulation prior, while the spatial cue supplies the missing target information needed to guide actions under novel language--object mappings. Concretely, we introduce a lightweight cue-conditioned adapter. The adapter is first trained with contrastive objectives to produce salient and spatially discriminative cue representations, and is then supervised to predict a diagonal affine transformation over the generated action chunk, aligning policy steering with the cued target. Across tabletop and mobile-base settings, our method improves instruction following and manipulation success on both in-domain and out-of-domain objects, achieving up to near $2\times$ improvement in average task success rate with negligible inference overhead.

</details>


### [91] [Sparse-WAM: Accelerating World Action Models via Action-Guided Sparse Imagination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38984)
*Xinling Xie,Haodong Wang,Jiazhi Mi,Zhiming Liu,Zicong Hong,Xiaoyi Pang,Qianli Liu,Yangjia Hu,Ying Chen,Zhengyang Yan,Song Guo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 Sparse-WAM：一种无需训练、由动作引导的稀疏想象框架，通过选择性处理未来帧 token 来加速世界-动作模型（WAM）推理，并在保持任务性能的同时显著提速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WAM 通过联合预测未来视觉状态和动作来提升机器人控制的泛化能力，但推理成本很高，因为去噪过程中会反复处理密集的未来帧 token。已有方法多通过 token 剪枝优先保持视觉保真度，却未利用动作相关性来决定在 WAM 联合去噪中应保留哪些未来帧 token。

Method: 作者观察到：连续去噪步骤中，动作 token 对未来帧 token 的注意力（action-to-future attention）在空间分布上存在显著重叠，尽管未来表示持续更新。基于此提出 Action-Guided Token Selection，保留逐帧动作相关区域以及跨帧上下文。针对朴素实现会带来注意力打分和 token 打包开销的问题，进一步提出 Pilot 引擎，通过轻量级打分和跨步骤复用 token 选择来降低稀疏推理开销。

Result: 在 LIBERO 上使用 FastWAM-Joint，以及在 RoboLab-120 上使用 Cosmos 3 Edge，Sparse-WAM 在 NVIDIA RTX 4090 上相比密集 eager 推理分别取得约 2.0 倍和 1.8 倍推理加速，同时基本保持任务性能。

Conclusion: Sparse-WAM 表明，利用动作相关性进行未来帧 token 稀疏选择，并结合高效推理引擎，可在无需训练的情况下有效加速 WAM 推理，并在较大程度上保持任务表现。

Abstract: World-action models (WAMs) leverage pretrained video models to improve generalization in robot control by jointly predicting future visual states and actions. This capability comes at a substantial inference cost, as dense future-frame tokens are repeatedly processed during denoising. Prior methods address this by token pruning that prioritizes visual fidelity to reduce denoising costs in video diffusion models. However, these methods do not use action relevance to determine which future-frame tokens to retain during joint denoising in WAMs. In this paper, we propose Sparse-WAM, a training-free framework for action-guided sparse imagination that selectively processes future-frame tokens to accelerate WAM inference. We observe substantial overlap in the spatial distribution of attention from action tokens to future-frame tokens (action-to-future attention) between consecutive denoising steps, despite continued updates to the future representations. Motivated by this, we develop Action-Guided Token Selection to retain frame-specific action-relevant regions together with cross-frame context. However, a naive implementation can incur attention-scoring and token-packing overhead that offsets the computational savings from pruning. We therefore introduce Pilot, an efficient engine that reduces sparse inference overhead through lightweight scoring and cross-step reuse of token selections. On LIBERO with FastWAM-Joint and RoboLab-120 with Cosmos 3 Edge, Sparse-WAM achieves inference speedups of approximately $2.0\times$ and $1.8\times$, respectively, over dense eager inference on an NVIDIA RTX 4090, while largely preserving task performance.

</details>


### [92] [SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38982)
*Jiaheng Hu,Roberto Martin-Martin,Peter Stone,Rocky Duan,Zhenyu Jiang,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SimEX 将模拟实验集成到机器人自动研究中，让 LLM 编码智能体在模拟中开发机器人工具箱，并通过少量真实试验校正模拟器与适配工具箱，从而在极少真实交互下高效获得真实机器人操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将 LLM 编码智能体用于物理世界面临两大问题：直接生成方法（如 Code as Policies）对机器人与物理环境理解不足；在物理世界进行迭代试错（物理自动研究）则实验成本高且存在安全风险。

Method: SimEX 分两阶段。第一阶段：智能体在模拟中进行开放式探测-优化迭代，构建具备鲁棒性和泛化能力的机器人工具箱。第二阶段：通过少量真实试验联合适配工具箱与模拟器；每次试验用于校正模拟器，再用校正后的模拟器诊断失败并筛选候选修复方案。评估涵盖 sim-to-sim 与真实机器人。

Result: 在毛巾折叠、条码扫描和盘子操作等真实操作任务中，SimEX 使编码智能体无需任何演示、仅用 10 分钟真实机器人交互即可高效获得技能。

Conclusion: 模拟不仅是需要高保真复现真实世界的训练数据来源，更可作为“大致正确”的实验室，供编码智能体发展出操控真实机器人所需的知识与流程，是实现物理智能的关键组件。

Abstract: Coding agents powered by large language models (LLMs) have shown remarkable abilities to autonomously reason about and achieve goals in the digital world. However, bringing this success to the physical world remains challenging. On the one hand, direct generation methods (e.g., Code as Policies) often suffer from the LLMs' insufficient understanding of robots and physical environments. On the other hand, iterative trial-and-error tuning in the physical world (e.g., physical autoresearch) induces significant experimental cost and safety concerns. We introduce SimEX: Simulation-Integrated Robotics AutoResearch, an autoresearch framework that tightly integrates simulated experimentation, enabling coding agents to efficiently acquire physical capabilities for controlling real robots. SimEX operates in two stages. First, the agent conducts open-ended probe-and-optimize iterations in simulation, developing a robot toolbox with robust and generalizable capabilities. Second, the agent adapts the toolbox and the simulator together through only a few physical trials: each trial corrects the simulator, and the corrected simulator is used to diagnose failures and screen candidate repairs. We evaluate SimEX extensively in sim-to-sim settings and on physical robots. On challenging real-world manipulation tasks including towel folding, barcode scanning, and plate manipulation, SimEX enables coding agents to efficiently acquire robot skills without any demonstration and with only 10 minutes of real-robot interaction. These results suggest that simulation can be a critical component in achieving physical intelligence, not only as a source of training data that must closely replicate the real world, but also as a roughly correct laboratory where a coding agent develops the knowledge and procedures needed to act on the robot. More details and robot videos at this https URL

</details>


### [93] [Video2SwimFish: An Automated Pipeline for Reconstructing Controllable Fish Models and Biological Locomotion from Real Fish Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38966)
*Hangong Chen,Linfeng Cheng,Tahsin Zaman Jilan,Ian Fuller,Lee Caesar,Jiaye Wu,Yantian Zha*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Video2SwimFish：从真实鱼多视角视频自动构建可控鱼资产的流水线与基准，用于水下具身 AI；方法重建度量尺度变形网格、生成个体化内部关节、提取生物运动流形（BLM）作为低维动作空间并学习个体游泳策略，同时发布 120 条 6 物种数据及资产/策略，并揭示任务成功与运动保真度之间的不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下具身 AI 需要既可控又忠实于真实个体鱼形态与运动的仿真资产和游泳策略；现有工作缺少与真实个体绑定、可系统评估运动保真度的数据与基准。

Method: 输入单条鱼的同步多视角视频；由 VLM 选择规范帧并重建度量尺度可变形网格；通过 VLM actor-critic 循环生成适配个体形态的内部关节结构；从观测中线曲率提取生物运动流形（BLM），形成受真实鱼运动约束的低维动作空间；为每条重建鱼学习个体游泳策略。发布两类配对数据集：120 条个体、6 个物种的同步顶视与前视录像，以及由其派生的可控资产和个体游泳策略。基准评估轨迹跟随、基于视频的无奖励游泳行为迁移，以及模拟 BlueROV 水下机器人捕获资产的下游案例；除任务成功外，还评估轨迹形状、身体曲率和尾拍频率等个体运动保真度。

Result: 任务成功与运动保真度并不必然同步提升：任务完成率最高的方法并不是运动保真度最高的方法。

Conclusion: 忠实复现个体动物的运动仍是社区面临的一个开放挑战。

Abstract: We present Video2SwimFish, an automated pipeline and benchmark for building controllable fish assets from real-fish videos for underwater embodied AI. Given synchronized multi-view videos of an individual fish, the pipeline reconstructs a metrically scaled deformable mesh from a VLM-selected canonical frame, generates internal articulation adapted to that individual's morphology through a VLM actor-critic loop, and extracts a Biological Locomotion Manifold (BLM) from the fish's observed midline curvature. The BLM provides a low-dimensional action space bounded by real-fish motion, enabling an individual swimming policy to be learned for each reconstructed fish. We release two paired datasets: synchronized top- and front-view recordings of 120 individual fish across 6 species, and the controllable assets and individual swimming policies derived from them. Because every asset is tied to the animal it came from, the dataset supports a benchmark that evaluates locomotion learning not only on task success but on fidelity to that individual in trajectory shape, body curvature, and tail-beat frequency, across trajectory following, reward-free swimming behavior transfer from video, and a downstream case study in which a simulated BlueROV underwater robot captures one of the assets. We find that task success and locomotion fidelity do not necessarily improve together: the method achieving the highest task completion is not the method achieving the highest locomotion fidelity, and we identify faithful reproduction of individual animal locomotion as an open challenge for the community. Project website: this https URL .

</details>


### [94] [DrivingBench: Can Vision-Language Models Drive a Toyota Corolla?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38948)
*Aditya Ramabadran,Simon Mahns,Tobias Gessler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DrivingBench 是首个让通用视觉语言模型驾驶真实汽车的基准；模型通过工具接收丰田卡罗拉摄像头画面并直接输出转向与速度指令，在停车场锥桶赛道低速行驶，最终仅 GPT-6 Astra 完成赛道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字基准无法评估前沿模型在真实物理世界中执行日常人类技能（如驾驶）的能力；尤其需测试在推理延迟、持续运动和长时程目标下的观察、行动、监控、恢复与完成能力。

Method: 在丰田卡罗拉上构建低速停车场锥桶赛道，模型通过三个工具获取相机帧并直接控制转向和速度；车辆在模型思考时可能继续运动，新指令会替换当前执行指令，因此推理延迟被纳入任务。用 vendor-native harnesses（Codex、Claude Code、Cursor）对 GPT-6 Astra、Claude Fable 5.1、GPT-5.6 Sol、Grok 4.6 各进行同一对话内最多三次尝试；并分析动作接口设计原则、工具输出格式与任务表述对模型驾驶或拒绝行为的影响。

Result: GPT-6 Astra 是唯一完成赛道的模型，在第二次尝试成功；其他尝试均未通过赛道的 50%。四个模型中有两个在保留上下文的多轮尝试中取得实质性提升。工具输出格式与任务框架共同决定模型是否愿意驾驶或拒绝。

Conclusion: DrivingBench 将真实车辆控制纳入通用 VLM 评估，揭示当前模型在物理长时程驾驶任务中的局限，并公开 harness、提示、赛道地图、含视频与遥测的轨迹以支持可复现研究。

Abstract: Frontier models excel at many digital benchmarks, yet their ability to drive a real car, an everyday human skill, remains largely untested. We present DrivingBench, to our knowledge the first benchmark where general-purpose vision-language models must drive a real car. Through three tools, the models see camera frames from a Toyota Corolla and directly command its steering and velocity around a parking lot cone course at low speeds. The car may continue moving while the model thinks and new commands replace the currently running one, so inference latency is part of the task, testing the models' abilities to observe, act, monitor, recover, and complete a long-horizon objective under such constraints. We benchmark GPT-6 Astra, Claude Fable 5.1, GPT-5.6 Sol, and Grok 4.6 in vendor-native harnesses (Codex, Claude Code, Cursor) with up to three attempts each in one conversation; Astra is the only model to finish the course, on its second attempt, with no other attempt passing 50% of the course. Two of the four models improved materially across attempts with retained context. We also detail the design principles behind our action interface, and show how the tool output format and the framing of the task combined to determine whether models would drive at all or refuse. We release our harness, prompts, course map, and traces with video and telemetry for reproducibility.

</details>


### [95] [Local-Minimum Escaper: Programmatic Subgoal Generation for Robust Navigation in Unknown Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38928)
*Yin Gu,Xinming Zhang,Shanze Wang,Siwei Cheng,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 LME（Local-Minimum Escaper），一种仅依赖局部观测、基于可解释启发式子目标生成与推理的程序化分层框架，用于无地图导航中显式逃离局部极小值，无需额外训练，并可兼容不同局部规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 未知且部分可观测环境中的无地图导航仍具挑战，局部极小值会使机器人无法朝目标前进；现有局部导航方法缺乏显式逃逸机制，而深度强化学习方法通常只能通过奖励设计和策略优化隐式学习恢复行为。

Method: LME 是一个程序化分层框架，仅使用局部观测，通过可解释启发式准则选择候选子目标，准则同时考虑周围障碍物几何与候选位置安全性；随后由局部规划器生成低级运动指令。该设计统一处理有/无局部极小值的环境，独立于底层局部规划器，且无需额外训练。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的大量实验表明，LME 具有鲁棒的导航性能，并能泛化到具有挑战性的未见场景；生成的子目标可引导不同局部规划器，显著提升其逃离局部极小值的能力；在差速驱动和四足机器人上的成功部署进一步验证了其实用性与通用性。

Conclusion: LME 提供了一种显式、通用、可解释且无需训练的局部极小值逃逸方案，能够提升无地图导航的鲁棒性，并具备跨局部规划器和跨机器人平台的适用性。

Abstract: Mapless navigation in unknown and partially observable environments remains challenging for mobile robots, particularly when local minima prevent the robot from making progress toward its goal. Existing local navigation methods often lack an explicit mechanism for escaping such situations, while deep reinforcement learning (DRL) approaches typically learn recovery behaviors implicitly through reward design and policy optimization. In this work, we propose \textbf{LME} (Local-Minimum Escaper), a programmatic hierarchical framework that explicitly generates and reasons subgoals to guide robots out of local-minimum regions. LME operates solely on local observations and selects candidate subgoals using interpretable heuristic criteria that account for both surrounding obstacle geometry and candidate-location safety. A local planner then generates low-level motion commands toward the selected subgoal. This design enables LME to handle environments both with and without local minima within a unified framework, while remaining independent of the underlying local planner and requiring no additional training. Extensive experiments in simulated and real-world environments demonstrate that LME provides robust navigation performance and generalizes to challenging unseen scenarios. Furthermore, the generated subgoals can be used to guide different local planners, substantially improving their ability to escape local minima. Successful deployments on both differential-drive and quadruped robots further demonstrate the practical applicability and generality of the proposed framework.

</details>


### [96] [EmbodiRSI: Recursive Self-Improvement for Data-Efficient Robot Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38905)
*Haoran Lang,Haotao Lu,Shiyu Sang,Haoyang Luo,Guo Chen,Qun Li,Jingyi Yu,Ye Shi,Jingya Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EmbodiRSI 提出一种 real-to-sim-to-real 的 agentic 递归自我改进系统，利用部署场景构建任务特定仿真，通过策略执行反馈驱动纠错与自适应数据采集，在少量真实数据下高效提升机器人操作策略的仿真与真实环境成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作策略适应新任务和新环境高度依赖数据，而进一步改进所需的数据取决于策略当前能力与失败模式，因此需要一种反馈驱动、数据高效的自适应改进方法。

Method: EmbodiRSI 从目标部署场景构建任务特定仿真，作为低成本迭代训练与迁移回物理世界的环境，并利用策略执行反馈指导后续经验获取和策略更新。其核心包含两个互补机制：Collaborative Error Correction 从策略到达状态生成 agent 辅助的纠正轨迹；Adaptive Data Collection 将专家演示生成导向当前策略的弱点。该仿真工作区可复用，用于策略预热、可重复评估、失败诊断和跨 RSI 轮次的目标数据生成。

Result: 在 3 个桌面环境和 14 个子任务中，EmbodiRSI 经两轮 RSI 更新将场景平衡的仿真自主成功率从 50.4% 提升至 83.5%。每个子任务仅使用 400 条自适应仿真轨迹和 10 条真实世界精调轨迹，即可达到 83.1% 的场景平衡真实世界自主成功率，优于每个子任务使用 200 条真实世界演示适配所达到的 75.0%。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在部署特定仿真中进行反馈驱动的递归改进，能够实现具身策略向物理环境的数据高效适应。

Abstract: Adapting robot manipulation policies to new tasks and environments remains highly data-intensive, while the data needed for further improvement depends on the policy's current capabilities and failure modes. We introduce EmbodiRSI, an agentic system for recursive self-improvement (RSI) in a real-to-sim-to-real setting, where task-specific simulations are constructed from target deployment scenarios and used as low-cost environments for iterative policy improvement before transfer back to the physical world. EmbodiRSI uses policy execution feedback to guide subsequent experience acquisition and policy updates. Two complementary mechanisms close this loop: Collaborative Error Correction generates agent-assisted corrective trajectories from policy-reached states, while Adaptive Data Collection directs expert demonstration generation toward the current policy's weaknesses. The task-specific simulation serves as a reusable workspace for policy warm-up, repeatable evaluation, failure diagnosis, and targeted data generation across successive RSI rounds. Across three tabletop environments and 14 subtasks, EmbodiRSI increases scene-balanced autonomous simulation success from 50.4% to 83.5% over two RSI updates. With 400 adaptive simulated trajectories and only ten real-world refinement trajectories per subtask, EmbodiRSI achieves 83.1% scene-balanced autonomous real-world success, compared with 75.0% for adaptation using 200 real-world demonstrations per subtask. These results demonstrate that feedback-driven recursive improvement in deployment-specific simulations can enable data-efficient adaptation of embodied policies to physical environments.

</details>


### [97] [PRICE the Action Chunks: Physical Relational Credit Assignment for Embodied Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38890)
*Yangang Zou,Jiajun Lu,Weitao Zhou,Haibao Yu,Bozhou Zhang,Jiawei Wang,Honglong Tian,Minglei Li,Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出PRICE，在不依赖辅助评估器的情况下，仅利用终端成败结果，通过跨轨迹物理对应关系为动作块分配信用，从而改进VLA策略的RL后训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果的强化学习在post-train视觉-语言-动作策略时，将同一轨迹级优势赋给每个动作块；失败回合会错误惩罚有用早期动作。已有细粒度反馈方法通常需学习评估器、任务特定监督或额外模型训练。

Method: PRICE利用到达对应物理情形的rollout互为参考，其终端结果可作为评估局部进展的证据。方法包含：(i) 物理关系图，在对应动作块边界汇聚当前与历史结果以估计成功潜能；(ii) 置信度门控信用分配，利用潜能变化细化轨迹级监督。理论分析将oracle潜能变化与终端成功目标关联，并为结果无关证据池给出有限样本方向性界。

Result: 独立延续测试表明PRICE保留的信用与局部进展一致；在LIBERO、RoboTwin 2.0和真实机器人上，相比基于结果的基线，任务成功率更高且学习更快。

Conclusion: 跨轨迹物理关系可从终端结果中推断动作块级信用，为具身RL提供无需辅助评估器的有效后训练范式。

Abstract: Outcome-based reinforcement learning (RL) post-trains vision--language--action policies using terminal success signals, but assigns the same trajectory-level advantage to every action chunk. A failed episode can thus penalize useful early actions as if they caused the failure. Existing approaches seek finer-grained feedback through learned evaluators, adding task-specific supervision or additional model training. We explore, for the first time to our knowledge, whether physical relations across trajectories can provide action-chunk credit in embodied RL from terminal outcomes alone, without an auxiliary evaluator. The key insight is that rollouts reaching corresponding physical situations can serve as references for one another: their terminal outcomes provide evidence for assessing local progress. We introduce Physical Relations for Inferring Credit from Episodes(PRICE), with two components: (i) a physical relational graph that pools current and historical outcomes at corresponding chunk boundaries to estimate success potentials; and (ii) confidence-gated credit assignment that uses changes in these potentials to refine trajectory-level supervision. Our analysis connects oracle potential changes to the terminal-success objective and provides a finite-sample directional bound for outcome-independent evidence pools. Independent continuation tests show that PRICE's retained credits align with local progress, while experiments on LIBERO, RoboTwin 2.0, and real robots demonstrate improved task success over outcome-based baselines and faster learning.

</details>


### [98] [Benchmarking and Enhancing Skill-Level Memory for Partially Observable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38886)
*Yansong Shi,Jiange Yang,Xijie Yang,Shaowei Zhang,Yuhan Zhu,Tao Lu,Limin Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出部分可观测下机器人操作记忆基准 HIDE 与记忆增强框架 SEEK，评估现有策略在隐藏任务状态下的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人策略虽日益多样且可泛化，但可靠执行常依赖当前观测无法确定的隐藏任务状态，因此交互历史至关重要；现有评估缺乏对操作记忆和部分可观测性的系统基准。

Method: 提出 HIDE 基准，包含15个任务，覆盖重复计数、历史状态回忆和执行进度跟踪，具有随机初始配置及相似观测需依历史采取不同动作的决策点。提出 SEEK 框架，结合三种互补记忆机制以保留历史证据并跟踪执行状态。评估现有策略及记忆增强在仿真与真实实验中的表现。

Result: 现有策略在 HIDE 上表现显著受限；记忆增强提升仿真与真实任务成功率；单一机制对部分任务有益但可能损害其他任务；三种机制组合在评估配置中取得最高平均成功率。

Conclusion: 维持隐藏任务状态的内部表征很重要，记忆设计需匹配任务特定信息需求，组合互补记忆机制可提升部分可观测操作可靠性。

Abstract: Recent advances in robot learning have enabled manipulation policies to perform increasingly diverse tasks and generalize across environments. However, reliable execution often depends on hidden task states that cannot be determined from current observations alone, making interaction history essential. We introduce $HIDE$, a benchmark for evaluating manipulation memory under partial observability. HIDE comprises 15 tasks covering repetition counting, historical-state recall, and execution-progress tracking, with randomized initial configurations and decision points where similar observations require different actions depending on prior events. We further propose $SEEK$, a framework combining three complementary memory mechanisms to retain historical evidence and track execution state. Evaluations reveal substantial limitations in existing policies on HIDE, while memory augmentation improves task success in both simulation and real-world experiments. Individual mechanisms benefit some tasks but can degrade others; their combination achieves the highest average success rate on HIDE among the evaluated configurations. These findings highlight the importance of maintaining internal representations of hidden task states and matching memory design to task-specific information requirements.

</details>


### [99] [DODGER: Safety-Guided Reinforcement Learning for Robot Navigation Among Dynamic Obstacles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38873)
*Sanghyuk Park,Kwanwoo Lee,Taekyung Kim,Seohyeon Lim,Yisoo Lee*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DODGER，一种安全引导的强化学习框架：训练时直接执行策略动作，同时利用CBF滤波参考和约束违反来塑造避碰策略，从而在多个动态障碍物中实现安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类中心环境中运行的机器人必须安全避开多个动态障碍物。CBF可提供安全过滤，但现有基于CBF的RL方法在训练中仅执行安全过滤后的动作，会限制策略探索，尤其在安全依赖相对运动的动态场景中问题更突出。

Method: DODGER在训练rollout中直接执行策略生成的动作，而非仅执行CBF过滤后的动作；同时使用CBF过滤后的参考动作和约束违反信号来引导策略学习避碰行为。评估包括Dubins-car安全性分析，以及基于LiDAR感知的全阶人形仿真和真实世界人形实验，且运行时无需安全滤波器。

Result: 通过Dubins-car安全性分析验证了方法，并在全阶人形仿真和真实人形实验中实现了多个动态障碍物下的目标导向导航，运行时未使用安全滤波器。

Conclusion: DODGER表明可在训练中保持探索的同时，借助CBF信息塑造策略，从而在动态环境中实现无需运行时安全过滤器的安全导航。

Abstract: Robots operating in human-centered environments must safely navigate among multiple dynamic obstacles to avoid collisions with people and surrounding infrastructure. Control barrier functions (CBFs) provide an effective mechanism for safety filtering, and recent CBF-based reinforcement learning (RL) methods embed such safety information into learned policies. However, executing only safety-filtered actions during training can restrict policy exploration, a limitation that becomes particularly consequential in dynamic scenes where safety depends on relative robot-obstacle motion. We propose DODGER, a safety-guided RL framework that directly executes policy-generated actions to drive training rollouts while using CBF-filtered references and constraint violations to shape the policy toward collision-avoidance behavior. We evaluate DODGER through a Dubins-car safety analysis and demonstrate goal-directed navigation among multiple dynamic obstacles in full-order humanoid simulation and real-world humanoid experiments using LiDAR-based perception, without a runtime safety filter.

</details>


### [100] [Efficient Multi-Modal Planning with Reward-Guided Preference Optimization for Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38862)
*Chenglin Chen,Lujia Wang,Xinhu Zheng,Jun Ma,Haoang Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出EMPlan，一种基于奖励引导微调的高效多模态轨迹规划方法，通过稀疏锚点与偏移精修架构及两阶段训练，在NAVSIM上实现规划精度与效率的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶轨迹规划方法在计算效率与安全性保障上存在不足：模仿学习易受因果混淆影响；规则评分方法计算开销大且目标错位；偏好方法依赖严格成对标注，限制数据利用率。

Method: 设计混合架构，结合稀疏锚点与偏移精修模块进行高效多模态轨迹预测；稀疏锚点低延迟生成粗候选轨迹，偏移模块进一步精修以提高精度。采用预训练与奖励引导微调两阶段训练范式，微调阶段利用规则奖励信号和无配对偏好监督，使预训练策略偏向更安全的轨迹选择，且不增加额外推理成本。

Result: 在非反应式NAVSIM基准上评估，EMPlan在规划精度与效率之间取得良好平衡，并在实时约束下展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: EMPlan通过奖励引导微调与稀疏锚点-偏移精修架构，在不增加推理成本的前提下提升多模态轨迹规划的安全性与效率，为自动驾驶实时规划提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Safe and efficient trajectory planning is essential in autonomous driving. However, existing end-to-end approaches often fall short in both computational efficiency and safety guarantees. Methods based on imitation learning suffer from causal confusion, while rule-based scoring approaches often incur heavy computational overhead and suffer from objective misalignment. Additionally, preference-based methods rely on strict pairwise annotations, limiting data utilization. To overcome these limitations, we propose EMPlan, an efficient multi-modal trajectory planning method powered by reward-guided fine-tuning. We design a hybrid architecture that combines sparse anchors with an offset refinement module for efficient multi-modal trajectory prediction. Sparse anchors provide coarse trajectory candidates with low latency, which are subsequently refined by the offset module for higher prediction accuracy. To enhance safety without incurring additional inference costs, we adopt a two-stage training paradigm consisting of pretraining and reward-guided fine-tuning. During fine-tuning, we leverage rule-based reward signals and unpaired preference supervision to refine the pretrained policy toward safer trajectory selection. We evaluate EMPlan on the non-reactive NAVSIM benchmark, where it strikes a favorable balance between planning accuracy and efficiency, demonstrating superior performance under real-time constraints.

</details>


### [101] [Plan-Conditioned Imitation for Robust Object Retrieval under Self-Occlusion in Dense Clutter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38857)
*Kowndinya Boyalakuntla,Ajinkya Pawar,Abdeslam Boularias,Jingjin Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 TRACE：一种面向自遮挡下密集杂乱取物的计划条件模仿框架。它用单次无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生，由特权教师生成固定名义 rollout，再训练循环学生结合局部 rollout 上下文、部分物体观测和本体感觉来纠偏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在密集杂乱环境中取物通常需要重排，但机械臂移动时会遮挡物体；若反复撤回机械臂以恢复可见性，会打断执行流程并降低效率。因此需要一种在自遮挡下仍能稳定执行取物、且无需频繁撤回的策略。

Method: TRACE 先用单次无遮挡观测初始化数字孪生，并让特权教师生成固定的名义 rollout。循环学生融合局部 rollout 上下文、部分物体观测与本体感觉，选择动作以纠正相对预测的偏差。训练上先用行为克隆初始化学生，再用 DAgger 在 student-visited states 上以教师标签精炼。执行期间 rollout 保持固定，部署学生无需在线教师查询，也无需在 pushing 过程中额外仿真 rollout。

Result: 在 511 个仿真测试场景中，TRACE 成功率为 90.7%，高于名义回放的 43.4%，接近特权闭环教师的 96.7%。在匹配 26,373 标签预算下，student-state supervision 达到 87.8%，优于 expert-only cloning 的 66.7%。在 UR5e 真机上，TRACE 成功率为 90.0%，特权闭环教师为 95.0%，但总执行时间从 192.7 s 降至 67.3 s；TRACE 避免了教师在 pushing 中每次试验平均 16.8 次与感知相关的机械臂撤回，同时保留用于可抓取性评估的最终撤回。

Conclusion: TRACE 能在自遮挡下高效完成密集杂乱取物，性能接近特权闭环教师，并显著优于名义回放与仅专家克隆。它减少执行时间和感知相关撤回次数，且部署时无需在线教师或额外仿真 rollout。代码与数据将发布。

Abstract: Retrieving objects from dense clutter requires rearrangement during which the manipulator can occlude objects while moving them. Repeated arm withdrawals to restore visibility interrupt execution. We introduce TRACE, a plan-conditioned imitation framework for retrieval under self-occlusion. A single unoccluded observation initializes a digital twin, where a privileged teacher generates a fixed nominal rollout. A recurrent student combines local rollout context, partial object observations, and proprioception to select actions that can correct deviations from the prediction. Behavior cloning initializes the student; DAgger refines it with teacher labels on student-visited states. The rollout remains fixed throughout execution, so the deployed student needs neither online teacher queries nor additional simulator rollouts during pushing. On 511 simulation test scenes, TRACE achieves 90.7% success versus 43.4% for nominal replay and 96.7% for the privileged closed-loop teacher. At a matched 26,373-label budget, student-state supervision achieves 87.8% versus 66.7% for expert-only cloning, demonstrating gains beyond additional labels. On a UR5e, TRACE achieves 90.0% success versus 95.0% for the closed-loop teacher, while reducing total execution time from 192.7 s to 67.3 s. It avoids the teacher's 16.8 sensing-related arm retractions per trial during pushing, retaining a final withdrawal for graspability evaluation. Code and data will be released at: this https URL .

</details>


### [102] [Online Evolution Strategy for Flow-Matching VLA Policies via Self-Supervised Trajectory Distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38855)
*Gongxin Yao,Yongsheng Zhao,Jiayin Deng,Deng Liang,Han Gao,Lei Zhao,Baoping Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Online-ES，一种基于进化策略的 Flow Matching VLA 在线自适应框架，通过在动作轨迹空间进行演化探索并利用交互反馈优化策略分布，在无需价值模型和优势计算的情况下实现与强化微调相当的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于 Flow Matching 的 VLA 模型虽能学习条件动作轨迹分布，但该分布常不理想：成功与失败行为共存，且大量概率质量落在不利区域。现有方法未充分解决分布质量问题，因此需要一种能通过在线交互反馈直接优化轨迹分布的自适应方法。
