Method: Online-ES 不剪枝潜在噪声空间，而是在动作轨迹空间中进行进化探索：Flow Matching 生成的多样轨迹作为候选解，通过扰动采样轨迹并评估执行结果，构造自监督 MSE 目标，将轨迹空间的进化方向迁移到模型参数空间。理论上证明该目标是最优进化方向的无偏估计；同时将失败经验作为负反馈正则化进化方向，使策略远离已探索的失败区域。

Result: 在仿真和真实环境中的实验表明，Online-ES 能实现与强化微调相当的政策改进，且无需学习价值模型或计算优势函数。

Conclusion: Online-ES 通过进化策略有效在线自适应 Flow Matching VLA 模型，在动作轨迹空间利用成功与失败反馈优化策略分布，为机器人操作中的 VLA 策略改进提供了一种无需价值模型的高效方法。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models based on generative frameworks, such as Flow Matching, have recently achieved impressive performance in robotic manipulation. Unlike deterministic policies, Flow Matching enables VLA models to learn conditional action trajectory distributions, where latent noise vectors induce different actions under the same task scenario. However, we observe that these distributions are often ill-formed, with successful and failed behaviors coexisting while considerable probability mass remains in unfavorable regions. To this end, we propose Online-ES, an online adaptation framework for Flow Matching VLAs based on Evolution Strategy (ES), which refines the learned action trajectory distribution through interaction feedback. Instead of pruning the latent noise space, our method performs evolutionary exploration directly in the action trajectory space, where diverse trajectories generated by Flow Matching provide candidate solutions for adaptation. By perturbing sampled trajectories and evaluating their execution outcomes, we derive a self-supervised MSE objective that transfers the evolution direction from trajectory space into model parameter space. Mathematically, we prove that the proposed objective provides an unbiased estimator of the optimal evolution direction. Moreover, we also incorporate failure experiences as negative feedback to regularize the evolution direction, steering the policy away from previously explored failure regions. Experiments in both simulation and real-world environments demonstrate that Online-ES achieves policy improvement comparable to reinforcement fine-tuning, without learning a value model or computing advantages.

</details>


### [103] [Locomotion-Grounded Humanoid Soccer: Task-Gated Reinforcement Learning of a Multi-Directional Kicking Library](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38852)
*Abu Hanif Muhammad Syarubany,Jaehyun Jang,Hwanhee Kim,Kyuwon Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作反转人形足球技能栈：先训练通用、命令条件化的全身运动策略作为基座，再以任务门控层叠加N个动作引导的踢球技能；在29自由度Unitree G1上实现7个踢球技能，覆盖259.5°标称瞄准方向，并完成硬件验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有系统以运动跟踪为基座，仅按接近球时诱导的狭窄、确定性命令分布训练locomotion，导致运动能力本身从未被独立评估；同时技能组合与击球后稳定多依赖脚本化设计，泛化性和可组合性受限。

Method: 先训练通用、命令条件化的locomotion策略作为基座；在其上添加N个motion-guided踢球技能作为task-gated层。每个技能从同一可命令状态出发并返回该状态，使locomotion成为组合中心，将技能切换复杂度从O(N^2)降至O(N)，击球后稳定交还给已训练控制器。基于29-DoF Unitree G1重定向7个踢球技能，覆盖259.5°标称瞄准方向，包括前向参考无法表达的侧向、后向和弱脚踢球。

Result: 报告了射击精度，以及在完整技能库下的命令跟踪、地形适应和推力恢复结果，该评估维度是现有人形足球系统未报告的。技能库在硬件上针对前向、侧向、后向和命令化接近方式完成验证。

Conclusion: 以命令条件化locomotion为基座并在其上组合任务门控踢球技能，可扩展可达步态空间、简化技能组合、由控制器自主完成击球后稳定，并在真实人形机器人上实现多方向踢球能力。

Abstract: Recent humanoid soccer systems make motion tracking the substrate and derive locomotion from it, typically by steering a motion-reference anchor toward the ball. This yields strong shooting results, but locomotion is trained only on the narrow, deterministic command distribution ball approach induces, never evaluated as a capability in its own right. We invert the stack: a general, command-conditioned locomotion policy is trained first as the substrate, and N motion-guided kicking skills are added on top as task-gated layers, so the reachable gait space is set by the locomotion curriculum rather than any reference clip. Because every skill starts from and returns to this same commandable state, locomotion also becomes a composition hub (O(N) transitions rather than O(N^2)), and post-strike stabilisation is handed back to the trained controller rather than scripted per clip. We instantiate this on a 29-DoF Unitree G1 with seven retargeted kicking skills spanning 259.5 degrees of nominal aim direction, including lateral, rearward and weak-foot strikes a single forward-facing reference cannot express, and report shooting accuracy alongside command-tracking, terrain and push-recovery results with the full skill library attached, an axis prior humanoid soccer systems do not report. The library is validated on hardware across forward, lateral, rearward and commanded approaches.

</details>


### [104] [Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS): Scaling Coral Recruit Monitoring Through Robotics and Computer Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38846)
*Dorian Tsai,Scarlett Raine,Emilio Olivastri,Riki Lamont,Andrew Lui,Timothy Morris,Joshua Esplin,Christopher A. Brunner,F. Mikaela Nordborg,Reginald Wardleworth,Garima Samvedi,Karen Jackel,Matthew Dunbabin,Tobias Fischer,Andrea Severati*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 为突破珊瑚养殖中大规模宏观幼体监测瓶颈，提出结合机器人成像与计算机视觉的CGRAS，实现多物种珊瑚自动检测、计数与健康评估，并在实际设施中验证其显著降低监测人力与时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候变化严重威胁珊瑚礁，规模化珊瑚礁修复依赖珊瑚养殖等大规模幼体生产；但养殖设施中常有数万枚直径0.5–2毫米的宏观幼体需高频一致监测，人工评估成本过高，成为限制规模化扩繁与部署的瓶颈。

Method: 提出珊瑚养成机器人评估系统CGRAS，集成机器人成像与计算机视觉，自动采集标准化附着板图像，执行多物种珊瑚检测、计数和健康评估，并自动提取生长、存活和空间分布指标，为运营者优化生产、养成和部署流程提供及时反馈。

Result: 在大型珊瑚养殖设施中对标准化珊瑚沉降板进行验证，相较人工监测将时间和劳动力成本降低9.6倍，并对Acropora kenti珊瑚达到相对专家计数的96.4%一致性。

Conclusion: CGRAS表明机器人成像与计算机视觉可自动化、高通量监测珊瑚养殖幼体，缓解规模化修复中的监测瓶颈，为数据驱动的珊瑚生产与礁体修复流程优化提供可行技术路径。

Abstract: Climate change is the largest threat to coral reefs, with increasing global impacts accelerating the need for scalable reef restoration technologies. Large-scale reef restoration depends on the mass production of corals, such as through coral aquaculture. Coral seeding with recruits grown in aquaculture facilities is a feasible restoration approach, but effective production requires consistent, high-frequency monitoring of tens of thousands of macroscopic (0.5-2mm diameter) recruits, making conventional manual assessment prohibitively labor-intensive. To address this monitoring bottleneck, we introduce the Coral Grow-out Robotic Assessment System (CGRAS) which combines robotic imaging and computer vision to automate data acquisition, perform multi-species detection and counting of corals, and evaluate coral health. CGRAS automatically extracts coral growth, survival and spatial distribution metrics, with the aim of providing timely feedback to operators for optimizing production, grow-out and deployment workflow processes. We demonstrate CGRAS in a large aquaculture facility on standardized coral settlement tiles, reducing the time and labor costs by a factor of 9.6 as compared to manual monitoring, whilst achieving 96.4% agreement for Acropora kenti corals relative to expert counts.

</details>


### [105] [PhaseSync-Exo: Human Clock Anchored Reference Adaptation for Dynamic Gait Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38824)
*Kaijie Qi,Yuehan Wang,Kaiming Xu,Chong Li,Jiakuo Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PhaseSync-Exo，一种面向人机协同行走的外骨骼控制框架，通过双 IMU 步态重建、幅频解耦重定向、课程训练循环控制与人类时钟锚定适配器（HCA），在仿真中实现连续相位获取与持续人机时间对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人机感知的外骨骼行走需从有限传感重建步态、在动态约束下跟踪多样运动，并保持人类自身时序；现有方法易出现相位漂移、初始相位不一致或突变重置，破坏连续性与前进性。

Method: 方法包括：1）双 IMU CNN-Transformer 步态重建；2）因子化幅度-步频重定向，配合课程训练循环控制策略；3）人类时钟锚定适配器 HCA，将人类时钟吸引与机器人相对反馈结合，调整参考速率并保持前进与连续性。通过两组动态对比，在冻结策略且无需重训练下隔离 HCA 的时序收益。

Result: 重建模块在留出记录上 MAE 为 3.24°；冻结跟踪策略完成 356/357 组幅频试验。与机器人相对校正相比，HCA 将人类时钟相位 MAE 从 95.55° 降至 10.99°；初始人机相位不一致时，较固定时钟延续将相位 MAE 从 72.02° 降至 13.90°；较立即相位重置，HCA 将瞬态参考跟踪髋关节 RMSE 降低 22.7%，且无参考跳变。420 次时序 rollout 均未跌倒。

Conclusion: 仿真结果表明，HCA 支持连续相位获取并持续对齐独立人类时钟，为人机协同外骨骼行走中的时序保持提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Human-aware exoskeleton walking requires reconstructing gait, tracking diverse motions under dynamic constraints, and preserving human timing. We present PhaseSync-Exo, which combines two-IMU CNN-Transformer reconstruction, factorized amplitude-cadence retargeting with curriculum-trained recurrent control, and a human-clock-anchored adapter (HCA). HCA combines human-clock attraction with robot-relative feedback to adjust reference rate while preserving forward progression and continuity. The reconstruction module achieves a mean absolute error (MAE) of 3.24 degrees on a held-out recording, and the frozen tracking policy completes 356 of 357 amplitude-frequency trials. Two complementary dynamic comparisons isolate HCA's timing benefit without retraining. Against robot-relative correction, HCA reduces human-clock phase MAE from 95.55 degrees to 10.99 degrees, limiting reference drift. When human and delivered phases initially differ, it reduces phase MAE from 72.02 degrees to 13.90 degrees versus fixed-clock continuation. Compared with immediate phase reset, HCA reduces transient reference-tracking hip RMSE by 22.7% without reference jumps. All 420 timing rollouts complete without falls. These simulation results support continuous phase acquisition and sustained alignment to an independent human clock.

</details>


### [106] [HALO: Heterogeneous Allocation Via Localized Observations for the Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38760)
*Andrew Meighan,Hyungsub Kim,Or Dantsker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出HALO，一种面向可扩展机器人车队的混合式车辆路径问题求解方法，将VRP分解为分配与路由，支持去中心化、部分可观测环境下的实时在线决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有车队控制算法多解决集中式路由问题，最多约1000个任务且依赖受控环境，未考虑去中心化车队中有限的观测与通信范围，难以实际部署。

Method: HALO将VRP拆分为分配和路由两部分，为机器人提供机载实时解；分配阶段采用异构图神经网络框架，并通过独特消息传递层显式分离空间分布学习与任务-机器人兼容性学习。

Result: 在部分可观测在线VRP变体中，HALO显著优于启发式基线，且解质量接近全知离线HALO；在传统静态单仓库VRP上，较严格针对该变体优化的SOTA架构最高提升14.06%；所有测试中执行时间最快。

Conclusion: HALO在保持实时性和解质量方面表现突出，具备大规模、实时机器人车队部署潜力。

Abstract: Scalable robotic fleets have become increasingly popular for various applications such as package delivery, warehouse management, and military operations. Prior fleet control algorithms solve centralized routing problems with up to $1{,}000$ tasks in controlled environments, yet they fail to consider realistic constraints such as limited observation and communication ranges typical of decentralized fleets. Thus, deploying existing fleet control algorithms into real-world settings is currently infeasible. To tackle this, we propose Heterogeneous Allocation via Localized Observations (HALO) to solve the Vehicle Routing Problem (VRP). HALO is a hybrid method that splits the VRP into allocation and routing portions to provide onboard, real-time solutions to robots in dynamic environments. During the allocation phase, HALO utilizes a heterogeneous graph neural network framework with unique message passing layers to explicitly separate the learning of spatial distributions and task-to-robot compatibility. Evaluation results on a partially observable, online variant of the VRP show HALO significantly outperforms the heuristic baseline while maintaining similar solution quality to an all-knowing offline variant of HALO. While HALO is explicitly designed for partially observable environments, it imposes no strict upper bound on the observation space allowing us to test HALO on the traditional static, single-depot VRP. Here, HALO outperforms state-of-the-art architectures strictly optimized for the static variant of the VRP by up to $14.06\%$. Throughout all testing, this framework maintains the quickest execution times which emphasizes its potential for large-scale, real-time deployment.

</details>


### [107] [AIfred: Augmented Learning through Functional Robotic Embodiment at the Desk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38737)
*Gregorio Orlando,Milan Groshev,Eduardo Castelló Ferrer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出桌面机械臂投影系统AIfred，将AI生成指导直接投射到手写工作区，在数学学习迁移和绘画任务上优于屏幕式ChatGPT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 桌面学习和创意活动依赖手写，但现有生成式AI通过独立屏幕提供指导，造成思考空间与辅助空间分离。

Method: 设计AIfred：末端执行器带投影仪的桌面机械臂，结合工作区感知、上下文感知内容生成和机器人投影，支持数学作业、图像生成和绘画。用户研究n=36，对比笔记本电脑上的ChatGPT（GPT-5.6 Luna）。另请独立艺术设计教授评审绘画。

Result: 数学作业有辅助时两者相当（6.7 vs 7.3/10, p=.41）；撤去辅助后AIfred短时学习迁移提升60%（7.0 vs 4.4/10, p=.003）。教授在36例中33例认为AIfred绘画更优。

Conclusion: 空间共置的AI辅助对指导与工作共享同一空间框架的任务更有益。

Abstract: Desk-based learning and creative activities benefit from handwritten engagement. However, current generative AI tools deliver guidance through a separate screen, creating a gap between where users think and where assistance appears. To address this, in this work we design AIfred, a desk-based robotic arm with a projector mounted at the end-effector that places AI-generated guidance alongside handwritten work. AIfred combines workspace perception, context-aware content generation, and robot-mediated projection to support math assignments, image generation, and drawing tasks. In a user study (n = 36), we compared AIfred against ChatGPT (GPT-5.6 Luna) running on a laptop. Both tools performed comparably while assistance was available during the math assignment (6.7 vs. 7.3/10, p = .41), but AIfred improved short-term learning transfer by 60% once assistance was withdrawn (7.0 vs. 4.4/10, p = .003). In addition, independent art and design professors ranked drawings produced with AIfred better in 33 of 36 cases. Our findings indicate that spatially co-located AI assistance benefits tasks whose guidance shares a spatial frame with the work.

</details>


### [108] [Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Learning for Forward-Consistent Inverse Flapping-Wing Aerodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38730)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出BCS-GRU，将循环时序预测作为主表示，把谱信息限制为受控的通道自适应残差校正，并用前向一致性目标提升扑翼气动逆建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼气动力与力矩依赖瞬时翅膀构型和此前运动历史，从期望气动轨迹反求合适运动学是困难逆问题；现有序列模型能建模时序依赖，谱方法可利用周期结构，但无约束频域增强会干扰时序表示，并造成跨运动学变量与预测时域的不一致修正。

Method: 提出有界通道自适应谱残差门控循环单元（BCS-GRU）：保留循环时序预测作为主表示，仅将谱信息用于输出特定的受控校正；引入因果对齐的前向一致性目标，通过单独训练且冻结的气动代理模型评估预测运动学。

Result: 在统一episode级协议下，BCS-GRU在逆预测上优于循环与自适应谱基线，且长预测时域收益更大；前向一致微调进一步提升基于代理模型的气动一致性，同时保持平均运动学精度。

Conclusion: 受控谱校正与前向一致学习为历史感知的扑翼气动逆建模提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Flapping-wing vehicles regulate aerodynamic forces and moments through coordinated variations in stroke, deviation, and pitch motion. Because the resulting loads depend on both the instantaneous wing configuration and its preceding motion history, recovering suitable wing kinematics from a desired aerodynamic trajectory is a challenging inverse problem. Existing sequence models capture temporal dependencies, while spectral methods can exploit the periodic structure of flapping motion. However, unrestricted frequency-domain augmentation may interfere with temporal representations and produce inconsistent corrections across kinematic variables and prediction horizons. We propose the Bounded Channel-Adaptive Spectral Residual Gated Recurrent Unit (BCS-GRU), which retains recurrent temporal prediction as its primary representation and restricts spectral information to a controlled output-specific correction. We further introduce a causally aligned forward-consistency objective that evaluates predicted kinematics through a separately trained and frozen aerodynamic surrogate. Experiments under a unified episode-level protocol show that BCS-GRU improves inverse prediction over recurrent and adaptive-spectral baselines, with greater benefits at longer prediction horizons. Forward-consistent fine-tuning further improves surrogate-based aerodynamic consistency while maintaining mean kinematic accuracy. These results demonstrate that controlled spectral correction and forward-consistent learning provide an effective framework for history-aware inverse modelling of flapping-wing aerodynamics.

</details>


### [109] [FlapKAD: A Simulation Dataset of Coupled Wing Kinematics and Aerodynamic Dynamics for Flapping-Wing Aerial Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38719)
*Haichuan Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FlapKAD——一个面向扑翼飞行器的时序对齐仿真数据集与双向序列预测基准，用于研究机翼运动学、气动响应和飞行状态之间的耦合动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扑翼飞行器实验与建模受限于缺乏大规模、时间对齐的记录，难以同步获取机翼运动学、气动响应和飞行状态；现有数据集规模有限，并受测量噪声以及高频扑动下快速变化机翼运动与动态响应之间的时间错位影响。

Method: 构建FlapKAD：一个按飞行片段组织的仿真数据集，包含2000个刚性翼飞行片段和720152个有效时间步，在同一时钟下同步记录双侧机翼运动学、气动响应和飞行状态；基于这些时间对齐片段建立统一的双向序列预测基准，其中正向任务根据已实现的扑动角和扭转角历史预测未来垂直力系数与机体垂直速度，反向任务则根据响应历史重建未来扑动角和扭转角轨迹；在多个预测时域上对8种代表性时间序列架构进行基准测试。

Result: 基准测试显示模型行为具有方向依赖性和时域依赖性；扭转角的重建误差系统性地高于扑动角；增加架构复杂度并未带来一致优势。

Conclusion: FlapKAD为研究扑翼飞行器中机翼运动学、气动响应与飞行状态的耦合动力学提供了可复现的数据集和基准。

Abstract: Experimental investigation and modeling of flapping-wing aerial vehicles are limited by the scarcity of large-scale records that temporally align wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states. Existing datasets are often limited in scale and affected by measurement noise and temporal misalignment between rapidly varying wing motion and the associated dynamic response, particularly during high-frequency flapping. We introduce FlapKAD, an episode-structured simulation dataset comprising 2,000 rigid-wing flight episodes and 720,152 valid time steps, with bilateral wing kinematics, aerodynamic responses, and flight states recorded synchronously within a common clock. FlapKAD supports a unified bidirectional sequence-prediction benchmark constructed from the same temporally aligned episodes. The forward task predicts future vertical force coefficients and body vertical velocity from histories of realized flap and twist angles, whereas the inverse task reconstructs future flap- and twist-angle trajectories from the corresponding response histories. A benchmark of eight representative time-series architectures across multiple prediction horizons reveals direction- and horizon-dependent model behavior, systematically higher reconstruction errors for twist angle than for flap angle, and no consistent advantage from increased architectural complexity. FlapKAD provides a reproducible dataset and benchmark for studying coupled wing-kinematic, aerodynamic, and flight-state dynamics in flapping-wing aerial vehicles.

</details>


### [110] [Path-Following Control and Terramechanics Analysis for Planetary Rovers Under Wheel-to-Wheel Traction Asymmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38715)
*Ryuya Matsuoka,Keisuke Takehana,Kentaro Uno,Toshinori Kuwahara,Kazuya Yoshida*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无模型、仅依赖减速的滑移转向行星车路径跟踪控制策略，用于在持续牵引不对称的可变形松散地形上避免滑移-沉陷并实现路径修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于运动学的控制器在路径修正时往往加速车轮，容易造成滑移-沉陷被困；因此需要在牵引不对称和松散地形条件下实现安全、鲁棒的路径跟踪。

Method: 该方法不依赖模型，仅通过减速控制：限制最大指令速度，并选择性减速转弯外侧车轮，使其进入负滑转状态以充当机械锚进行航向修正；在四轮独立驱动EX1上设置左右不同履齿高度以引入显著牵引不对称并评估性能。

Result: 实验表明，该仅减速控制可在最高0.7 m/s的多种速度范围内抑制累积侧向漂移且不引起沉陷；车载多轴力测量进一步证明，目标减速实现了底盘动态载荷均衡，并完全恢复对侧驱动轮的原生推力能力。

Conclusion: 仅减速控制能够有效应对持续牵引不对称，避免滑陷，并通过地面力学机制恢复牵引能力，为松散地形上的行星车路径跟踪提供了无模型解决方案。

Abstract: This paper proposes a control strategy for path following that is model-free and relies solely on deceleration for skid-steering planetary rovers navigating deformable loose terrain under continuously imposed traction asymmetry. While conventional controllers that are based on kinematics frequently cause slip-sinkage entrapment by accelerating the wheels during path correction, the proposed approach prevents this failure by setting an upper limit on the maximum commanded velocity. Heading correction is achieved solely through the selective deceleration of the outer wheels, which are located on the outside of the turn, driving them into a negative slip regime to act as a mechanical anchor. The system was evaluated using the four-wheel independent-drive rover EX1 under an asymmetric wheel configuration with different left and right grouser heights that induces significant deviations from the path. Experimental results demonstrate that this deceleration-only control successfully suppresses accumulated lateral drift across various velocity regimes up to 0.7 m/s without causing sinkage. Crucially, direct force measurements from onboard multi-axis sensors provide important empirical evidence of the underlying terramechanics, proving that the targeted deceleration establishes dynamic load equalization across the chassis and completely restores the native thrust capability of the opposite driving wheel.

</details>


### [111] [CEER2: Directional and Tunable End-Effector and Root Compliance for Humanoid Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38709)
*Xinyuan Luo,Chunyuan Yang,Boyuan Chen,Xianyi Cheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种面向人形机器人行走-操作任务的柔顺框架，将末端执行器的方向性可调柔顺与可选择的根部柔顺结合，通过分层强化学习实现外力响应与任务运动保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人形机器人虽能跟踪复杂全身运动，但物理接触时需在保持任务所需运动的同时应对外力；且末端执行器与身体不同方向上的响应需求不同，例如某方向顺应接触力、另一方向保持运动精度，身体则可能需抵抗或跟随外力。

Method: 构建柔顺框架：末端执行器具备方向性与在线可调刚度，根部柔顺可选择用于外力抑制或力跟随。采用分层强化学习控制器，以高层末端执行器与根部命令调制固定的全身跟踪策略，并利用本体感受历史估计交互力。

Result: 在仿真与真实人形机器人实验中展示了方向刚度控制、在线刚度调整、不同根部柔顺模式、柔顺操作以及协同搬运。

Conclusion: 该框架使人形机器人能在复杂接触交互中按方向与任务需求灵活调节柔顺性，兼顾外力响应与全身运动跟踪，适用于行走-操作及协作任务。

Abstract: Humanoids are increasingly capable of tracking complex whole-body motions, but physical interaction introduces a different challenge. When a robot makes contact with a person or the environment, it needs to respond to external forces while preserving the motion needed for the task. This response can vary across directions in the end-effectors and on the body. For example, an end effector may need to accommodate contact force in one direction while maintaining motion accuracy in another, while the robot body may resist an external force or move with it. We present a compliance framework for humanoid loco-manipulation that combines directional and tunable end-effector (EE) compliance with selectable root compliance for external force rejection or force following. A hierarchical reinforcement learning controller modulates a fixed whole-body tracking policy through high-level EE and root commands, while interaction forces are estimated from proprioceptive history. Our simulation and real-world experiments on a humanoid demonstrate directional stiffness control, online stiffness adjustment, distinct root compliance, compliant manipulation, and collaborative carrying.

</details>


### [112] [Residual Wrench Certification and Margin-Aware Control Synthesis for Aerial Physical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38707)
*Abhimanyu Khadga,Abhinav Sinha,Shashi Ranjan Kumar*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种任务相关的残差力旋量权限认证框架，用于执行器受限多旋翼在悬停与接触载荷后的权限量化、保持与控制综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多旋翼执行器能力有限，悬停或接触后仍需保证剩余力旋量权限，以支撑接触任务、跟踪控制与形态比较。

Method: 基于凸几何推导预设凸储备（欧氏球、加权椭球）的有符号裕度；由执行器内部性界获得可计算的储备证书，支持松弛最大化分配；通过将指令投影到收紧可行集来保持储备；进一步将可用储备与结构化增益综合相联系，认证满足执行器限制的局部跟踪域，并证明标称指数收敛与鲁棒最终有界。

Result: 在给定任务与形态族下，同总推力时优化八旋翼比优化六旋翼具有更大裕度；闭环仿真涵盖固定几何八旋翼与变倾转四旋翼的持续刚性墙接触，且匹配装机推力时四旋翼局部证书给出更高的归一化推压极限；超出可实现边界时，八旋翼因旋翼推力极限、四旋翼因倾转伺服极限出现预测的权限丧失。

Conclusion: 该框架能够量化并保持受限执行器多旋翼在悬停与接触后的残余力旋量权限，并支持形态选择、任务比较与带执行器约束的控制综合。

Abstract: We develop a task-relative framework for certifying residual wrench authority after hover and contact loading in multirotors with bounded actuators. Using convex geometry, we derive signed margins for prescribed convex reserves, including Euclidean balls and weighted ellipsoids. We obtain computable reserve certificates from actuator-interiority bounds to support slack-maximizing allocation. To preserve the required reserve, we propose command projection onto a tightened feasible set. We then connect the available reserve to structured gain synthesis and certify a local tracking region that respects actuator limits. Within this region, we establish nominal exponential convergence and robust ultimate boundedness. For the prescribed task and morphology family, we show that the optimized octarotor attains a larger margin than the optimized hexarotor at equal total thrust. We evaluate the proposed framework in closed-loop simulations of sustained rigid-wall contact using a fixed-geometry octarotor and a variable-tilt quadrotor with matched installed thrust. The quadrotor's local certificate admits a higher normalized push limit for the prescribed task family. In tests beyond the realizability boundaries, we observe the predicted loss of authority through rotor-thrust limits for the octarotor and tilt-servo limits for the quadrotor.

</details>


### [113] [TERRA: Terrain-Aware Reconstruction, Retargeting and Control for Musculoskeletal Locomotion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38653)
*Merkourios Simos,Chengkun Li,Bianca Ziliotto,Alexander Mathis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: TERRA 是一个端到端流程，用于地形感知的运动重定向与肌肉驱动运动控制，可从无场景运动数据中恢复支撑地形并训练统一策略，使肌肉骨骼智能体在非平坦地形上运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肌肉骨骼建模与强化学习已能让肌肉驱动智能体复现复杂人体运动，但大多局限于平地；原因包括运动数据集缺少对齐的地形几何，以及将地形交互重定向到复杂肌肉骨骼身体上很困难。

Method: TERRA 仅从运动学轨迹出发，结合地形先验、估计的接触和负自由空间证据来恢复任务相关支撑几何；在重定向中考虑解剖、肌腱连续性和接触约束；利用五个数据集生成运动-地形配对，在 9.4 小时多样化运动数据上训练单一肌肉驱动控制策略。

Result: 在重建、重定向和留出跟踪基准上，TERRA 提升了地形精度，显著减少解剖与交互违规，并在支持的各类地形上取得最高完成率。

Conclusion: TERRA 提供了从无场景运动数据到肌肉驱动智能体在多样非平坦地形上运动的实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in musculoskeletal modeling and reinforcement learning have enabled muscle-actuated agents to reproduce increasingly complex human motions. Yet these capabilities remain largely confined to flat ground, in part because motion datasets rarely include aligned terrain geometry and because retargeting terrain interactions to complex musculoskeletal bodies is challenging. We present TERRA, an end-to-end pipeline for terrain-aware retargeting and control of musculoskeletal locomotion. From kinematic trajectories alone, TERRA combines terrain priors, estimated contacts, and negative free-space evidence to recover task-relevant support geometry. TERRA further considers anatomical, tendon-continuity, and contact constraints during retargeting. Using the resulting motion-terrain pairs from five datasets, we successfully train a single muscle-actuated control policy on 9.4 hours of diverse locomotion. Across reconstruction, retargeting, and held-out tracking benchmarks, TERRA improves terrain accuracy, sharply reduces anatomical and interaction violations, and achieves the highest observed completion rate over supported terrain families. Overall, TERRA provides a practical route from scene-less motion data to muscle-actuated locomotion over diverse non-flat terrain. Project website: this https URL

</details>


### [114] [Yggdrasil: a Layer-First 3D Scene Graph for Real-Time Querying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38640)
*Arshia Akhavan,Ermanno Bartoli,Afnan Algharbi,Alireza Hoseinpur,Iolanda Leite,Bryan Donyanavard*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 Yggdrasil，首个同时面向生成与消费效率的 3D 场景图：采用层级化图结构，可表达已有室内/室外、扁平/层级表示，并原生支持下游任务的位置与语义查询；查询速度最高提升 121 倍，集成到三个人体/导航/规划管线中可消除最高 99% 的场景图耗时。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 3D 场景图研究主要关注生成，较少优化其消费效率，导致先进感知管线需绕开自身场景图并付出延迟代价，难以满足实时感知循环的时间预算。

Method: 设计 Yggdrasil：一种 layer-first 层级图，由通用节点、边和层构成，可统一表达现有管线已产生的室内或室外、扁平或层级表示，并原生回答下游任务提出的位置与语义查询；将其集成到三个已发表管线：人体轨迹预测、物体目标导航、人体感知运动规划。

Result: 与公开 DSG 基线相比，Yggdrasil 查询最高快 121 倍；所测每个查询耗时 2 至 127 微秒，比 200 微秒关键帧预算低三到五个数量级，且在工作站和嵌入式设备上均成立；在三个集成管线中，最多移除其场景图耗时的 99%。

Conclusion: Yggdrasil 是首个为生成和消费同时高效的 3D 场景图，能显著降低查询延迟并满足实时感知预算，支持多种下游任务；实现、基准测试框架及三个集成均已在线公开。

Abstract: Robotic agents use 3D scene graphs (3DSG) to perform tasks ranging from scene understanding to scene interaction. Although an extensive body of work addresses scene graph generation, little attention has been paid to optimizing the graph for consumption, which leaves state-of-the-art perception pipelines to work around their own scene graph and to pay a latency cost that does not fit the real-time budget a perception loop runs on. We present Yggdrasil, the first 3D scene graph designed to be efficient for both generation and consumption: a layer-first hierarchical graph built from generic nodes, edges, and layers, which expresses the representations existing pipelines already produce, indoor or outdoor, flat or hierarchical, while natively answering the positional and semantic queries downstream tasks issue. Against a published DSG baseline, Yggdrasil answers queries up to $121\times$ faster, and every query we measure falls between 2 and 127 microseconds, three to five orders of magnitude inside the 200 microsecond keyframe budget a 3DSG consumer lives in, on both a workstation and embedded class device. We integrate Yggdrasil into three published pipelines spanning human trajectory prediction, object-goal navigation, and human-aware motion planning, where it removes up to 99% of the time each spends on its scene graph. The implementation, benchmark harness, and all three integrations are available online.

</details>


### [115] [Dense Temporal Motion Retargeting for Legged Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38617)
*Jaeryeong Kim,Taerim Yoon,Jin Cheng,Sungjoon Choi,Stelian Coros*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出DTMR，一种联合优化时序与控制的稠密时序运动重定向方法，用GPU并行采样MPC，实现人形机器人动态全身技能迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人与动物运动与机器人形态存在差距，尤其跳跃等动态运动需精细调整时序和控制，两者相互依赖。

Method: DTMR在单次优化中联合优化时序与控制，并对每个控制步进行稠密时序调整，仅变形需要改变时序的运动部分；采用基于采样的模型预测控制（MPC）在GPU上并行求解。

Result: 在两小时人类运动数据和四种人形机器人上评估，DTMR优于基线，尤其在动态运动上；允许更多时序变形可提高重定向精度；与优化时序维度的基线相比，在相同变形预算下更精确且约快19倍；训练策略可迁移到真实人形机器人。

Conclusion: DTMR能高效、精确地重定向动态人体运动，并支持真实人形机器人的策略迁移。

Abstract: Legged robots can learn expressive whole-body skills from the motions of humans and animals. Due to the morphology gap between the source and the robot, however, the motion must be tailored to the dynamic properties of the robot. In particular, dynamic motions such as a jump require careful adjustment, since their timing and control are interdependent. We propose dense temporal motion retargeting (DTMR), which jointly optimizes timing and control within a single optimization, where dense means that the timing is adjusted for every control step. This dense formulation enables DTMR to deform only the parts of the motion that need a change in timing. The problem is solved with sampling-based model predictive control (MPC) in parallel on a GPU. We evaluate DTMR against baselines on two hours of human motion with four humanoid robots, where the results show that DTMR outperforms baseline methods, particularly on dynamic motions. We also show that allowing more temporal deformation yields more precise retargeting. We further compare DTMR with a baseline that optimizes the temporal dimension, where the result shows that DTMR retargets more precisely under the same deformation budget while being ~19x faster. Lastly, policies trained on our references transfer to a real humanoid robot.

</details>


### [116] [Correcting WHERE, Preserving HOW: Compositional Generalization for Vision-Language-Action Models via Referential Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38616)
*Yanyan Zhang,Disheng Liu,Xinpeng Li,Chaoda Song,Mohsen Hariri,Debargha Ganguly,Wang Yang,Kai Ye,Bryce Grant,Vipin Chaudhary,Yu Yin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出免训练的 Referential Guidance（ReGuide）包装器，通过语义与几何重绑定引导机械臂末端进入指令指代物的示范支持构型，从而在冻结 VLA 策略下提升组合泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA 模型因机器人训练数据多样性不足，易利用视觉捷径，将动作与任务无关视觉特征关联，而非任务语义，导致难以对已见元素进行新组合（组合泛化）。现有方法依赖任务相关感知或定向数据多样化，缺乏对未见重组的显式机制，且常需修改骨干网络并重新训练。

Method: 观察到组合偏移下 VLA 常在全局 grounding 失败，但仍保留局部操作技能。ReGuide 是一种免训练包装器：给定 grounding 模块提供的物体位姿，结合语义重绑定与几何重绑定，将末端执行器引导至指令指代物的示范支持配置，使冻结策略可继续执行。

Result: 在多个 VLA 骨干网络的仿真实验及真实机器人实验中，ReGuide 在组合偏移下分别将成功率最多提升 56.8 和 75.0 个百分点，同时保持标准任务性能。

Conclusion: ReGuide 以无需训练、无需修改冻结策略的方式，为 VLA 提供显式组合泛化机制，在组合环境变化下显著提升鲁棒性并保持原有任务能力。

Abstract: While Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models enable flexible action generation, their generalization across diverse environmental elements, including manipulated objects, destinations, and backgrounds, is limited by the lack of diversity in robotic training data. Trained end-to-end on such data, VLAs tend to exploit visual shortcuts, associating actions with task-irrelevant visual features rather than the intended task semantics. These shortcuts block recomposition of elements already seen by the policy, that is, compositional generalization. Existing approaches mitigate such entanglement through task-relevant perception or targeted data diversification, but offer no explicit mechanism for unseen recomposition and require backbone-specific modifications with retraining. We observe that under such recomposition, VLAs often fail at global grounding while retaining local manipulation skills that recover near the correct target in familiar configurations. Therefore, we propose Referential Guidance (ReGuide), a training-free wrapper that, given object poses from a grounding module, combines semantic and geometric rebinding to guide the end-effector into demonstration-supported configurations of the instructed referent, where the frozen policy can resume execution. Experiments in simulation across multiple VLA backbones as well as on a real robot show that ReGuide improves success rates under compositional shifts by up to 56.8 and 75.0 percentage points, respectively, while preserving standard-task performance.

</details>


### [117] [FAST-Sync: Fast Group Synchronization for any Matrix Lie Group](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38594)
*Shane Holmes,Yiran Luo,Firat Taxpulat,David M. Rosen,Frank Dellaert*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Fast-Sync 是一种用于群同步的快速线性近似初始化方法，可推广 chordal 初始化到任意矩阵李群，并通过利用 Kronecker 积结构与同步图拓扑提升速度、可扩展性与精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 群同步问题广泛存在于机器人、计算机视觉等状态估计任务中，但通常高维且非凸，难以直接求解，因此需要高质量的初始化来支撑局部流形优化或可认证全局方法。

Method: 提出 Fast-Sync：将 chordal 初始化推广到任意矩阵李群；并引入两项算法增强，一是利用问题数据矩阵中的 Kronecker 积结构，二是利用同步图的拓扑结构。

Result: 在多类群同步任务上的实验表明，Fast-Sync 能提供高质量初始化，使局部优化器高效恢复全局最优群同步解，并在较大测量噪声下仍保持高成功率。

Conclusion: Fast-Sync 可作为群同步的快速线性初始化器，有效提升后续局部或全局优化方法的求解效率与成功率。

Abstract: Group synchronization (GS) is the problem of estimating a set of $N$ unknown elements $g_1,\ldots, g_N \in \mathcal{G}$ in a group $\mathcal{G}$, given noisy measurements of a subset of their pairwise ratios $g_i^{-1} g_j$. GS problems lie at the core of many state estimation tasks in robotics and computer vision, including 3D vision, robotic mapping, inertial navigation, and molecular reconstruction. Unfortunately, GS problems are typically both high-dimensional and non-convex, and therefore hard to solve in general. In this paper, we present Fast-Sync, a fast linear approximation method for GS that is suitable for initializing local manifold-based optimizers or certifiable global methods. Our approach generalizes chordal initialization to arbitrary matrix Lie groups, and additionally proposes two new key algorithmic enhancements: we show how to exploit both the Kronecker-product structure in the problem data matrix and the topology of the synchronization graph to improve speed, scalability, and accuracy. Experimental evaluation across several GS tasks demonstrates that Fast-Sync provides high-quality initializations that enable local optimizers to efficiently recover globally optimal GS solutions, achieving high success rates even with considerable measurement noise.

</details>


### [118] [Drone Soccer: Learning to Manipulate with Multicopter Downwash](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38588)
*Neelay Joglekar,Bavin Saravanan,Yutong Wang,Varun Kandiyappan,Junyi Geng,Sebastian Scherer*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该工作探索把多旋翼无人机的螺旋桨下洗气流从扰动转变为主动操控工具，设计无人机足球任务，并用简化下洗动力学模型训练强化学习策略实现带球，且成功迁移到真实世界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼主要用于感知、建图和探索任务，近年发展的空中操控无人机（UAM）虽能执行移动操控，但现有先进UAM通常忽略下洗或将其视为干扰。作者因此提出：能否在操控过程中主动利用下洗作为工具。

Method: 设计无人机足球任务以验证基于下洗操控的可行性；建立简化的下洗动力学模型；基于该模型训练强化学习策略，让无人机完成带球（dribble）动作。

Result: 所训练的策略能够实现足球带球，并进一步成功迁移到真实世界部署中。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了基于下洗的多旋翼新型操控能力的关键见解，表明主动利用下洗进行空中操控具有可行性。

Abstract: Although multicopter drones are traditionally designed for "perception-only" tasks, like mapping and exploration, recent work has sought to develop Unmanned Aerial Manipulators (UAMs) to solve mobile manipulation tasks. Aerial manipulation performance can be impacted by "downwash," the airflow produced by propellers, but current state-of-the-art UAMs either ignore downwash or treat it as a disturbance. Instead, is it possible to actively use downwash as a tool during manipulation? We design a drone soccer task to explore the feasibility of downwash-based manipulation. Specifically, we develop a simplified downwash dynamics model which we use to train an RL policy to dribble a soccer ball. We further demonstrate that our policy transfers to real world deployment. This work provides key insights into novel manipulation capabilities for multicopters.

</details>


### [119] [Data-Efficient Adaptation of a Driving VLA to Class 8 Trucks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38570)
*Satyajeet Das,Aaron Buxbaum,Niels Joubert,Gaurav S. Sukhatme*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出“先适应后引导”（adapt-then-steer）策略，将现成乘用车VLA迁移到Class 8卡车：先少量事故/施工场景微调动作生成栈，再用Flow Velocity Steering（FVS）残差模块进一步优化轨迹，实现数据高效迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Class 8卡车在几何、动力学和操控要求上与乘用车差异显著，乘用车VLA难以直接迁移，尤其在事故现场、施工区等非结构化场景；而从零训练卡车驾驶VLA成本高。

Method: 以NVIDIA Alpamayo 1.5为基座模型，仅微调其动作生成栈，使用几百个真实高速公路施工与事故相关场景；随后固定适配后的VLA，引入FVS：一种紧凑、以流时间为条件的残差模块，在每步动作序列生成中向动作空间流速度添加学习到的修正。

Result: 在场景不相交的留出集开环评估中，定向微调使6.4 s全时域单候选平均位移误差（ADE）和最终位移误差（FDE）较基座模型减少一半以上；FVS进一步将微调模型全时域ADE和FDE分别降低13.9%和16.5%。相同数据预算下，定向监督比通用卡车驾驶监督的全时域ADE低19%–26%，且与使用约65倍通用卡车驾驶场景微调的模型竞争力相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明，adapt-then-steer可支持面向Class 8卡车的数据高效车辆域迁移。

Abstract: Class 8 trucks differ from passenger cars in geometry, dynamics, and maneuvering requirements. As a result, vision-language-action (VLA) models trained for passenger vehicles do not readily transfer to Class 8 trucks, particularly in unstructured scenarios such as accident scenes and construction zones. Rather than training a truck-driving VLA from scratch, we propose an adapt-then-steer strategy that adapts an off-the-shelf VLA to generate trajectories for Class-8 trucks in these challenging scenarios. In the adapt stage, we use NVIDIA's Alpamayo 1.5 as the base model, fine-tuning only its action-generation stack on a few hundred real-world construction and accident-related highway scenarios. In the steer stage, we introduce Flow Velocity Steering (FVS) to further refine the model's predictions while holding the adapted VLA fixed. FVS is a compact, flow-time-conditioned residual module that adds learned corrections to the action-space flow velocity used to update the action sequence at each generation step. In open-loop evaluation on a scenario-disjoint held-out set, targeted fine-tuning more than halves single-candidate average displacement error (ADE) and final displacement error (FDE) over the entire 6.4 s horizon compared to the base model. Using the same targeted demonstrations, FVS further reduces the fine-tuned model's full-horizon ADE and FDE by 13.9% and 16.5%, respectively. At matched data budgets, targeted supervision yields 19-26% lower full-horizon ADE than general truck-driving supervision, while the targeted model remains competitive with a model fine-tuned on approximately 65 times as many general truck-driving scenarios. These results support adapt-then-steer for data-efficient vehicle-domain transfer to Class 8 trucks. Our project website is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [120] [Systematically Exploring the Capabilities of GPT-6 Astra as Embodied Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38537)
*Galbot Team,Xuchuan Chen,Xiaoqian Cheng,Yu Deng,Lihe Ding,Shaocong Dong,Xiangjun Gao,Haozhe Jia,Zekai Li,Zhoujian Li,Yunrui Lian,Sikai Liang,Chenghuai Lin,Dairu Liu,Jiahang Liu,Qingtao Liu,Yuxuan Ma,Zekun Qi,Jiayi Su,He Wang,Ruochen Xu,Tianyu Xu,Xudong Xu,Zhe Xu,Mi Yan,Siming Yan,Li Yi,Ruixi Yu,Jinlu Zhang,Yintianrun Zhang,Zhikai Zhang,Zhizheng Zhang,Yixin Zheng,Weiyi Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 评估GPT-6 Astra作为通用具身策略，在六类机器人任务中能生成数值动作并与学习策略混合控制，但任务决策有用而物理控制可靠性不足，且推理延迟与算力开销严重限制实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大模型能否从高层规划扩展到直接控制、与已学策略协作及反馈驱动适应，评估其作为通用机器人策略在抓取、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动和人形全身操作中的能力与瓶颈。

Method: 在六个领域开展综合评测：夹爪操作、灵巧操作、移动操作、导航、运动、人形loco-manipulation；比较直接控制、与π0.5等学习策略混合控制、经验引导与反馈适应；统计成功率、token消耗、模型调用次数和推理时延，部分任务在推理期间暂停物理。

Result: 夹爪操作可修正目标并为后续策略准备接触条件，混合控制在RoboDojo子集达48%；灵巧操作混合控制在十次经验引导DexJoCo试验中达50%，直接手内控制难以协调手指接触；移动操作在RoboCasa365达38.7%；导航在RxR指令跟随达92%、HM3D目标搜索达82%，但搜索绕路严重；运动五次数值参考生成均未在单障碍赛道到达目标，稳定性与前进有改善；人形loco-manipulation在30个HumanoidBench任务中13个超过基线；50个RoboDojo实例每条件下策略辅助与直接控制分别消耗624.8M和1.132B tokens；30秒运动运行需250次模型调用，平均每次39.86秒，推理时暂停物理。

Conclusion: Astra能提供有用的任务决策，但与可靠物理控制之间仍存在明显鸿沟；推理延迟和token开销构成实际控制瓶颈，混合控制总体优于直接控制，部分任务仍需专用策略或更高效率推理。

Abstract: GPT-6 Astra exhibits a remarkable ability to generate numerical robot actions, extending its role beyond high-level planning. To assess Astra's capabilities as general-purpose embodied policies, we conduct comprehensive evaluations across six domains, examining direct control, cooperation with learned policies, and feedback-driven adaptation. In gripper manipulation, Astra can correct task targets and prepare contact conditions for subsequent policy execution; hybrid control with {\pi}0.5 achieves 48% success on the evaluated RoboDojo subset. In dexterous manipulation, hybrid control achieves 50% success in ten experience-guided DexJoCo trials, while direct in-hand control struggles to coordinate finger contacts. In mobile manipulation, hybrid control reaches 38.7% success on the evaluated RoboCasa365. In navigation, Astra leads our local comparisons, reaching 92% success on RxR instruction following and 82% on HM3D object search, although search incurs substantial detours. In locomotion, dense motion-reference generation remains unreliable: none of five sequential attempts on a single obstacle course reaches the goal, despite improvements in stability and forward progress. In humanoid loco-manipulation, Astra exceeds baseline methods on 13 of 30 HumanoidBench tasks with pretrained whole-body controllers. These findings reveal a gap between useful task decisions and reliable physical control. Inference latency further constrains practical control: across 50 RoboDojo instances per condition, policy-assisted and direct control consume 624.8 million and 1.132 billion tokens. A 30-second locomotion run requires 250 model calls averaging 39.86 seconds each, with physics paused during inference.

</details>


### [121] [Behavioral Persistence and Incomplete Functional Transfer of Co-evolved Communication in Evolutionary Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38527)
*Fernando Montes-Gonzalez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文评估将协同演化出的通信协议从2D仿真直接迁移到3D物理环境且不重新训练网络权重的效果，发现迁移仅是部分且不对称的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验涌现通信协议能否在不重新训练的情况下从2D仿真直接迁移到真实3D物理环境，并区分信号翻译与导航/生态条件对迁移成败的影响。

Method: 两个e-puck型机器人由带残差连接的GRU网络控制，在具社会信号的食物搜寻任务中评估。感官与运动翻译层需三项修正以稳定物理运行，其中包括基于训练后残差权重中可测不对称性来校准饥饿项。另设实验在社会通道中加入显式方向信息。

Result: 即使有修正，迁移仍部分且不对称：30个测试种子中仅一个智能体在一次中到达食物源，另一智能体从未到达；任务成功需两智能体均到达食物区域。加入显式方向信息可观察到接收方轨迹变化并在若干特定情况下改善，但不足以让第二个智能体到达食物源。

Conclusion: 成功迁移涌现通信可能不仅依赖保留信号过程本身，还依赖保留协议演化时的生态与导航条件；仅靠信号翻译不足以解释迁移限制。

Abstract: This work evaluates the direct transfer of a co-evolved communication protocol from a 2D simulation to a 3D physical environment, without retraining the network weights. Two e-puck-type robots, controlled by a GRU network with residual connection, were evaluated in a food-seeking task with social signaling. The sensory and motor translation layer required three corrections for stable physical operation, including the calibration of a hunger term based on a measurable asymmetry in the trained residual weights. Even with these corrections, the transfer was partial and asymmetric: one agent reached the food source in one of thirty tested seeds, while the other did not reach it in any. Task success was measured by both agents reaching the food area. An additional experiment incorporating explicit directional information in the social channel produced observable changes in the trajectory of the receiving agent and improvements in several specific cases. However, these improvements were not enough to allow the second agent to reach the food source, suggesting that the limitation may not be explained solely by signal translation, but also by the ability to navigate under the new physical constraints. The results suggest that successful transfer of emergent communication may depend not only on preserving the signaling process itself, but also on preserving the ecological and navigational conditions under which the protocol evolved.

</details>


### [122] [Onboard Vision and MPC Navigation for Underwater Robots: An Open BlueROV2 Platform for Multi-Robot Experiments & Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38511)
*Victor Nan Fernandez-Ayala,Wiktor Kowalczyk,Cezary Banaszek,Dimos V. Dimarogonas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源的BlueROV2自主水下机器人平台，集成了机载视觉与非线性模型预测控制（NMPC），用于受限环境中的自主导航与对接，并提供可复现的仿真与低成本物理对接站。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主水下机器人在受限环境中作业需要鲁棒的感知、状态估计与控制能力；同时，为促进可复现开发，需要开源、模块化且支持多机器人仿真的软硬件平台。

Method: 平台采用NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX与Intel RealSense D435i立体相机，并集成于模块化耐压舱中。通过水下校准的立体深度与实时目标检测，获取附近BlueROV2在相机坐标系与机体坐标系下的相对位置。采用基于四元数的估计器融合外部位姿与惯性测量。基于非线性六自由度模型的NMPC控制器跟踪规划的导航与对接轨迹。还提供开源的基于物理的PX4 SITL与Gazebo环境、多机器人仿真工具以及低成本物理对接站。

Result: 实验评估了水下感知、机载计算性能、状态估计、轨迹跟踪与自主对接。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个开源、集成化的BlueROV2平台，支持受限水下环境中的自主导航与对接研究，并增强了开发的可复现性。

Abstract: Autonomous underwater robots require robust perception, estimation and control to operate in confined environments. This paper presents an open-source BlueROV2 platform combining onboard vision with nonlinear Model Predictive Control (NMPC) for autonomous navigation and docking. The platform integrates an NVIDIA Jetson Orin NX and an Intel RealSense D435i stereo camera in a modular pressure housing. Underwater-calibrated stereo depth and realtime object detection provide relative position measurements of nearby BlueROV2 vehicles in the camera and body frames. A quaternion-based estimator fuses external pose and inertial measurements, while an NMPC controller based on a nonlinear six-degree-of-freedom model tracks planned navigation and docking trajectories. To support reproducible development, we also provide open-source physics-based PX4 SITL and Gazebo environments, multi-robot simulation tools and a lowcost physical docking station. Experiments evaluate underwater perception, onboard computational performance, state estimation, trajectory tracking and autonomous docking.

</details>


### [123] [What to Attend, What to Keep: Skill-Conditioned Visuotactile Representation with Progress-Guided Event Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38494)
*Amir-Hossein Shahidzadeh,Seungjae Lee,Eadom Dessalene,Shanthosh Raaj Mohanram Mageswari,Soroush Etemad,Furong Huang,Cornelia Fermüller,Yiannis Aloimonos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种技能条件化的多模态表示，将视觉与触觉按当前技能动态融合，并利用稀疏事件记忆保留最近K个已执行技能的终端观测，以更好估计技能进度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操作中视觉、触觉和语言的重要性随阶段变化：视觉引导接近，触觉随时间演化决定抓取、对齐与接触；但现有多模态操作策略通常使用固定时间上下文和融合策略，忽视了不同技能对各模态需求的差异。因此，作者在原始技能层面研究视觉与触觉应如何结合，即每个技能需要从每个传感器获取什么信息。

Method: 提出技能条件化表示：查询到的技能条件化地融合模态特定的短期观测token，同时关注一个稀疏事件记忆，该记忆保留最近K个已执行技能的终端观测。

Result: 在三个接触丰富任务上以技能进度估计进行评估：相比微调的SOTA进度模型，滑移检测延迟降低87%；相比仅视觉消融，扭转完成延迟降低67.5%；通过稀疏事件记忆，盲搜索任务上的进度误差降低92%。增益集中在完成由接触或任务历史定义之处。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在多模态表示中，观测形成而非仅策略架构，才是核心挑战。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation integrates vision, touch, and language, whose importance shifts across stages: vision guides reaching, while touch, through its evolution over time, decides grasping, alignment, and contact. Yet existing multi-modal manipulation policies typically use fixed temporal contexts and fusion strategies, despite shifts in what each modality contributes across different skills. We study how vision and touch should be combined at the level of primitive skills, asking what each skill needs from each sensor, and propose a skill-conditioned representation in which the queried skill conditions fusion over modality-specific short-term observation tokens while attending to a sparse event memory that retains terminal observations from the last $K$ executed skills. Evaluated by skill progress estimation on three contact-rich tasks, it reduces slip-detection delay by 87% against fine-tuned SOTA progress models, twist-completion delay by 67.5% against a vision-only ablation, and progress error on a blind search task by 92% through sparse event memory. Gains concentrate exactly where completion is defined by contact or task history. More broadly, our results suggest that observation formation not only policy architecture is a central challenge in multi-modal representation. Project Website: this http URL

</details>


### [124] [CADeT: Causal-Aware Deformation Transmission for Indirect Robotic Manipulation of Soft Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38483)
*Junlei Hu,Dominic Jones,Pietro Valdastri*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CADeT，将结构因果模型与主动感知结合，在线推断深层可变形组织的变形传递模式并估计状态相关黏附雅可比，用信念感知MPC实现间接形状控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在机器人辅助微创手术中，机器人无法直接接触深层可变形解剖结构，中间组织会空间滤波变形传递；仅被动观测时，解耦模式与阻塞模式可能产生相似运动响应，导致模式识别歧义，从而难以实现间接操控。

Method: CADeT将结构因果模型（SCM）与主动感知集成：正常操作时控制动作更新传递模式信念；当歧义持续时，选择额外探测动作以提高模式可区分性；在线估计状态相关黏附雅可比；将模式信念与学习到的雅可比嵌入信念感知模型预测控制器（MPC），用于间接目标形状控制。

Result: 在仿真和da Vinci研究套件（dVRK）上，使用体模与离体猪组织验证，CADeT相比所评估的无模型和基于模型基线，取得更高的模式识别准确率与更快的形状误差收敛。

Conclusion: 结果表明，在所评估条件下，主动感知能够改善模式识别与间接变形控制。

Abstract: Indirect manipulation of deep-seated deformable anatomy inaccessible to the robot is challenging in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS) because intervening tissues spatially filter deformation transmission. Passive observations can be ambiguous because the Decoupled and Blocked modes may produce similar motion responses. We propose CADeT, a causal-aware deformation transmission framework that integrates structural causal model (SCM) with active sensing to infer a latent transmission mode and estimate a state-dependent adhesion Jacobian online. During normal manipulation, control actions update the mode belief; when ambiguity persists, an additional probing action is selected to improve mode distinguishability. The mode belief and learned Jacobian are incorporated into a belief-aware model predictive controller for indirect target-shape control. Validation in simulation and on the da Vinci research kit (dVRK), using phantom and ex vivo porcine tissues, shows higher mode-identification accuracy and faster shape-error convergence than the evaluated model-free and model-based baselines. These results show that active sensing improves mode identification and indirect deformation control under the evaluated conditions.

</details>


### [125] [Diffusion-2BC: Hybrid Diffusion and Regression Training for Offline Behavior Cloning in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38472)
*Bruno Maciel Machado,Eric Aislan Antonelo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Diffusion-2BC 在共享视觉编码器上结合扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失，辅助分支仅用于训练，推理仍为扩散策略；该方法在 Claw 与 CARLA 鸟瞰导航中提升了闭环可靠性，并保留了多模态行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 行为克隆常用 MSE 回归，但难以匹配同一观测对应多个有效动作的演示数据；扩散策略虽能建模条件多模态动作分布，但在有限数据下联合学习视觉特征与控制时，闭环性能可能不稳定。

Method: 提出 Diffusion-2BC：共享视觉编码器，同时优化扩散去噪目标与辅助确定性行为克隆损失；辅助回归分支只在训练阶段使用，推理阶段保持基于扩散的策略。在受控 Claw 环境与 CARLA 鸟瞰导航中评估，包括路线条件驾驶、多交叉口无路线导航以及 Town01 到 Town02 的跨地图评估。

Result: 在 Claw 任务中，Diffusion-2BC 将平均掩码距离误差较扩散行为克隆基线降低约 10%，较标准确定性行为克隆降低 85%。在无路线 CARLA 中，其在 Town01 和 Town02 下均比两个基线在终止前行驶明显更远。定性 rollout 还显示出不同路线选择，体现所提扩散智能体的多模态行为。

Conclusion: 辅助回归信号可提升扩散行为克隆的闭环可靠性，同时在受控基准中保持多模态预测能力。

Abstract: Behavior cloning provides an offline route to autonomous-driving policy learning, but mean-squared-error regression is poorly matched to demonstrations in which one observation admits several valid actions. Diffusion policies can represent conditional multimodal action distributions, yet their closed-loop performance may be unstable when visual features and control are learned from limited data. This paper presents Diffusion-2BC, which combines a diffusion denoising objective with an auxiliary deterministic behavior-cloning loss over a shared visual encoder. The auxiliary branch is used only during training; inference remains diffusion-based. The proposed method is evaluated in the controlled Claw environment and in bird's-eye-view CARLA navigation, including route-conditioned driving, route-free navigation through multiple intersections, and cross-map evaluation from Town01 to Town02. In the Claw task, Diffusion-2BC reduced the mean mask-distance error by approximately 10% relative to a diffusion-based behavior-cloning baseline and by 85% relative to standard deterministic behavior cloning. In route-free CARLA, Diffusion-2BC traveled substantially farther before termination under the evaluation protocol than both baselines in Town01 and Town02. Additional qualitative rollouts revealed distinct route choices, showing the multimodal behavior of the proposed diffusion-based agent. The results indicate that an auxiliary regression signal can improve the closed-loop reliability of diffusion behavior cloning while preserving multimodal prediction in the controlled benchmark.

</details>


### [126] [L1-MPPI: L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral for Agile UAV Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38467)
*Lukáš Kotek,Ondřej Procházka,Vojtěch Vonásek,Martin Saska,Robert Pěnička*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出L1自适应模型预测路径积分（L1-MPPI），将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联，用于提升高速无人机轨迹跟踪在模型不确定性和外部扰动下的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPPI无人机控制方法通常未显式建模气动效应、变载荷，且常忽略底层电机控制器动态，导致高速跟踪在未知载荷或模型失配时性能下降。

Method: 将L1自适应控制与MPPI级联；在MPPI所用动态模型中纳入底层飞行控制器与电机动态，并引入反映底层控制器行为的迭代混合方案；利用L1增广补偿模型不确定性和外部扰动，如附加载荷与气动阻力失配。

Result: 在仿真和真实飞行中均提升跟踪性能；35%质量增加时，相比普通MPPI降低RMSE 58.61%，相比使用在线质量估计器的MPPI降低RMSE 38.59%；真实实验中速度达13.50 m/s，加速度达2.5 g。

Conclusion: L1-MPPI通过结合自适应补偿与更完整的底层动态建模，显著增强了无人机在未知载荷和模型不确定性下的高速轨迹跟踪鲁棒性与精度。

Abstract: This work proposes the L1 Adaptive Model Predictive Path Integral (L1-MPPI). It cascades L1 adaptive control with the Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) to improve tracking of high-speed UAV trajectories. Thanks to the L1augmentation, the tracking remains accurate even under model uncertainties and external disturbances, such as an additional payload or a mismatch in the modeled aerodynamic drag. In contrast to existing MPPI approaches for UAV control that do not explicitly model aerodynamic effects, varying payloads, and typically neglect the dynamics of low-level motor controllers, our L1-MPPI approach enhances the dynamic model used in the MPPI by incorporating the low-level flight controller and motor dynamics, as well as an iterative mixing scheme that reflects the approach of the low-level controller. The proposed method demonstrates improved tracking performance in both simulation and the real world, even when the UAV is subjected to an unknown payload. In flight with 35% mass increase, our approach lowers the RMSE by 58.61% with respect to plain MPPI. Compared to the same MPPI using an online mass estimator in place of the L1 augmentation, the RMSE is lower by 38.59%. During the real-world experiments the UAV reaches speeds up to 13.50 m/s and accelerations up to 2.5 g.

</details>


### [127] [TrafficSignBench: Rule-Centric Closed-Loop Evaluation of Traffic-Sign Compliance in Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38463)
*Victoria Smirnova,Viktoriia Zinkovich,Gregorii Bukhtuev,Artem Belyaev,Andrey Kuznetsov,Denis Shepelev,Vlad Shakhuro*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出 TrafficSignBench，一个以交通标志为中心的大规模基准，用于系统且可解释地评估自动驾驶规划器的交通规则合规性，并利用显式交通标志约束生成合规专家轨迹以微调现有规划器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动驾驶规划器通常使用驾驶得分、到达率和碰撞率等聚合指标评估，未显式衡量交通规则合规性，导致规划器可在基准上获得高分却仍存在不安全或违法行为，限制其真实部署。

Method: TrafficSignBench 结合基于真实地图的仿真以生成真实道路布局，并采用面向规则的过程式场景生成以可扩展、均衡覆盖代表性不足的规则。实现对应 34 种交通标志的交通规则，每种规则配备自动检查器，在闭环执行中检测违规。生成 29,000 个多样道路场景和 29 种测试场景类型，并构建 5,800 个测试场景。此外，通过显式交通标志约束将现有规划器转化为规则合规轨迹专家，从而可扩展生成高质量 oracle 轨迹用于微调。

Result: 实验表明，当前自动驾驶规划器在标准评估指标上表现强劲，但在交通规则合规性方面表现较差；基于交通标志约束的专家轨迹生成方法能够为微调提供可扩展的高质量 oracle 轨迹。

Conclusion: TrafficSignBench 填补了交通规则合规性系统评估的空白，提供可解释、可控且可扩展的评测框架，并展示了通过规则约束生成合规专家轨迹来提升规划器规则合规性的可行路径。

Abstract: Autonomous driving planners are typically evaluated using aggregate metrics such as driving score, destination rate, and collision rate, which do not explicitly measure compliance with traffic rules. As a result, planners can achieve high benchmark scores while still exhibiting unsafe or illegal behaviors, limiting their applicability to real-world deployment. To address this gap, we introduce TrafficSignBench, a large-scale, traffic sign-centric benchmark for systematic and interpretable evaluation of traffic-rule compliance in autonomous driving. Our framework combines real-map-based simulation for realistic road layouts with rule-targeted procedural scenario generation for scalable and balanced coverage of underrepresented rules. We implement traffic rules corresponding to 34 traffic signs, each equipped with an automatic rule checker for detecting violations during closed-loop execution. This design yields 29,000 diverse road scenes and 29 distinct testing scenario types, enabling controlled evaluation of rule-specific planner behavior. We construct 5,800 testing scenes and demonstrate that current autonomous driving planners can exhibit poor traffic-rule compliance despite strong performance on standard evaluation metrics. To address this limitation, we transform existing planners into rule-compliant trajectory experts via explicit traffic-sign constraints, enabling scalable generation of high-quality oracle trajectories for fine-tuning.

</details>


### [128] [Observability Analysis and Online Calibration of Visual-Inertial-Wheel Odometry for 4WIS4WID Mobile Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38462)
*Branimir Ćaran,Vladimir Milić,Bojan Šekoranja,Bojan Jerbić*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种带在线标定的视觉-惯性-轮式里程计（VIWO）框架，用于四轮独立转向驱动（4WIS4WID）移动机器人；通过四轮驱动速度和转向角推导二维里程计模型，并分析可观测性与退化运动。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对4WIS4WID移动机器人，需要一种能够在线标定轮式内参并融合视觉惯性信息的里程计方法，以提升定位精度和鲁棒性。

Method: 建立基于四轮纵向滚动与横向无侧滑约束的二维里程计模型，开发预积分模型与解析雅可比以实现高效滤波与标定；对线性化VIWO系统进行可观测性分析，表明仅驱动模型使转向偏移不可观测，而冗余模型恢复其可观测性；识别VINS的四个标准不可观测方向及里程计参考系任意放置引入的三个额外方向；刻画零偏航角速率、恒定转向、车轮不滚动等退化运动，并推导固定参考系后十三个车轮内参的激励条件。

Result: 在仿真和真实4WIS4WID移动机器人实验中验证了系统性能。

Conclusion: 所提VIWO框架能有效在线标定并提升4WIS4WID机器人里程计的可观测性与鲁棒性，且对退化运动具有明确的可观测性条件。

Abstract: In this paper, we present a visual-inertial-wheel odometry (VIWO) framework with online calibration for four-wheel independently steered and driven (4WIS4WID) mobile robots. We derive a 2D odometry model directly from the four driving velocities and steering angles, using both the longitudinal rolling constraints and the lateral no-slip constraints of all wheels. A preintegration model and analytical Jacobians are developed for efficient filtering and calibration. An observability analysis of the linearized VIWO system shows that a drive-only model makes all steering offsets unobservable, whereas the proposed redundant model restores their observability. The analysis also identifies four standard VINS unobservable directions and three additional directions associated with the arbitrary placement of the odometry reference frame. Furthermore, we characterize several degenerate motions, including zero yaw rate, constant steering, and a non-rolling wheel, and derive the corresponding excitation conditions for the thirteen wheel intrinsics that remain after fixing the odometry frame reference. The performance of the proposed system has been demonstrated in both simulation and real-world experiments on a 4WIS4WID mobile robot.

</details>


### [129] [BIND: Binding 3D Robot Actions to 2D Image Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38443)
*Cameron Smith,Arsh Tangri,Vitor Guizilini,Yue Wang,Zubair Irshad,Sergey Zakharov*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 BIND，一种将 3D 机器人动作绑定到对应 2D 图像特征的动作表示，用于视觉运动策略，显著提升数据效率和对分布外物体位置与相机视角的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人策略通常用 MLP 从预训练视觉编码器产生的单一全局特征向量回归动作，需要仅从演示中学习动作与图像特征的对应关系；虽然图像特征语义丰富且空间鲁棒，但策略对相机视角和物体位置的细微变化脆弱，且数据效率低。

Method: BIND 通过相机几何而非学习来提供动作-特征关系：离散化候选末端执行器位置体积，将每个候选绑定到其在各相机视图投影处的预训练特征，并通过评分每个候选的位置与图像绑定特征组合来选择动作。

Result: 在真实机器人上研究数据效率、对未见物体位置和相机视角的分布外鲁棒性，以及长时程任务执行和灵巧性。BIND 在少至 5 次演示时接近完美成功，并在剧烈相机视角变化和留出物体位置下优雅退化，而坐标回归基线完全失败。

Conclusion: BIND 是一种高数据效率且鲁棒的动作表示，利用相机几何将 3D 动作与 2D 图像特征绑定，优于坐标回归基线。

Abstract: We introduce BIND, a new action representation for visuomotor robot policies that binds 3D robot actions to their corresponding 2D image features, yielding strong data efficiency gains and robustness to out-of-distribution object positions and camera viewpoints. The action heads of current robot policies are typically formulated as an MLP regression from a single global feature vector produced by a pre-trained vision encoder. This global formulation requires the policy network to discover, from demonstrations alone, the relationship between target robot actions and the image features they project onto. The consequence is that although modern image features are semantically descriptive, spatially robust, and even multiview-consistent, the policies built on them are brittle to subtle changes in camera viewpoint and object placement--and surprisingly data-inefficient. BIND closes this gap by supplying the action-feature relationship through camera geometry rather than learning: it discretizes a volume of candidate end effector positions, attaches each candidate to the pre-trained features at its projection in each camera view, and selects actions by scoring each candidate's position and image-bound feature combination. On a real robot, we study data efficiency and out-of-distribution robustness to unseen object positions and camera viewpoints, as well as general long-horizon task execution and dexterity. We find BIND to be highly data-efficient and robust: it achieves near-perfect success on tasks with as few as 5 demonstrations, and degrades gracefully under steep camera-viewpoint shifts and held-out object positions where coordinate-regression baselines completely fail.

</details>


### [130] [Embodiment-aware control by inference over the operator: a simulation study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38437)
*Sara Falcone*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出具身感知控制器UEE，通过隐式视线/瞳孔信号与任务结果推断操作者具身状态及视-本体感觉线索权重，并依偏好选择设备设置；仿真中能快速适配并接近oracle，但依赖可校准信号与变化建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统遥操作按通道保真度调优，而操作者是否将设备体验为身体一部分（具身感SoE）仅事后问卷测量；预测加工虽主张减少预测-反馈失配，但预测不可观测，且单纯最小化失配会通过移除反馈来作弊。

Method: 将问题建模为离散主动推断智能体：UEE从隐式注视、瞳孔信号和任务结果推断操作者具身程度与视-本体感觉线索权重，在显式偏好下选择有界设备参数；用300个异构合成操作者仿真，并与固定控制、无模型bandit、同推断的期望效用控制器及朴素预测误差最小化等方法比较。

Result: 多数操作者半分钟内找到合适设置，后半程具身感达1.68，优于最佳固定设置的0.73（0-2量表）；不读取操作者的自适应不优于固定控制，无模型bandit更差；同推断的期望效用控制器表现相同，说明收益来自贝叶斯推断与显式偏好而非主动推断的信息寻求项；朴素预测误差最小化会不给反馈并降低任务成功率（0.74 vs 0.90）；模型族外收益缩小但仍在，群体多样性越高收益越大，信号无信息或线索权重中途变化未建模时收益消失。

Conclusion: 具身感知控制有效但条件脆弱；面向人的研究须首先确保信号校准并建模线索权重变化。

Abstract: Teleoperation systems are tuned for channel fidelity, while whether the operator experiences the device as part of the body, the Sense of Embodiment (SoE), is measured only afterwards, by questionnaire. Predictive-processing accounts suggest controlling devices to reduce the mismatch between the operator's predictions and the returned feedback, but those predictions are unobservable, and an objective that only penalizes mismatch is minimized by removing feedback. We formulate an embodiment-aware controller, the Universal Embodiment Engine (UEE), that infers the operator's embodiment and visuo-proprioceptive cue weighting from implicit gaze and pupil signals and task outcome, and chooses bounded device settings under explicit preferences, cast as a discrete Active Inference agent. In simulations with 300 heterogeneous synthetic operators, the UEE found the suitable setting within half a minute for most operators, before identifying their exact type, and came close to an oracle in the second half of the session (embodiment 1.68 against 0.73 for the best fixed setting, on a 0-2 scale). Adapting without reading the operator did no better than fixed control, and model-free bandits did worse, whereas an expected-utility controller with the same inference did exactly as well: the benefit comes from Bayesian inference over the operator with explicit preferences, not from the information-seeking term of Active Inference. A naive prediction-error minimizer withheld feedback, as its objective implies, and lost task success (0.74 vs 0.90). The benefit shrank but persisted for operators outside the controller's model family, grew with the variety of the population, and vanished when the controller trusted an uninformative signal or when cue weighting changed mid-session without being modeled. These failures show what studies with people must establish first: calibrated signals and a model of change.

</details>


### [131] [A Reachability-based Safety Certificate for Dynamical System Motion Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38421)
*Aditya Vats,Tianyi Xia,Nadia Figueroa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于后向可达管的值函数安全证书，用于保障动态系统（DS）运动策略在未知环境中的部署安全；该值函数可计算且能避免局部调制/几何CBF导致的碰撞、伪吸引子和鞍点，并在五类DS构造及Franka机械臂上验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DS是带稳定性与收敛保证的向量场运动策略，但部署到未知环境时必须局部重塑，常用调制或几何控制屏障函数（CBF）。这些局部策略受障碍几何与DS复杂度影响，可能导致不可避免的碰撞或伪吸引子；而通常基于控制系统的可达性值函数因维数灾难难以求解。

Method: 从后向可达管概念出发构造值函数，度量标称DS rollout轨迹上的最坏情况安全性。作者证明：在基于DS的从演示学习设定中，无控制输入使可达性问题退化为确定性rollout；特定稳定性条件将无限时域截断为有限时域，从而得到良定义的值函数。进一步证明该值函数是标称DS流下无障碍区域的最大前向不变子集，并采用CBF-on-V作为安全证书。

Result: 在五类DS构造上验证：解析DS、Neural ODE、微分同胚潜空间DS、LPV-DS、SE(3)，并在Franka机械臂上验证。调制与几何CBF在正对不安全区域时存在鞍点问题，而CBF-on-V完全避免该缺陷。

Conclusion: 该值函数安全证书为DS策略部署提供了可计算的安全保证，克服了局部重塑方法的碰撞、伪吸引子与鞍点问题，适用于多种DS构造并成功应用于真实机械臂。

Abstract: Dynamical Systems (DS) are reactive motion policies representing vector fields trained with theoretical guarantees of stability and convergence. To ensure safety during deployment in unknown environments they must be locally reshaped, either through modulation or geometric control barrier function strategies. However, depending on the geometry of the obstacles and the complexity of the DS, these local strategies can lead the system to unavoidable collisions or spurious attractors. In this work, we certify safety with a value function drawn from the notion of backward reachability tube, which measures the worst-case safety along a rollout trajectory of the nominal DS. Usually, such a value function is intractable for a controlled system due to curse of dimensionality. We show that in the DS-based learning-from-demonstration setting, the absence of a control input collapses the reachability problem to a deterministic rollout, and the presence of certain stability conditions truncates the infinite horizon to a finite one, resulting in a well-defined value function. We further show that the value function we devised is the maximal forward-invariant subset of the obstaclefree region for the nominal DS flow. The application of this certificate function is validated across five DS constructions - analytical, Neural ODE, diffeomorphic latent space, LPV-DS, SE(3)and validate it on a Franka manipulator. Modulation and geometric CBFs also suffer from saddle point in cases of headon approach towards an unsafe zone. We show that CBF-on-V avoids this pitfall entirely.

</details>


### [132] [PneuTac: Tactile Manipulation with Soft Pneumatic Robots via Unified MPM-Gaussian Splatting Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38418)
*Shaohong Zhong,Marco Pontin,Joe Watson,Perla Maiolino,Ingmar Posner*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 PneuTac：面向软气动机器人与触觉传感器的统一触觉反馈操作框架，结合 MPM 动力学建模、3DGS 渲染与视觉 real-to-sim，并用代理模型训练感知与动作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体机器人可借顺应性实现安全接触，视触觉传感器可提供高分辨率触觉感知；但带顺应性机器人的触觉操作学习受限于缺乏高效仿真，且现有仿真器常将二者分开建模，存在难以高效克服的校准差距。

Method: 用物质点法（MPM）建模软体机器人与可变形触觉膜动力学，用 3D Gaussian Splatting（3DGS）渲染；通过简单视觉方法完成 real-to-sim，再基于代理模型训练动作与感知网络以高效仿真；利用该框架驱动触觉引导流程，在仿真中采集演示数据。

Result: 在定制气动软指与触觉感知指尖上进行实验，并开展跨设备评估，表明 PneuTac 能准确建模带触觉传感器的软体机器人；在三个真实世界接触丰富的高顺应性操作任务上，使用仿真增强演示训练的策略优于仅用相同真实数据训练的基线。

Conclusion: PneuTac 是面向高顺应性硬件的实用触觉操作框架，可统一建模软体机器人与触觉传感，并通过仿真增强提升真实任务中的触觉操作策略性能。

Abstract: Soft robots and tactile sensors have demonstrated great potential in delicate manipulation tasks. Soft pneumatic robots enable safe contact through compliance, and vision-based tactile sensors offer high-resolution touch perception. However, learning tactile manipulation with compliant robots has been challenging, bottlenecked by the lack of efficient simulation. Existing simulators typically model them in isolation, and exhibit large calibration gaps that are difficult to overcome efficiently. We present PneuTac, a unified framework for tactile-feedback manipulation with soft pneumatic robots. We leverage the material point method (MPM) for modelling the dynamics of the soft robot and the deformable tactile membrane, and 3D Gaussian splatting (3DGS) for rendering. Real-to-sim modelling is done with a simple vision-based method, to then train action and perception networks for efficient simulation with surrogate models. We use the framework to drive a tactile-guided pipeline to collect demonstrations in simulation. Through experiments on a custom-designed pneumatic soft finger with a tactile sensing tip, together with additional cross-device evaluations, we show that PneuTac is capable of accurately modelling soft robots with tactile sensors, and that policies trained with simulation-augmented demonstrations outperform baselines trained on the same real data on three real-world contact-rich compliant manipulation tasks, making it a practical framework for tactile manipulation on compliant hardware.

</details>


### [133] [Draft: A Parametric Tool for Robot Design Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38405)
*David Nguyen,Marcelo Coelho,Sangbae Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Draft：一种无需CAD的参数化生成工具，可将串链参数树编译为仿真就绪的MJCF模型，并用真实硬件趋势约束参数，从而支持形态与控制的协同设计和权衡评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人性能常受限于形态与控制联合迭代的高成本：每次CAD修改都需先转为仿真模型才能开展控制工作；现有协同设计方法多依赖单一平台的模型生成器，或缺乏真实世界数据来证明设计合理性。

Method: Draft是一个通用参数化生成工具，其引擎可将任意参数化串链树编译为仿真就绪的MJCF模型，无需CAD。它允许工程师通过易调模型探索设计权衡，并评估设计变化对控制器性能的影响。Draft利用对114个执行器和49个已发表机器人描述拟合的趋势来约束每个设计的自由参数，使生成的机器人锚定于真实硬件。

Result: 通过构建四款现成机器人的数字孪生进行整体验证，其质量与真实值达到1.10×几何平均折叠误差；最后通过两阶段强化学习课程评估三种四足机器人，展示Draft如何暴露设计权衡。

Conclusion: Draft降低了形态-控制协同设计的门槛，能生成有真实硬件依据的仿真模型，并有效揭示设计权衡，为机器人协同优化提供了平台无关的工具。

Abstract: Robot performance is often limited by the cost of iterating on morphology and control together, since every computer-aided design (CAD) change has to be carried into a simulation-ready model before control work begins. Co-design methods attempt to close this gap, but each uses a model generator written for a single platform or lack the use of real-world data to suggest that designs are plausible. We present Draft, a parametric generation tool whose generalized engine compiles any parametric tree of serial chains into a simulation-ready MJCF model, without CAD. It allows engineers to explore design tradeoffs through easily adjustable models and evaluate how changes influence controller performance. Draft grounds the free parameters of each design using trends fitted to a survey of $114$ actuators and $49$ published robot descriptions, so that a generated robot is anchored to real-world hardware. We validate those trends wholistically by building twins of four off-the-shelf robots, whose masses agree to $1.10\times$ geometric mean fold error. Finally, we demonstrate how Draft exposes design tradeoffs by evaluating three quadrupeds through a two-stage reinforcement learning curriculum.

</details>


### [134] [Memorize, Adapt, Ignore: Diagnosing Robot Learning Mechanisms under Training Data Variation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38401)
*Ke Zhang,Danica J. Sutherland,Chao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文利用经验神经切线核（NTK）诊断机器人操作策略中训练数据变异的作用机制，区分记忆与适应、识别对任务无关因素的忽略，并据此给出域随机化设计等实用指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据变异（如仿真中的域随机化或模仿学习演示筛选）是提升机器人操作策略鲁棒性的主要手段，但其内在机制尚不清晰，实践中通常依赖昂贵的试错来选择随机化参数。

Method: 在ManiSkill的pick-and-place强化学习，以及LIBERO和RoboTwin上微调视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型等设置中，围绕物体尺寸、颜色、类型、场景光照和语言提示进行随机化案例研究；从模型行为和内部表征两方面分析，以经验NTK为主要诊断工具，并引入基于NTK的信噪比，比较不同表征，最终用基于ACT的模仿学习在真实硬件上验证。

Result: 经验NTK能区分内部学习机制的转变：变异不足时策略表现为记忆不同情形（如分别学习大立方体和小立方体的做法），变异充分时则表现为适应当前情形；基于NTK的信噪比还能判断策略是否学会忽略任务无关因素（如将蓝色和红色立方体同等处理，而非分别学习蓝色子策略和红色子策略）。

Conclusion: 这些诊断信号可用于设计域随机化方案、选择模型和检测捷径学习；相关发现在不同表征间进行了比较，并通过基于ACT模仿学习的真实机器人实验得到验证。

Abstract: Training data variation, whether through designing a domain randomization (DR) scheme in simulation or curating demonstrations for imitation learning, is a primary lever for improving the robustness of robotic manipulation policies. Yet its underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood, and practitioners typically select randomization parameters through expensive trial and error. We investigate these mechanisms through a series of case studies, randomizing object size, color, and type as well as scene lighting and linguistic prompts across settings including pick-and-place RL in ManiSkill and fine-tuning of vision-language-action (VLA) models on LIBERO and RoboTwin. We examine both model behavior and internal representations, using the empirical neural tangent kernel (NTK) as our primary diagnostic tool. We show that the NTK distinguishes a shift in the internal learning mechanism from \textit{memorizing} different situations with insufficient variation (e.g.\ learning what to do for a large cube, and what to do for a small cube) to \textit{adapting} to the situation at hand with sufficient variation. An NTK-based signal-to-noise ratio also helps distinguish when policies have learned to \emph{ignore} task-irrelevant factors (e.g.\ treating blue and red cubes identically, instead of learning a blue sub-policy and a red sub-policy). We use these diagnostics to develop practical guidance for designing DR schemes, selecting models, and detecting shortcut learning. We further compare different kinds of representations and validate our findings with real-world hardware experiments using ACT-based imitation learning.

</details>


### [135] [GestAdapt: Workspace-Conditioned Co-Speech Gesture Generation for Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38400)
*Bosong Ding,Xianglin Zhang,Miao Xin,Murat Kirtay,Giacomo Spigler*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 GestAdapt：一种以指定腕部工作空间为条件、在生成阶段适配可用空间的共语手势生成框架，并在真实机器人上验证其优于无工作空间约束与事后约束修正的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人共语手势不仅需适配语音与本体，还需适配执行动作时可用工作空间；同一语音可对应不同手势，因此机器人应直接生成符合空间约束的合适动作，而非先形成无约束轨迹再修正，以免损害手势自然度。

Method: GestAdapt 以规定腕部工作空间为条件进行共语手势生成；通过共享运动表示从六个互补共语语料库中学习，并支持重定向到不同机器人本体。

Result: 定量评估表明生成动作在满足工作空间约束的同时仍接近真实动作分布。用户研究中，修改工作空间约束下生成的手势平均质量得分为 3.24/5，高于无工作空间变体 2.43/5，低于参考动作 3.68/5。在真实机器人评估中，所有对比动作均在同一工作空间约束下重定向至 Reachy2 人形机器人；本框架生成的动作在 69.7% 的成对比较中排名第一，优于无工作空间变体及事后约束的重定向真实动作。

Conclusion: 结果支持在生成过程中将手势适配到可用工作空间，而非事后修改无约束轨迹以满足空间约束；后者可能牺牲手势自然度。

Abstract: Co-speech gestures for robots must adapt not only to speech and embodiment, but also to the workspace available for performing the motion. Since the same speech can be accompanied by different gestures, a robot can respond to workspace constraints, e.g., gestures for speech next to a wall. In these scenarios, the robot should gesture in a suitable motion rather than simply correcting an unconstrained one. To achieve this goal, we present GestAdapt, a workspace-conditioned framework that conditions co-speech gesture generation on a prescribed wrist workspace. The GestAdapt framework learns from six complementary co-speech corpora through a shared motion representation and supports retargeting to different robot embodiments. Quantitative evaluation shows that generated motions remain close to the real-motion distribution while respecting the workspace. In a user study, gestures generated under modified workspace constraints receive a mean quality score of 3.24/5, above our no-workspace variant (2.43/5) and below the reference motions (3.68/5). In a real robot evaluation, all compared motions are retargeted to the Reachy2 humanoid robot under identical workspace constraints. Motions generated with our framework rank first in 69.7\% of comparisons, higher than our no-workspace variant baseline and retargeted ground-truth motions constrained afterward. Overall, the results support adapting gestures to the available workspace during generation, rather than modifying unconstrained trajectories afterward to satisfy workspace constraints, potentially compromising gesture naturalness.

</details>


### [136] [TALK-Dem: Benchmarking Embodied Task Planning under Dementia-Associated Communication Patterns](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38371)
*Guangxin Zhao,Yiran Hu,Yuan Cao,Chenxi Jiang,Jianfei Yang,Yegang Du,Yasuyuki Taki,Yoshifumi Kitamura,Lin Gu,Zhi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出TALK-Dem基准，用于评估LLM驱动机器人在痴呆相关言语交流下的任务规划鲁棒性，并提出CARE检索增强方法以提升本地可部署辅助机器人的任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM机器人任务规划假设用户指令清晰、完整且聚焦任务；但面对真实用户尤其是痴呆症患者（PLWD）时，规划器易出错甚至带来人身安全风险，因此需要评估并改善沟通鲁棒性。

Method: 构建TALK-Dem基准，包含4,800条指令，覆盖指代不精确、物体替换、空话、话题漂移和侵入五种交流模式及三个强度等级；在六个开源权重LLM上测试理想与非理想指令差异；提出Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience（CARE），检索既往已解决任务为当前任务提供解释与规划上下文，并与标准提示基线比较。

Result: 六个开源权重模型在痴呆相关交流模式下性能较理想指令最多下降22.3个百分点，显示显著鲁棒性缺口；CARE在六个模型上总体优于标准提示，平均任务成功率较原始提示提升18.1个百分点。

Conclusion: 结果强调，面向本地可部署辅助机器人，既要评估真实交流条件下的规划鲁棒性，也需发展有效的自适应策略；TALK-Dem数据集已公开。

Abstract: Existing LLM-driven robot task planners rely on a taken-for-granted assumption of an ideal user whose instructions are clear, complete, and task-focused. However, when interacting with real-world users, especially those experiencing cognitive impairments, such as people living with dementia (PLWD), the planners often make mistakes and even pose physical safety risks. We proposed TALK-Dem (Talking Attributes and Linguistic Knowledge in Dementia), the first benchmark for evaluating LLM-driven robot task planning under dementia-associated verbal communication. TALK-Dem contains 4,800 instructions and covers five typical communication patterns, including Referential Imprecision, Object Substitution, Empty Speech, Topic Drift, and Intrusion, at three intensity levels. Experiments across six open-weight LLMs reveal a substantial robustness gap. Across communication patterns, open-weight models exhibited performance drops of up to 22.3 percentage points compared to ideal instructions. This revealed a critical gap and even danger for real-world applications, especially in assistive robotics, where locally deployable models are necessary due to privacy concerns and connectivity constraints. To mitigate this issue, we proposed the Context-Aware Retrieval from Experience (CARE) method, which retrieves relevant previously resolved tasks to provide task-specific interpretation and planning context. CARE generally outperformed standard prompting baselines across the six open-weight models, improving average task success by 18.1 percentage points over the vanilla prompt. These results highlighted the importance of both evaluating communication robustness and developing effective adaptation strategies for locally deployable assistive robots. The TALK-Dem dataset is publicly available at this https URL .

</details>


### [137] [A Two-Echelon Covering Tour Vehicle Routing Problem with Drones for Post-Disaster Relief](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38227)
*Dang Viet Anh Nguyen,Rajesh Piplani,Aldy Gunawan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出灾后救援物资配送的两级覆盖巡回车辆路径问题（2E-CTVRP），建立混合整数线性规划模型，并提出混合元启发式算法GRASP-ILS-PR进行求解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 灾后救援物资配送需要在受灾区域外围设置卫星点，并协调卡车与无人机的两级运输。该问题涉及卫星分配、卡车路径排序以及受害者聚类到卫星的分配，目标是优化救援物资到达时间。

Method: 在第一级，卡车队将物资和无人机从中心仓库运至受灾区域外围的卫星点；在第二级，无人机从卫星点并行出发，向受害者聚类中心配送物资，卡车需在卫星点等待无人机返回。作者将2E-CTVRP建模为混合整数线性规划，并提出GRASP-ILS-PR混合元启发式算法，结合贪婪随机构造、单轨迹搜索以及针对聚类到卫星分配的周期性路径重连。

Result: 在110个新基准实例上，GRASP-ILS-PR在30秒内找到所有已知最优解；在多数较大实例上匹配或优于商业求解器1小时所得解，在50个卫星点的实例上最多改进11%。该算法明显优于结合进化路径重连的传统GRASP，并且比使用相同搜索组件的模拟退火算法结果更稳定。对两种车队配置的分析表明，多数实例中少量大型卡车可最小化累计到达时间，而多辆小型卡车在每个实例中都能更早、更公平地向受害者配送物资。

Conclusion: 2E-CTVRP为灾后两级卡车-无人机救援配送提供了有效建模框架，GRASP-ILS-PR在求解质量和稳定性上表现优异；同时，车队配置需在累计到达时间与配送公平性/及时性之间权衡。

Abstract: We introduce the two-echelon covering tour vehicle routing problem (2E-CTVRP) for the distribution of relief supplies after a disaster.
