In the first echelon, a fleet of trucks transports supplies and drones from a central depot to satellites at the periphery of the affected area. In the second echelon, drones launched in parallel from the satellites deliver the supplies to the centroids of victim clusters, which are obtained by clustering the victim locations, and each truck waits at a satellite until its drones have returned. The problem combines the assignment of satellites to trucks, the sequencing of the truck routes, and the assignment of clusters to satellites, and minimizes the sum of the arrival times of the trucks at the satellites and at the depot. We formulate the 2E-CTVRP as a mixed integer linear program and propose a hybrid metaheuristic, GRASP-ILS-PR, which combines greedy randomized construction, a single-trajectory search, and periodic path relinking on the assignment of clusters to satellites. On a new benchmark set of 110 instances, GRASP-ILS-PR finds all known optimal solutions within 30 seconds, matches or improves on the one-hour solutions of a commercial solver on most larger instances, and improves on them by up to 11% for instances with 50 satellites. It clearly outperforms a conventional GRASP with evolutionary path relinking and yields more consistent results than a simulated annealing algorithm that uses the same search components. An analysis of two fleet configurations shows that few large trucks minimize the cumulative arrival time in most instances, whereas many small trucks deliver supplies to the victims earlier and more equitably in every instance.

</details>


### [138] [SynIL: Leveraging Synergy for Offline Imitation Learning from Imperfect Demonstration Datasets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38225)
*Yuto Tanaka,Kyo Kutsuzawa,Martina Doku,Dai Owaki,Mitsuhiro Hayashibe*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出 SynIL：基于运动协同（motor synergy）的自动化、无标签示范质量评估框架，通过自监督奖励回归生成稠密转移级奖励，用于离线强化学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿学习依赖大量示范数据，但次优或噪声示范会严重降低性能；现有质量评估方法需要人工预选专家参考数据或任务特定启发式，难以扩展。

Method: SynIL 受神经科学证据启发，认为运动协同这一低维协调结构与运动熟练度直接相关；算法量化协同表现，并通过自监督奖励回归生成稠密、转移级奖励信号。

Result: 在 D4RL 运动基准和多人类 Robomimic 操作数据集上，协同衍生奖励与真实奖励强相关；SynIL 显著优于行为克隆（BC），性能与基于真实环境奖励的离线强化学习相当，并在稀疏奖励人类遥操作场景中更优。

Conclusion: SynIL 提供了一种无需标签、可扩展的示范质量评估与奖励生成方法，能提升离线模仿/强化学习对次优或噪声数据的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Imitation learning enables robots to acquire complex skills directly from massive demonstration datasets, but its performance degrades severely when datasets are contaminated with suboptimal or noisy demonstrations. While prior quality-assessment methods attempt to filter or reweight data, they typically rely on manual pre-selection of expert reference data or task-specific heuristics, limiting scalability. To address this challenge, we introduce SynIL (Synergy-based Imitation Learning), a novel framework for automated, label-free demonstration quality assessment in offline reinforcement learning. Grounded in neuroscientific evidence that motor synergy, a low-dimensional coordinated structure in movement, correlates directly with motor proficiency, SynIL algorithmically quantifies synergy manifestation to generate dense, transition-level reward signals via self-supervised reward regression. Comprehensive evaluations on D4RL locomotion benchmarks and multi-human Robomimic manipulation datasets demonstrate that synergy-derived rewards correlate strongly with ground-truth rewards. Furthermore, SynIL substantially outperforms Behavior Cloning (BC) and achieves performance comparable to, and in sparse-reward human teleoperation scenarios, superior to, offline reinforcement learning trained on true environment rewards.

</details>


### [139] [Fiatlux: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Humanoid Ladder Climbing and Light-Bulb Replacement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38216)
*Pavel Bushuyeu,Yujin Chen,Anton Nikolaev,Brian Shu,Igor Molybog*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Fiatlux：基于NVIDIA Isaac Lab的灯泡更换长时程基准。Unitree G1人形机器人需在单一回合内搬梯、攀爬、更换并处置灯泡，任务被分解为12个子任务环境，按难度加权门限评分，支持部分得分，并提供标准/特权观测、评估协议、基线实现及遥操作记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准通常分别评估桌面操作、平地家务或人形行走与操作，未能在同一长时程任务中同时评测垂直移动与对易碎负载的灵巧操作，因此缺少统一、可复现的评测平台。

Method: 构建Isaac Lab仿真基准：单回合中，人形机器人将梯子置于天花板或墙面灯具下，攀爬，将灯座中的旧灯泡换为新灯泡，并将旧灯泡放入回收箱。回合分解为12个子任务环境，采用难度加权门限评分；成功要求新灯泡就位、旧灯泡处置、两者均未掉落且不超越易碎性约束，未完全成功可获部分得分。观测分为标准模式（物理机器人可感知或估计的信号）与特权模式（精确仿真状态）。论文规定评估协议，并提供RSL-RL PPO、零样本NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 VLA模型与全身控制器等参考基线，以及用于定义和校验成功门限的遥操作记录。

Result: 提出Fiatlux基准、评估协议与参考基线，覆盖RSL-RL PPO、零样本GR00T N1.7 VLA及全身控制器；同时公开基准代码与遥操作记录，用于支持长时程垂直移动与易碎物体灵巧操作的可复现评测。

Conclusion: Fiatlux填补了在同一长时程回合中联合评测垂直移动与易碎负载灵巧操作的空白，为通用人形机器人提供了可复现的基准、评分机制、基线实现及验证数据。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks evaluate tabletop manipulation, flat-floor household activity, or humanoid locomotion and manipulation as separate task groups; none scores vertical mobility and dexterous work on a fragile payload in one long-horizon episode. We present Fiatlux, a light-bulb replacement benchmark built on NVIDIA Isaac Lab. In one episode, a Unitree G1 humanoid positions a step ladder under a ceiling or wall fixture, climbs it, exchanges a spent bulb in a socket for a fresh one, and leaves the spent one in a disposal crate. We decompose the episode into twelve subtask environments scored on difficulty-weighted gates. The goal is a successful replacement, with the fresh bulb seated, the spent one disposed of, neither dropped, and a fragility bound not crossed. Runs that fall short can earn partial credit. Observations are split into a standard mode (signals a physical robot could sense or estimate) and a privileged mode (exact simulator state). We specify the evaluation protocol and provide reference baseline implementations spanning RSL-RL PPO, zero-shot NVIDIA GR00T N1.7 Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models, and whole-body controllers. Additionally, we provide the teleoperated recordings used to specify and check the success gates. The benchmark code and the teleoperated recordings are available at this http URL .

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Game-Guided Skill Discovery through Self-Play for Playable Agent Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.40137)
*Seungeun Rho,Jeonghwan Kim,Xue Bin Peng,Sehoon Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GGSD 通过游戏中的自博弈发现可直接由人类操作的运动技能：训练分层智能体与过去自身竞争，高层策略从少量离散技能中选择，底层技能条件策略学习对应行为；训练后人类可替换高层策略，用相同离散技能控制智能体，并通过技能切换涌现组合行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可玩技能为具身智能体控制提供紧凑抽象，避免低层动作控制；有效技能应同时具备语义区分性、可解释性和表达力，而现有无监督技能发现方法往往难以兼顾。

Method: 提出 Game-Guided Skill Discovery（GGSD）框架，以竞争性游戏玩法引导技能发现。采用分层智能体与自身历史版本自博弈：高层策略在少量离散技能集中选择，底层技能条件策略学习相应技能行为。训练完成后，人类可直接替换高层策略，通过同一组离散技能操控智能体；技能间的转换可产生涌现式连招行为，扩展个体基元之外的表达力。

Result: 在 Ant、Franka-arm 和 Unitree G1 环境中，GGSD 产生可由人类操作的技能；人类能够组合这些技能解决未见任务，如 Maze 和 CubePush，且无需额外训练。

Conclusion: 将技能发现建立在竞争性游戏玩法之上，可同时获得语义区分、可解释且表达力强的可玩技能，使人类能直接控制智能体并通过技能组合完成新任务。

Abstract: We present Game-Guided Skill Discovery (GGSD), a framework that uses self-play in games to discover motor skills that are directly playable by humans. Playable skills provide a compact abstraction for controlling embodied agents through a small set of learned behaviors rather than low-level actions. To be effective, these skills should be semantically distinct, interpretable, and expressive; properties that existing unsupervised skill-discovery methods often fail to achieve simultaneously. GGSD achieves these desiderata by grounding skill discovery in competitive gameplay. A hierarchical agent competes against its past selves, with a high-level policy selecting from a small discrete skill set and a skill-conditioned low-level policy learning the corresponding behaviors. After training, a human can replace the high-level policy and directly control the agent through the same discrete skills. Despite the small number of high-level actions, skill transitions give rise to emergent combo behaviors, expanding expressivity beyond individual primitives. Across Ant, Franka-arm, and Unitree G1 environments, we show that GGSD produces human-playable skills that humans can compose to solve unseen tasks, such as Maze and CubePush, without additional training. An interactive demo is available at this https URL .

</details>


### [141] [Markovian Dynamics Enforcer: Feasibility Preserving Correction on Learned Dynamics Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.39888)
*Kevin Yu,Tao Guo,Constantinos Antoniou,Panagiotis Angeloudis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MaDE：一种时不变的事后动力学约束算子，可将任意轨迹预测器给出的状态转移投影到学习到的可行动力学流形上；无需真实控制即可训练。它通过推断控制、用已知物理加学习残差的补全模型重算状态，并用梯度法降低不等式违反，从而按构造保证与模型动态一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经轨迹预测器虽能实现低预测误差，但可能违反动力学、执行器限制或状态约束，尤其在控制未观测且动力学仅部分指定时。因此需要一种通用后处理模块，使任意预测器的输出更符合可行动力学与约束。

Method: MaDE 是时间不变的事后算子，训练于可行状态，不需要真实控制。对每个状态转移提议，它先推断一个控制，再通过“已知物理 + 学习残差”的补全模型重算状态；随后用基于梯度的不等式缩减修正该控制，在迭代预算内尽力满足不等式约束。由于每次修正迭代都重新进入补全模型，返回状态相对于该模型和给定的前一状态锚点按构造满足动态一致性。该冻结算子可附加到循环网络、结构化状态空间模型和 Transformer 预测器下游。

Result: 在完全指定的模拟系统上，MaDE 将动力学残差降至几乎为零；在欠指定系统上，其真实动力学残差小于基线。在记录车辆轨迹上，对运动学自行车模型的一步残差为：MaDE 0.0071–0.0072，原始预测器 0.1703–0.1714。MaDE 使平均位移误差增大 1.57–1.83 倍。

Conclusion: MaDE 能有效提升轨迹预测的动力学可行性与约束满足程度，且可作为通用后处理模块兼容多种预测器；但其代价是平均位移误差上升，体现精度与可行性之间的权衡。

Abstract: Neural trajectory predictors can reach low prediction error while violating dynamics, actuator limits, or state constraints, especially when controls are unobserved and dynamics are partially specified. We introduce the Markovian Dynamics Enforcer (MaDE), a time-invariant post-hoc operator mapping state-transition proposals onto a learned feasible dynamics manifold, trained on feasible states without ground-truth controls. For each transition it infers a control and recomputes the state through a completion model of known physics plus a learned residual. It then corrects that control by gradient-based inequality reduction, so inequality satisfaction is best-effort within an iteration budget. Since every correction iterate re-enters the completion model, the returned state is dynamically consistent by construction relative to that model and the supplied previous-state anchor. MaDE drives dynamics residuals to essentially zero on fully specified simulated systems, and on an underspecified system leaves a smaller true-dynamics residual than the baselines. Designed to attach to arbitrary predictors, the frozen operator is evaluated downstream of recurrent, structured state-space, and transformer predictors. On recorded vehicle trajectories the one-step residual against a kinematic bicycle model is 0.0071 to 0.0072 for MaDE and 0.1703 to 0.1714 for raw predictors. MaDE raises average displacement error by a factor of 1.57 to 1.83.

</details>


### [142] [Learning to Plan from Random Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38383)
*Deqian Kong,Guangyan Sun,Sheng Cheng,Sirui Xie,Bo Pang,Jianwen Xie,Tony Geng,Caiwen Ding,Ying Nian Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 随机探索无需策略改进训练即可支持长程规划：用条件能量模型学习不同时间尺度的时序对数密度比，并结合局部动力学模型进行规划，在迷宫、自我中心导航和操作任务中验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标指定前的随机探索经验能否在不进行策略改进训练的情况下支持长程规划，并分析随机游走中时序关系所包含的信息：短时域在扩散极限下揭示测地几何，长时域在混合抹除区别前揭示区域间连通性。

Method: 通过随机游走分析时序关系；用条件能量模型以时域条件嵌入估计时序对数密度比，并通过噪声对比估计在观测对上训练，无需动作或奖励标签。规划器在朝向目标时查询不同时域的学习关系；测试时用单独的局部动力学模型预测候选动作结果，时序模型通过选择或聚合不同时域的估计改进来评估其目标进展。智能体执行一个动作后固定两模型重新规划。

Result: 实验在状态和图像输入下展示了基于随机探索的长程迷宫规划；学习到的分数场、嵌入探针和规划路径呈现多尺度认知地图特性；还展示了基于随机探索的自我中心导航和基于次优数据的操作规划。

Conclusion: 随机探索结合时序关系学习可形成多尺度认知地图，并在无需策略改进训练的情况下实现长程规划，可推广到迷宫、自我中心导航和操作规划等任务。

Abstract: Random exploration reveals how an environment can be traversed before a goal is specified. Can this experience support long-range planning without policy-improvement training? Our random-walk analysis explains what temporal relations contain: short horizons reveal geodesic geometry in the diffusion limit, while longer horizons reveal connectivity between regions before mixing removes these distinctions. We learn these relations with a conditional energy-based model that estimates temporal log-density ratios through horizon-conditioned embeddings. The model is trained on observation pairs by noise-contrastive estimation, without action or reward labels. The planner queries these learned relations at different horizons as it moves toward the goal. At test time, a separate local dynamics model predicts candidate action outcomes, and the temporal model evaluates their progress toward the goal by selecting or aggregating estimated improvements across horizons. The agent executes one action and replans with both models fixed. Experiments demonstrate long-range maze planning from random exploration using states and images. Learned score fields, embedding probes, and planned routes exhibit properties of a multiscale cognitive map. We further demonstrate egocentric navigation from random exploration and manipulation planning from suboptimal data.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [Refusals That Bend: Measuring and Predicting Task Malleability in Embodied VLM Planners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38971)
*Leo Y. Lin,Mikhail Kuznetsov,Muslum Ozgur Ozmen,Z. Berkay Celik*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该工作研究具身视觉语言模型（VLM）规划器已给出的安全拒绝是否会被环境中加入普通物体所推翻，并提出任务层面的“可塑性”（malleability）概念，表明其可在查询目标前预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具身VLM作为机器人高层规划器需在未见环境中保持安全对齐，但现有红队攻击假设可优化提示、像素或环境文本，现有基准也只考察固定场景中的危害识别或缓解，均未检验已给出的拒绝在环境日常变化后是否仍然成立。

Method: 在无像素、梯度或提示对抗控制的前提下，向环境加入单个日常物体；在846个受宪法防护规划器初始拒绝的任务上测试拒绝被翻转为服从的比例；比较任务特定物体与从固定列表抽取、且不了解环境或指令的物体；并利用小型开源VLM的多信号组合预测任务可塑性。

Result: 20.2%的初始拒绝任务可被一个或多个物体翻转为服从，且所需物体数量因任务而异；与任务无关的固定列表物体绕过安全的频率与针对特定任务提出的物体大致相当；最常与最少被绕过的任务差异在于指令和环境中危害的显著性；小型开源VLM信号组合识别可塑性任务的频率是随机选择的2.4倍。

Conclusion: 普通物体，无论由对手放置还是由环境日常重排引入，都足以推翻安全拒绝；由于易受影响性由任务指定方式决定，建议在部署前按任务评估可塑性。

Abstract: Embodied vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly deployed as high-level planners for robots because they generalize across diverse environments. However, this requires their safety alignment to also hold in unseen environments. Existing red-teaming assumes an adversary who optimizes the prompt, the pixels, or text in the environment, and existing benchmarks ask whether a planner recognizes or mitigates a hazard in a fixed scene. Neither asks whether a refusal the planner has already given survives an ordinary change to the environment. We ask that question by placing a single everyday object into the environment, with no pixel, gradient, or prompt under adversarial control. On $846$ tasks that a constitution-guarded planner initially refuses, we find $20.2\%$ of tasks can be flipped to compliance by one or more objects, and the number of objects differs from one task to another. In addition, the object need not be chosen for the task, i.e., items drawn from a fixed list, with no knowledge of the environment or the instruction, bypass safety about as often as items proposed for the specific task. We qualitatively contrast the tasks bypassed most and least often and find that the distinction lies in how conspicuous the hazard is in the instruction and environment. Susceptibility to safety bypass is therefore a property of the task, which we call its \emph{malleability}, and we show that it can be predicted before the target is ever queried. A composite of signals read from a small open-source VLM identifies malleable tasks $2.4\times$ as often as picking at random. Everyday objects, whether placed by an adversary or introduced by ordinary rearrangement of the environment, are thus sufficient to overturn a refusal. Because susceptibility is determined by how a task is specified, we recommend assessing malleability per task prior to deployment.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [144] [Fast and Scalable Multi-Agent Distribution Matching via Partitioned Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38960)
*Kooktae Lee,Ruchika Singh*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出一种基于最优传输的可扩展框架，通过分块局部传输与有限时域控制交替，实现多智能体终端分布匹配，并保持与Wasserstein目标的严格联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最优传输可自然度量分布失配并将智能体分配给目标空间分布，但大规模系统中全局离散传输计算代价过高，需在可扩展性与理论保证之间取得平衡。

Method: 将智能体和目标样本按空间对应关系分块，求解较小的局部传输问题；在质量平衡条件下，受限耦合对全局问题仍可行，并给出Wasserstein代价上界。局部分配生成目标位置，用于线性与非线性动力学下的有限时域智能体控制；交替进行局部分配与控制，并建立传输代理目标的逐周期下降保证。

Result: 所提框架在保持与Wasserstein目标严格联系的同时，支持大规模终端分布匹配；仿真验证了结果的技术合理性。

Conclusion: 该框架为多智能体终端分布匹配提供了可扩展且具理论保障的最优传输方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a scalable optimal-transport-based framework for terminal distribution matching in multi-agent systems. While optimal transport provides a natural way to measure distributional mismatch and assign agents to a desired spatial distribution, global discrete transport can become computationally expensive for large-scale systems. We address this bottleneck by partitioning agents and target samples into spatially corresponding blocks and solving smaller local transport problems. Under a mass-balance condition, the resulting restricted coupling remains feasible for the global problem and provides an upper bound on the Wasserstein cost. The local assignments generate target locations for finite-horizon agent control, applicable to both linear and nonlinear dynamics. By alternating local assignment and control, we establish a cycle-to-cycle descent guarantee for the resulting transport surrogate. The proposed framework therefore enables scalable terminal distribution matching while retaining a rigorous connection to the Wasserstein objective. The technical soundness of the proposed results is validated through simulations.

</details>
